import type { TextRun, PositionedRun, LayoutResult } from './types';
import { measureText } from './measure';

type Token = TextRun & { width: number; fsize: number; ffamily: string; style: string };

const PARAGRAPH_GAP_PX = 8;

export function layoutRuns(
  runs: TextRun[],
  maxWidth: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): LayoutResult {
  if (!runs.length || maxWidth <= 0) {
    return { lines: [], totalHeight: 0, lineHeight: 0 };
  }

  let baseFontSize = 16;
  let baseFontFamily = 'Arial';
  for (const run of runs) {
    if (run.fontSize) baseFontSize = run.fontSize;
    if (run.fontFamily) baseFontFamily = run.fontFamily;
  }

  const tokens = tokenize(runs, baseFontSize, baseFontFamily);
  const paragraphGap = PARAGRAPH_GAP_PX;

  const lines: PositionedRun[][] = [];
  let currentLine: PositionedRun[] = [];
  let currentLineMaxFontSize = 0;
  let cursorX = 0;
  let cursorY = 0;
  let pendingNewlines = 0;
  let currentIndent = 0; // <--- РќРћР’РћР•: РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РґР»СЏ СЃРїРёСЃРєРѕРІ

  let leadingGapY = 0;

  const flushLine = () => {
    if (!currentLine.length) return;
    lines.push(currentLine);

    const lh = Math.max(
      currentLineMaxFontSize,
      Math.round(currentLineMaxFontSize * lineHeightCoef),
    );
    cursorY += lh;
    cursorX = 0;
    currentLine = [];
    currentLineMaxFontSize = 0;
  };

  const applyPendingNewlines = (isEnd: boolean) => {
    if (pendingNewlines === 0) return;
    const approxLh = Math.round(baseFontSize * lineHeightCoef);

    if (lines.length === 0 && currentLine.length === 0) {
      if (!isEnd) {
        const pairs = Math.floor(pendingNewlines / 2);
        if (pairs > 0) {
          leadingGapY += paragraphGap;
          for (let p = 1; p < pairs; p++) leadingGapY += approxLh + paragraphGap;
        } else {
          leadingGapY += approxLh;
        }
      }
      pendingNewlines = 0;
      return;
    }

    flushLine();

    if (!isEnd) {
      const pairs = Math.floor(pendingNewlines / 2);
      if (pairs > 0) {
        cursorY += paragraphGap;
        for (let p = 1; p < pairs; p++) cursorY += approxLh + paragraphGap;
      }
    }
    pendingNewlines = 0;
  };

  const pushToken = (tk: Token, text: string, width: number) => {
    if (leadingGapY > 0 && currentLine.length === 0 && lines.length === 0) {
      cursorY += leadingGapY;
      leadingGapY = 0;
    }
    if (tk.fsize > currentLineMaxFontSize) currentLineMaxFontSize = tk.fsize;
    
    // РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї Рє РїРµСЂРІРѕР№ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°/СЃРїРёСЃРєР°
    if (cursorX === 0) {
      cursorX += currentIndent;
    }

    currentLine.push({
      text,
      bold: tk.bold,
      italic: tk.italic,
      underline: tk.underline,
      strike: tk.strike,
      color: tk.color,
      bgColor: tk.bgColor,
      href: tk.href,
      fontSize: tk.fsize,
      fontFamily: tk.ffamily,
      x: cursorX,
      y: cursorY,
      width,
      actualFontSize: tk.fsize,
    });
    cursorX += width;
  };

  for (const tk of tokens) {
    if (tk.text === '\n') {
      pendingNewlines++;
      continue;
    }

    // Р­РІСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєР°: РµСЃР»Рё С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РЅР°С‡РёРЅР°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃ РїСЂРѕР±РµР»РѕРІ РёР»Рё РјР°СЂРєРµСЂР° СЃРїРёСЃРєР° вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї
    if (cursorX === 0 && (tk.text.startsWith('вЂў') || tk.text.startsWith('1.') || tk.text.startsWith('2.'))) {
       currentIndent = 20; // РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РґР»СЏ РјР°СЂРєРµСЂР°
    } else if (cursorX === 0 && tk.text.trim() === '') {
       currentIndent = tk.width; // РћС‚СЃС‚СѓРї РґР»СЏ С‚Р°Р±СѓР»СЏС†РёРё
    } else {
       currentIndent = 0;
    }

    const isSpace = /^\s+$/.test(tk.text);
    if (isSpace && cursorX === 0 && pendingNewlines === 0) continue;

    applyPendingNewlines(false);

    if (cursorX + tk.width <= maxWidth) {
      pushToken(tk, tk.text, tk.width);
      continue;
    }

    if (cursorX > 0) flushLine();
    if (isSpace) continue;

    if (tk.width > maxWidth) {
      const chars = Array.from(tk.text);
      let chunk = '';
      let chunkWidth = 0;

      for (const ch of chars) {
        const cw = measureText(ch, tk.fsize, tk.ffamily, tk.style);
        if (chunkWidth + cw > maxWidth && chunk.length > 0) {
          pushToken(tk, chunk, chunkWidth);
          flushLine();
          chunk = ch;
          chunkWidth = cw;
        } else {
          chunk += ch;
          chunkWidth += cw;
        }
      }
      if (chunk.length) pushToken(tk, chunk, chunkWidth);
      continue;
    }

    pushToken(tk, tk.text, tk.width);
  }

  applyPendingNewlines(true);
  flushLine();

  let totalHeight = 0;
  for (const line of lines) {
    for (const r of line) {
      const bottom = r.y + Math.max(r.actualFontSize, r.actualFontSize * lineHeightCoef);
      if (bottom > totalHeight) totalHeight = bottom;
    }
  }

  return { lines, totalHeight, lineHeight: 0 };
}

function tokenize(runs: TextRun[], baseFontSize: number, baseFontFamily: string): Token[] {
  const tokens: Token[] = [];
  for (const run of runs) {
    const fs = run.fontSize ?? baseFontSize;
    const ff = run.fontFamily ?? baseFontFamily;
    const style =
      run.bold && run.italic ? 'italic bold'
      : run.bold ? 'bold'
      : run.italic ? 'italic'
      : 'normal';

    for (const part of run.text.split(/(\n)/)) {
      if (part === '') continue;
      if (part === '\n') {
        tokens.push({ ...run, text: '\n', width: 0, fsize: fs, ffamily: ff, style });
        continue;
      }
      for (const w of part.split(/(\s+)/)) {
        if (w === '') continue;
        tokens.push({
          ...run,
          text: w,
          width: measureText(w, fs, ff, style),
          fsize: fs,
          ffamily: ff,
          style,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  return tokens;
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/richText/measure.ts
   NFR-PERF-07, REG-B10: РєСЌС€ С€РёСЂРёРЅ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° (LRU, 4096).
   ============================================================ */

let measureCtx: CanvasRenderingContext2D | null | undefined;

function getCtx(): CanvasRenderingContext2D | null {
  if (measureCtx === undefined) {
    measureCtx =
      typeof document !== 'undefined'
        ? document.createElement('canvas').getContext('2d')
        : null;
  }
  return measureCtx;
}

const cache = new Map<string, number>();
const MAX_CACHE = 4096;

export function measureText(
  text: string,
  fontSize: number,
  fontFamily: string,
  fontStyle: string,
): number {
  if (!text) return 0;

  const family = fontFamily || 'Arial, sans-serif';
  const size = typeof fontSize === 'number' && isFinite(fontSize) && fontSize > 0 ? fontSize : 16;
  const key = `${fontStyle}|${size}|${family}|${text}`;
  
  const cached = cache.get(key);
  if (cached !== undefined) {
    cache.delete(key);
    cache.set(key, cached);
    return cached;
  }

  const ctx = getCtx();
  let width: number;
  if (!ctx) {
    width = text.length * size * 0.55;
  } else {
    const style = fontStyle || 'normal';
    ctx.font = `${style} ${size}px ${family}`;
    const m = ctx.measureText(text);
    width = isFinite(m.width) && m.width > 0 ? m.width : text.length * size * 0.55;
  }

  if (cache.size >= MAX_CACHE) {
    const oldestKey = cache.keys().next().value;
    if (oldestKey !== undefined) cache.delete(oldestKey);
  }
  cache.set(key, width);
  return width;
}

import type { BaseStyle, RichBlock } from '../types';
import { parseHtmlToRuns } from './runs';
import { parseTableEl } from './table';

export function parseHtmlToBlocks(html: string, base: BaseStyle): RichBlock[] {
  if (!html) return [];
  if (typeof DOMParser === 'undefined') {
    const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(html, base);
    return runs.length ? [{ kind: 'text', runs }] : [];
  }

  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
  return parseNodesToBlocks(doc.body.childNodes, base);
}

export function parseCellBlocks(cellEl: HTMLElement, base: BaseStyle): RichBlock[] {
  const clone = cellEl.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
  const directPs = Array.from(clone.querySelectorAll(':scope > p')) as HTMLElement[];

  while (directPs.length) {
    const p = directPs[0];
    if (p.textContent?.trim() || p.querySelector('br, table')) break;
    p.remove();
    directPs.shift();
  }
  while (directPs.length) {
    const p = directPs[directPs.length - 1];
    if (p.textContent?.trim() || p.querySelector('br, table')) break;
    p.remove();
    directPs.pop();
  }

  while (clone.firstChild && (clone.firstChild as Element).nodeName === 'BR') {
    clone.removeChild(clone.firstChild);
  }
  while (clone.lastChild && (clone.lastChild as Element).nodeName === 'BR') {
    clone.removeChild(clone.lastChild);
  }

  return parseNodesToBlocks(clone.childNodes, base);
}

const CONTAINER_TAGS = new Set([
  'div', 'section', 'article', 'main', 'aside', 'figure',
  'blockquote', 'header', 'footer', 'nav', 'span',
]);

function parseNodesToBlocks(
  nodes: NodeListOf<ChildNode> | ChildNode[],
  base: BaseStyle,
): RichBlock[] {
  const blocks: RichBlock[] = [];
  let pendingHtml = '';

  const flushText = () => {
    if (!pendingHtml.trim()) { pendingHtml = ''; return; }
    const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(pendingHtml, base);
    if (runs.length) blocks.push({ kind: 'text', runs });
    pendingHtml = '';
  };

  const visit = (node: Node) => {
    if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
      pendingHtml += (node.textContent ?? '')
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
      return;
    }
    if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) return;

    const el = node as HTMLElement;
    const tag = el.tagName.toLowerCase();

    // F-RT-03: РЎРїРµС†-РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР° РѕР±РµСЂС‚РѕРє TipTap
    if (tag === 'div' && el.classList.contains('tableWrapper')) {
      const table = el.querySelector('table');
      if (table) {
        flushText();
        try {
          const data = parseTableEl(table, base, parseCellBlocks);
          if (data && data.cells.length) {
            blocks.push({ kind: 'table', data });
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[richText] parseTableEl failed:', err);
        }
        return;
      }
    }

    if (tag === 'table') {
      flushText();
      try {
        const data = parseTableEl(el, base, parseCellBlocks);
        if (data && data.cells.length) {
          blocks.push({ kind: 'table', data });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[richText] parseTableEl failed:', err);
        const runs = parseHtmlToRuns(el.textContent ?? '', base);
        if (runs.length) blocks.push({ kind: 'text', runs });
      }
      return;
    }

    if (CONTAINER_TAGS.has(tag) && el.querySelector('table')) {
      for (const child of Array.from(el.childNodes)) {
        visit(child);
      }
      return;
    }

    pendingHtml += el.outerHTML;
  };

  for (const node of Array.from(nodes)) {
    visit(node);
  }
  flushText();

  return blocks;
}

import type { TextRun, BaseStyle } from '../types';

/* ============================================================
   HTML в†’ TextRun[]

   Р’РђР–РќРћ:
   - Р’РµРґСѓС‰РёРµ Рё РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹ РЎРћРҐР РђРќРЇР®РўРЎРЇ РєР°Рє '\n\n'.
     РС… РїРѕС‚РѕРј РѕР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ layoutRuns РєР°Рє leading gap / gap РјРµР¶РґСѓ
     Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё. Р‘РµР· СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ Enter РІ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРµ РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ В«С‚РµСЂСЏР»СЃСЏВ».
   - РћР±СЂРµР·Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј РћР”РРќ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€Р°СЋС‰РёР№ '\n\n' вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ
     С‚РµС…РЅРёС‡РµСЃРєР°СЏ РїСѓСЃС‚Р°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµРіРѕ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°.
     Р’СЃС‘, С‡С‚Рѕ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ, вЂ” РѕСЃРѕР·РЅР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹.
   ============================================================ */

export function parseHtmlToRuns(html: string, base: BaseStyle): TextRun[] {
  if (!html) return [];
  if (typeof DOMParser === 'undefined') {
    return [{ ...baseToRun(base), text: html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '') }];
  }

  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
  const runs: TextRun[] = [];

  const pushLine = (style: Partial<TextRun>) => {
    if (runs.length && runs[runs.length - 1].text.endsWith('\n')) return;
    runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n' });
  };

  /**
   * РџР°СЂР°РіСЂР°С„РЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·СЂС‹РІ. force=true вЂ” РґР»СЏ РїСѓСЃС‚РѕРіРѕ Р±Р»РѕРєР°
   * (<p></p> / <p><br></p>): РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј '\n\n' РґР°Р¶Рµ РµСЃР»Рё
   * РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РёР№ СѓР¶Рµ РЅР° '\n\n'. Р­С‚Рѕ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ В«РґС‹СЂРєРёВ» РјРµР¶РґСѓ
   * Р°Р±Р·Р°С†Р°РјРё, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ РёРЅР°С‡Рµ СЃС…Р»РѕРїС‹РІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ.
   */
  const pushParagraphBreak = (style: Partial<TextRun>, force = false) => {
    const last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    if (!force) {
      if (last && last.text.endsWith('\n\n')) return;
      if (last && last.text.endsWith('\n')) {
        runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n' });
        return;
      }
    }
    runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: '\n\n' });
  };

  const makeListMarker = (depth: number, ordered: boolean, index: number): string => {
    const indent = '      '.repeat(Math.max(0, depth));
    const bullet = ordered ? `${index + 1}. ` : 'вЂў ';
    return indent + bullet;
  };

  function walk(node: Node, style: Partial<TextRun>, listDepth = 0) {
    if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
      const text = node.textContent || '';
      if (text) runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text });
      return;
    }
    if (node.nodeType !== Node.ELEMENT_NODE) return;

    const el = node as HTMLElement;
    const tag = el.tagName.toLowerCase();

    if (tag === 'br') {
      pushLine(style);
      return;
    }
    if (tag === 'table') return;

    /* ---------- РЎРїРёСЃРєРё: ul / ol ---------- */
    if (tag === 'ul' || tag === 'ol') {
      const ordered = tag === 'ol';
      const liList = Array.from(el.children).filter(
        (c) => (c as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'li',
      );
      liList.forEach((li, idx) => {
        const marker = makeListMarker(listDepth, ordered, idx);
        runs.push({ ...baseToRun(base), ...style, text: marker });

        li.childNodes.forEach((child) => {
          if (child.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
            const ct = (child as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase();
            if (ct === 'p') {
              child.childNodes.forEach((c) => walk(c, style, listDepth));
              return;
            }
            if (ct === 'ul' || ct === 'ol') {
              pushLine(style);
              walk(child, style, listDepth + 1);
              return;
            }
          }
          walk(child, style, listDepth);
        });

        pushLine(style);
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєРё ---------- */
    const next: Partial<TextRun> = { ...style };
    const baseFontSize = base.fontSize || 16;
    if (tag === 'h1') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.75); next.bold = true; }
    else if (tag === 'h2') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.4); next.bold = true; }
    else if (tag === 'h3') { next.fontSize = Math.round(baseFontSize * 1.15); next.bold = true; }

    /* ---------- РРЅР»Р°Р№РЅ-СЃС‚РёР»Рё С‚РµРіР°РјРё ---------- */
    if (tag === 'b' || tag === 'strong') next.bold = true;
    if (tag === 'i' || tag === 'em') next.italic = true;
    if (tag === 'u') next.underline = true;
    if (tag === 's' || tag === 'strike' || tag === 'del') next.strike = true;

    if (tag === 'a') {
      const href = el.getAttribute('href') || '';
      if (href) next.href = href;
      next.underline = true;
      if (!next.color) next.color = '#2563eb';
    }
    if (tag === 'mark') {
      const bg = el.style.backgroundColor || el.getAttribute('data-color');
      if (bg) next.bgColor = bg;
    }

    /* ---------- РРЅР»Р°Р№РЅ-СЃС‚РёР»Рё С‡РµСЂРµР· style="..." ---------- */
    const s = el.style;
    if (s) {
      if (s.color) next.color = s.color;
      if (s.backgroundColor) next.bgColor = s.backgroundColor;
      if (s.fontSize) {
        const n = parseFloat(s.fontSize);
        if (!Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0) next.fontSize = n;
      }
      if (s.fontFamily) {
        next.fontFamily = s.fontFamily.replace(/["']/g, '').split(',')[0].trim();
      }
      const fw = s.fontWeight;
      if (fw === 'bold' || (fw && parseInt(fw, 10) >= 600)) next.bold = true;
      if (s.fontStyle === 'italic') next.italic = true;
      const td = s.textDecoration || '';
      if (td.includes('underline')) next.underline = true;
      if (td.includes('line-through')) next.strike = true;
    }

    const isBlock = tag === 'p' || tag === 'div' || tag === 'h1' || tag === 'h2' || tag === 'h3';

    if (isBlock) {
      /* <p></p> Рё <p><br></p> СЃС‡РёС‚Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹РјРё вЂ” РґР»СЏ РЅРёС…
         РїР°СЂР°РіСЂР°С„РЅС‹Р№ СЂР°Р·СЂС‹РІ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РїСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
         СЃС…Р»РѕРїС‹РІР°РЅРёРµ РЅРµ СЃСЉРµР»Рѕ РїСѓСЃС‚СѓСЋ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ. */
      const wasEmpty = !(el.textContent || '').trim();

      el.childNodes.forEach((child) => walk(child, next, listDepth));
      pushParagraphBreak(style, wasEmpty);
      return;
    }

    el.childNodes.forEach((child) => walk(child, next, listDepth));
  }

  walk(doc.body, {});

  /* Р’РµРґСѓС‰РёРµ \n РќР• РѕР±СЂРµР·Р°РµРј вЂ” РѕРЅРё РґР°СЋС‚ РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї СЃРІРµСЂС…Сѓ Р±Р»РѕРєР°,
     РµСЃР»Рё РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ РѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёР» РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ Р°Р±Р·Р°С†С‹ РїРµСЂРµРґ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРј. */

  /* Trailing вЂ” РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј РѕРґРёРЅ \n\n (С‚РµС…РЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№). */
  if (runs.length) {
    const last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    if (last.text === '\n\n' || last.text === '\n') runs.pop();
  }

  return runs;
}

/* ============================================================ */

export function baseToRun(base: BaseStyle): Omit<TextRun, 'text'> {
  return {
    bold: base.fontStyle.includes('bold'),
    italic: base.fontStyle.includes('italic'),
    underline: base.textDecoration.includes('underline'),
    strike: base.textDecoration.includes('line-through'),
    color: base.fill,
    fontSize: base.fontSize,
    fontFamily: base.fontFamily,
  };
}

import type {
  BaseStyle, CellAlign, CellVerticalAlign,
  RichTableCell, RichTableCellPadding, RichTableData, RichBlock,
} from '../types';

export type ParseCellBlocksFn = (cellEl: HTMLElement, base: BaseStyle) => RichBlock[];

export function parseTableEl(
  table: HTMLElement,
  base: BaseStyle,
  parseCellBlocks: ParseCellBlocksFn,
): RichTableData | null {
  if (!table) return null;

  const trList = Array.from(
    table.querySelectorAll(
      ':scope > thead > tr, :scope > tbody > tr, :scope > tfoot > tr, :scope > tr',
    ),
  );

  const cells: RichTableCell[] = [];
  const grid: (RichTableCell | null)[][] = [];

  const ensureRow = (r: number) => { while (grid.length <= r) grid.push([]); };
  const isOccupied = (r: number, c: number): boolean => {
    const row = grid[r];
    if (!row) return false;
    return row[c] !== undefined && row[c] !== null;
  };

  trList.forEach((tr, r) => {
    ensureRow(r);
    let c = 0;
    while (isOccupied(r, c)) c++;

    const cellEls = Array.from(tr.childNodes).filter(
      (ch): ch is HTMLElement =>
        ch.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE &&
        ((ch as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'td' ||
          (ch as HTMLElement).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'th'),
    );

    for (const cellEl of cellEls) {
      while (isOccupied(r, c)) c++;

      const colspan = Math.max(1, parseInt(cellEl.getAttribute('colspan') || '1', 10) || 1);
      const rowspan = Math.max(1, parseInt(cellEl.getAttribute('rowspan') || '1', 10) || 1);
      const isHeader = cellEl.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'th';

      const cell: RichTableCell = {
        blocks: parseCellBlocks(cellEl, base),
        isHeader,
        colspan,
        rowspan,
        rowIndex: r,
        colIndex: c,
        rawWidths: parseRawWidths(cellEl),
        bgColor: cellEl.style.backgroundColor || undefined,
        borderColor: cellEl.style.borderColor || undefined,
        borderWidth: parseFloatOrUndef(cellEl.style.borderWidth),
        borderStyle: cellEl.style.borderStyle || undefined,
        padding: parsePadding(cellEl),
        align: readCellAlign(cellEl, isHeader),
        verticalAlign: readCellVerticalAlign(cellEl),
      };
      cells.push(cell);

      for (let rr = 0; rr < rowspan; rr++) {
        ensureRow(r + rr);
        for (let cc = 0; cc < colspan; cc++) {
          grid[r + rr][c + cc] = cell;
        }
      }
      c += colspan;
    }
  });

  let colCount = 0;
  for (const row of grid) {
    if (!row) continue;
    for (let i = row.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (row[i] !== undefined && row[i] !== null) {
        if (i + 1 > colCount) colCount = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  if (colCount === 0) colCount = 1;

  const colWidths = resolveColWidths(table, cells, colCount);

  return { cells, rowCount: grid.length, colCount, colWidths };
}

/* ---------- helpers ---------- */

const ALIGN_VALUES: CellAlign[] = ['left', 'center', 'right', 'justify'];

function normalizeAlign(v: string | undefined): CellAlign | null {
  const s = (v || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (s === 'start') return 'left';
  if (s === 'end') return 'right';
  if (ALIGN_VALUES.includes(s as CellAlign)) return s as CellAlign;
  return null;
}

function readCellAlign(cellEl: HTMLElement, _isHeader: boolean): CellAlign {
  const onCell = normalizeAlign(cellEl.style.textAlign);
  if (onCell) return onCell;
  const firstBlock = cellEl.querySelector(':scope > p, :scope > h1, :scope > h2, :scope > h3, :scope > div, :scope > blockquote, :scope > li') as HTMLElement | null;
  if (firstBlock) {
    const fromBlock = normalizeAlign(firstBlock.style.textAlign);
    if (fromBlock) return fromBlock;
  }
  const anyAligned = cellEl.querySelector('[style*="text-align"]') as HTMLElement | null;
  if (anyAligned) {
    const fromAny = normalizeAlign(anyAligned.style.textAlign);
    if (fromAny) return fromAny;
  }
  return 'left';
}

function readCellVerticalAlign(cellEl: HTMLElement): CellVerticalAlign {
  const onCell = (cellEl.style.verticalAlign || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (onCell === 'middle' || onCell === 'center') return 'middle';
  if (onCell === 'bottom') return 'bottom';
  if (onCell === 'top' || onCell === 'baseline') return 'top';
  const firstBlock = cellEl.firstElementChild as HTMLElement | null;
  if (firstBlock) {
    const v = (firstBlock.style.verticalAlign || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (v === 'middle' || v === 'center') return 'middle';
    if (v === 'bottom') return 'bottom';
  }
  return 'top';
}

function resolveColWidths(table: HTMLElement, cells: RichTableCell[], colCount: number): number[] | null {
  const fromCells = readCellWidths(cells, colCount);
  if (fromCells) return fromCells;
  const fromColgroup = readColgroup(table, colCount);
  if (fromColgroup && fromColgroup.hasRealWidth) return fromColgroup.widths;
  if (fromColgroup) return fromColgroup.widths;
  return null;
}

function findColgroup(table: HTMLElement): Element | null {
  for (const child of Array.from(table.children)) {
    if (child.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'colgroup') return child;
  }
  return null;
}

function readColgroup(table: HTMLElement, colCount: number): { widths: number[]; hasRealWidth: boolean } | null {
  const colgroup = findColgroup(table);
  if (!colgroup) return null;
  const colEls = Array.from(colgroup.children).filter(c => c.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'col') as HTMLElement[];
  if (colEls.length !== colCount) return null;
  const widths: number[] = new Array(colCount).fill(0);
  let hasRealWidth = false;
  colEls.forEach((colEl, i) => {
    const w = parseFloat(colEl.style.width);
    const aw = parseFloat(colEl.getAttribute('width') || '');
    if (!Number.isNaN(w) && w > 0) { widths[i] = w; hasRealWidth = true; }
    else if (!Number.isNaN(aw) && aw > 0) { widths[i] = aw; hasRealWidth = true; }
  });
  return { widths, hasRealWidth };
}

function readCellWidths(cells: RichTableCell[], colCount: number): number[] | null {
  const widths: number[] = new Array(colCount).fill(0);
  let any = false;
  for (const cell of cells) {
    if (!cell.rawWidths || !cell.rawWidths.length) continue;
    if (cell.rawWidths.length === cell.colspan) {
      for (let i = 0; i < cell.colspan; i++) {
        const col = cell.colIndex + i;
        if (col < colCount && widths[col] === 0) { widths[col] = cell.rawWidths[i]; any = true; }
      }
    } else if (cell.rawWidths.length === 1) {
      if (widths[cell.colIndex] === 0) { widths[cell.colIndex] = cell.rawWidths[0]; any = true; }
    }
  }
  return any ? widths : null;
}

function parseFloatOrUndef(v: string | null | undefined): number | undefined {
  if (!v) return undefined;
  const n = parseFloat(v);
  return Number.isNaN(n) ? undefined : n;
}

export function parsePadding(el: HTMLElement): RichTableCellPadding | undefined {
  const s = el.style;
  if (!s) return undefined;
  const top = parseFloat(s.paddingTop), right = parseFloat(s.paddingRight), bottom = parseFloat(s.paddingBottom), left = parseFloat(s.paddingLeft);
  const anySide = [top, right, bottom, left].some((n) => !Number.isNaN(n));
  let p: RichTableCellPadding | null = null;
  if (anySide) {
    p = { top: Number.isNaN(top) ? 0 : top, right: Number.isNaN(right) ? 0 : right, bottom: Number.isNaN(bottom) ? 0 : bottom, left: Number.isNaN(left) ? 0 : left };
  }
  const shorthand = s.padding;
  if (shorthand) {
    const parts = shorthand.trim().split(/\s+/).map((x) => parseFloat(x) || 0);
    if (parts.length === 1) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[0], bottom: parts[0], left: parts[0] };
    else if (parts.length === 2) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[0], left: parts[1] };
    else if (parts.length === 3) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[2], left: parts[1] };
    else if (parts.length >= 4) p = { top: parts[0], right: parts[1], bottom: parts[2], left: parts[3] };
  }
  return p ?? undefined;
}

function parseRawWidths(cellEl: HTMLElement): number[] | null {
  const fromAttr = cellEl.getAttribute('colwidth');
  if (fromAttr && fromAttr !== 'null') {
    const arr = fromAttr.split(',').map((x) => parseFloat(x.trim())).filter((n) => !Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0);
    if (arr.length) return arr;
  }
  const sw = cellEl.style?.width;
  if (sw) {
    const n = parseFloat(sw);
    if (!Number.isNaN(n) && n > 0) return [n];
  }
  return null;
}

/* ============================================================
   Fallback: plain text в†’ HTML РґР»СЏ РµРґРёРЅРѕРѕР±СЂР°Р·РЅРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР°.
   ============================================================ */

export function plainToHtml(text: string): string {
  const esc = (text || '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  return `<p>${esc.replace(/\n/g, '<br>')}</p>`;
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/richText/tableLayout.ts

   Р Р°СЃС‡С‘С‚ РіРµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёРё rich-С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†. Р§РёСЃС‚С‹Р№ РєРѕРґ Р±РµР· React/Konva.
   Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ Р»РµР¶Р°Р» РІ presentation/components/elements/tableLayout.ts
   Рё РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°Р»СЃСЏ РёР· lib/svg/exporters.ts в†’ lib в†’ presentation.
   ============================================================ */

import type {
  RichTableData, RichTableCell, RichTableCellPadding, RichBlock,
} from './types';
import { layoutRuns } from './layout';

export const CELL_PADDING_DEFAULT: RichTableCellPadding =
  { top: 8, right: 10, bottom: 8, left: 10 };

const MIN_ROW_HEIGHT = 12;

export const CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN = 8;

export const MAX_NEST_DEPTH = 4;

export type CellBlockItem =
  | { kind: 'text'; layout: ReturnType<typeof layoutRuns>; height: number }
  | { kind: 'table'; data: RichTableData; height: number };

export interface TableLayout {
  colOffsets: number[];
  rowOffsets: number[];
  cellItems: Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>;
  totalHeight: number;
  totalWidth: number;
}

export function cellPad(cell: RichTableCell): RichTableCellPadding {
  return cell.padding ?? CELL_PADDING_DEFAULT;
}

export function lineWidth(line: { width: number }[]): number {
  let w = 0;
  for (const r of line) w += r.width;
  return w;
}

export function computeTableLayout(
  data: RichTableData,
  width: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
  
): TableLayout {
  const { cells, rowCount, colCount } = data;

  const colPixelWidths = resolveColPixelWidths(data, width, colCount);
  const colOffsets = buildOffsets(colPixelWidths);

  const { cellItems, cellNaturalHeights } = measureCells(
    cells, colOffsets, colCount, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef,
  );

  const rowHeights = resolveRowHeights(cells, rowCount, cellNaturalHeights);
  const rowOffsets = buildOffsets(rowHeights);

  return {
    colOffsets,
    rowOffsets,
    cellItems,
    totalHeight: rowOffsets[rowOffsets.length - 1] ?? 0,
    totalWidth: colOffsets[colOffsets.length - 1] ?? 0,
  };
}

export function measureTableHeight(
  data: RichTableData,
  width: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): number {
  if (!data.cells.length) return 0;
  return computeTableLayout(data, width, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef).totalHeight;
}

function resolveColPixelWidths(
  data: RichTableData,
  totalWidth: number,
  colCount: number,
): number[] {
  const { colWidths } = data;
  if (!colWidths || colWidths.length !== colCount) {
    return Array(colCount).fill(totalWidth / colCount);
  }
  const explicit = colWidths.map((w) => (typeof w === 'number' && w > 0 ? w : 0));
  const totalExplicit = explicit.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const autoCount = explicit.filter((w) => w === 0).length;

  if (totalExplicit === 0) return Array(colCount).fill(totalWidth / colCount);
  if (autoCount === 0) return explicit.map((w) => (w / totalExplicit) * totalWidth);
  const remaining = Math.max(0, totalWidth - totalExplicit);
  const autoWidth = remaining / autoCount;
  return explicit.map((w) => (w > 0 ? w : autoWidth));
}

function buildOffsets(widths: number[]): number[] {
  const offsets = [0];
  for (let i = 0; i < widths.length; i++) offsets.push(offsets[i] + widths[i]);
  return offsets;
}

function measureCells(
  cells: RichTableCell[],
  colOffsets: number[],
  colCount: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): {
  cellItems: Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>;
  cellNaturalHeights: Map<RichTableCell, number>;
} {
  const cellItems = new Map<RichTableCell, CellBlockItem[]>();
  const cellNaturalHeights = new Map<RichTableCell, number>();

  for (const cell of cells) {
    const p = cellPad(cell);
    const cw = colOffsets[Math.min(cell.colIndex + cell.colspan, colCount)]
             - colOffsets[cell.colIndex];
    const innerW = Math.max(10, cw - p.left - p.right);
    const items = measureCellContent(cell, innerW, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef);
    const contentHeight = items.reduce((sum, it) => sum + it.height, 0)
      + Math.max(0, items.length - 1) * CELL_BLOCK_MARGIN;
    const minCellHeight = fallbackFontSize * lineHeightCoef + p.top + p.bottom;

    cellItems.set(cell, items);
    cellNaturalHeights.set(cell, Math.max(MIN_ROW_HEIGHT, minCellHeight, contentHeight + p.top + p.bottom));
  }
  return { cellItems, cellNaturalHeights };
}

function measureCellContent(
  cell: RichTableCell,
  innerWidth: number,
  fallbackFontSize: number,
  lineHeightCoef: number,
): CellBlockItem[] {
  const items: CellBlockItem[] = [];
  for (const block of cell.blocks as RichBlock[]) {
    if (block.kind === 'text') {
      const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef);
      items.push({ kind: 'text', layout, height: layout.totalHeight });
    } else {
      const h = measureTableHeight(block.data, innerWidth, fallbackFontSize, lineHeightCoef);
      items.push({ kind: 'table', data: block.data, height: h });
    }
  }
  return items;
}

function resolveRowHeights(
  cells: RichTableCell[],
  rowCount: number,
  cellNaturalHeights: Map<RichTableCell, number>,
): number[] {
  const rowHeights: number[] = new Array(rowCount).fill(MIN_ROW_HEIGHT);

  for (const cell of cells) {
    if (cell.rowspan !== 1) continue;
    const h = cellNaturalHeights.get(cell) ?? 0;
    if (h > rowHeights[cell.rowIndex]) rowHeights[cell.rowIndex] = h;
  }

  for (let iter = 0; iter < 10; iter++) {
    let changed = false;
    for (const cell of cells) {
      if (cell.rowspan <= 1) continue;
      const need = cellNaturalHeights.get(cell) ?? 0;
      let have = 0;
      for (let r = cell.rowIndex; r < cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan; r++) {
        have += rowHeights[r] ?? 0;
      }
      if (have + 0.5 < need) {
        const delta = (need - have) / cell.rowspan;
        for (let r = cell.rowIndex; r < cell.rowIndex + cell.rowspan; r++) {
          rowHeights[r] = (rowHeights[r] ?? 0) + delta;
        }
        changed = true;
      }
    }
    if (!changed) break;
  }
  return rowHeights;
}

// src/lib/serverExport.ts
/* ============================================================
   lib/serverExport.ts
   F-IO-11, F-IO-12, F-EI-01..04, NFR-SEC-06.
   РћС‚РїСЂР°РІРєР° СЃР»Р°Р№РґР° РЅР° СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂ СЌРєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚Р° в†’ Blob в†’ download.
   ============================================================ */

import type { Slide } from '../shared/types';
import { CONFIG } from '../shared/config';
import { embedImagesInSlide } from './embedImages';
import { download } from './download';

const TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;

function buildPayload(slide: Slide, extraOptions: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    slides: [slide],
    options: {
      embedImages: true,
      prettyPrint: false,
      ...extraOptions,
    },
  };
}

async function postForBlob(
  url: string,
  body: unknown,
  signal: AbortSignal,
): Promise<Blob> {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
    signal,
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const errText = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    throw new Error(`Server returned ${res.status}. ${errText}`);
  }
  return res.blob();
}

/**
 * F-IO-11: SVG С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂ (СЃ embed images).
 */
export async function exportSvgServer(slide: Slide): Promise<void> {
  const prepared = await embedImagesInSlide(slide);
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS);

  try {
    const blob = await postForBlob(
      `${CONFIG.EXPORT_API}/svg`,
      buildPayload(prepared),
      controller.signal,
    );
    const filename = `${(slide.name || 'slide').replace(/[^\w.-]+/g, '_')}.svg`;
    downloadBlob(blob, filename);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }
}

/**
 * F-IO-12: PDF/A-2b С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂ (СЃ embed images).
 */
export async function exportPdfaServer(slide: Slide): Promise<void> {
  const prepared = await embedImagesInSlide(slide);
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS);

  try {
    const blob = await postForBlob(
      `${CONFIG.EXPORT_API}/pdf?mode=pdfa`,
      buildPayload(prepared, { pdfVersion: 'pdf/a-2b' }),
      controller.signal,
    );
    const filename = `${(slide.name || 'slide').replace(/[^\w.-]+/g, '_')}.pdf`;
    downloadBlob(blob, filename);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }
}

/* ---------- download ---------- */

function downloadBlob(blob: Blob, filename: string): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '../../shared/types';

const INK = '#0a2540';       /* Р±Р°РЅРєРѕРІСЃРєРёР№ С‚С‘РјРЅРѕ-СЃРёРЅРёР№ */
const INK_SOFT = '#374151';  /* РґР»СЏ РІСЃРїРѕРјРѕРіР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° */

const shape = (
  o: Partial<StampShapeItem> & { kind: StampShapeItem['kind'] },
): StampShapeItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100,
  rotation: 0,
  cornerRadius: 0,
  stroke: INK, strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid',
  fill: undefined,
  ...o,
});

const text = (
  o: Partial<StampTextItem> & { text: string; mode: 'straight' | 'arc' },
): StampTextItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
  arcCx: 100, arcCy: 100, arcRadius: 60,
  arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
  arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
  fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
  textDecoration: '', fill: INK,
  ...o,
});

   lib/stamps/bankingStampsFilled.ts

   РљР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ С€С‚Р°РјРї РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ anchor-Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєСѓ:
   - РµСЃС‚СЊ РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂРЅР°СЏ С„РёРіСѓСЂР° (РѕР±РІРѕРґРєР°);
   - РµСЃС‚СЊ fill-СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚, РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅС‹Р№ Рє РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂСѓ С‡РµСЂРµР·
     fillTargetShapeId;
   - fillAnchorLocal вЂ” РґРѕР»СЏ (0..1) РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё bbox РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂР°,
     СѓРєР°Р·С‹РІР°СЋС‰Р°СЏ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ СЃРёРґР° flood fill.

   Р’ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРµ С‚Р°РєСѓСЋ Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєСѓ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕРјРµРЅСЏС‚СЊ С†РІРµС‚ РёР»Рё СЃРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ,

import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { uid } from '../uid';
import type { StampTemplate } from './types';

const INK = '#0a2540';
const SOFT = '#374151';
const WHITE = '#ffffff';
const GREEN = '#15803d';
const GREEN_LIGHT = '#dcfce7';
const RED = '#b91c1c';
const RED_LIGHT = '#fecaca';
const BLUE = '#1e40af';
const BLUE_LIGHT = '#dbeafe';
const YELLOW = '#ca8a04';
const YELLOW_LIGHT = '#fef9c3';

/* ============================================================
   Р¤Р°Р±СЂРёРєРё
   ============================================================ */

const shape = (
  o: Partial<StampShapeItem> & { kind: StampShapeItem['kind'] },
): StampShapeItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100,
  rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
  stroke: INK, strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid',
  fill: undefined,
  ...o,
});

const text = (
  o: Partial<StampTextItem> & { text: string; mode: 'straight' | 'arc' },
): StampTextItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
  arcCx: 100, arcCy: 100, arcRadius: 60,
  arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
  arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
  fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
  textDecoration: '', fill: INK,
  ...o,
});

/**
 * Anchor-Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєР°: РїРѕР»РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂСЏРµС‚ РіРµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёСЋ С†РµР»РµРІРѕРіРѕ РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂР°.
 * bbox = РІРµСЃСЊ С€С‚Р°РјРї, seed Р·Р°РґР°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РІ РґРѕР»СЏС… РѕС‚ bbox РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂР°.
 */
function anchorFill(
  container: StampShapeItem,
  color: string,
  anchorLocal: { x: number; y: number },
  stampW: number,
  stampH: number,
  name = 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°',
): StampShapeItem {
  return {
    id: uid(),
    kind: 'fill',
    x: 0, y: 0,
    width: stampW, height: stampH,
    rotation: 0,
    cornerRadius: 0,
    stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    fill: color,
    name,
    fillTargetShapeId: container.id,
    fillAnchorLocal: anchorLocal,
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   1. РљР РЈР“Р›РђРЇ РџР•Р§РђРўР¬ РЎ Р—РђР›РРўР«Рњ РљРћР›Р¬Р¦РћРњ
   ============================================================ */

function filledRingSeal(): StampTemplate {
  const W = 200, H = 200;
  const CX = 100, CY = 100;
  const R_OUTER = 96;
  const R_INNER = 74;

  const outer = shape({
    kind: 'ellipse',
    x: CX - R_OUTER, y: CY - R_OUTER,
    width: R_OUTER * 2, height: R_OUTER * 2,
    strokeWidth: 2, stroke: WHITE,
  });
  const inner = shape({
    kind: 'ellipse',
    x: CX - R_INNER, y: CY - R_INNER,
    width: R_INNER * 2, height: R_INNER * 2,
    strokeWidth: 1, stroke: WHITE,
  });

  /* Anchor РІ РєРѕР»СЊС†Рµ: РјРµР¶РґСѓ R_INNER Рё R_OUTER в†’ РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ y = 15 */
  const ringFill = anchorFill(
    outer, INK,
    { x: 0.5, y: 0.075 },  /* (100, 15) РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РєРѕР»СЊС†Р° */
    W, H, 'РљРѕР»СЊС†Рѕ РїРµС‡Р°С‚Рё',
  );

  return {
    id: 'bank-filled-ring',
    name: 'РџРµС‡Р°С‚СЊ СЃ РєРѕР»СЊС†РѕРј',
    description: 'РљСЂСѓРіР»Р°СЏ РїРµС‡Р°С‚СЊ СЃ Р·Р°Р»РёС‚С‹Рј РєРѕР»СЊС†РѕРј',
    icon: 'рџ”µ',
    width: W, height: H,
    shapes: [ringFill, outer, inner],
    texts: [
      text({
        text: 'Р‘РђРќРљ Р РћРЎРЎРР', mode: 'arc',
        arcCx: CX, arcCy: CY, arcRadius: 85,
        arcStartDeg: -160, arcEndDeg: -20, arcSpanDeg: 140,
        fontSize: 11, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: WHITE,
      }),
      text({
        text: 'РћР“Р Рќ 1027700132195', mode: 'arc',
        arcCx: CX, arcCy: CY, arcRadius: 85,
        arcStartDeg: 160, arcEndDeg: 20, arcFlip: true, arcSpanDeg: 140,
        fontSize: 8, fill: WHITE,
      }),
      text({
        text: 'РњРћРЎРљР’Рђ', mode: 'straight',
        x: CX - 60, y: CY - 18, width: 120,
        fontSize: 14, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: INK,
      }),
      text({
        text: 'РРќРќ 7707083893', mode: 'straight',
        x: CX - 60, y: CY + 4, width: 120,
        fontSize: 9, fill: SOFT,
      }),
    ],
  };
}

function sealWithEmblem(): StampTemplate {
  const W = 200, H = 200;
  const CX = 100, CY = 100;
  const R_OUTER = 96;
  const R_INNER = 76;
  const R_EMBLEM = 38;

  const outer = shape({
    kind: 'ellipse',
    x: CX - R_OUTER, y: CY - R_OUTER,
    width: R_OUTER * 2, height: R_OUTER * 2,
    strokeWidth: 2,
  });
  const inner = shape({
    kind: 'ellipse',
    x: CX - R_INNER, y: CY - R_INNER,
    width: R_INNER * 2, height: R_INNER * 2,
    strokeWidth: 1,
  });
  const emblem = shape({
    kind: 'ellipse',

/* ============================================================
   7. РџРћР›РћРЎРђРўР«Р™ РЁРўРђРњРџ (РґРёР°РіРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»РѕСЃС‹)
   ============================================================ */

function stripedStamp(): StampTemplate {
  const W = 260, H = 90;

  const frame = shape({
    kind: 'rect', x: 2, y: 2, width: W - 4, height: H - 4,
    cornerRadius: 4, strokeWidth: 2,
  });
  /* Р–С‘Р»С‚Р°СЏ РїРѕРґР»РѕР¶РєР° */
  const inner = shape({
    kind: 'rect', x: 6, y: 6, width: W - 12, height: H - 12,
    cornerRadius: 3, strokeWidth: 0, stroke: undefined,
  });
  const innerFill = anchorFill(
    inner, YELLOW_LIGHT,
    { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 },
    W, H, 'Р–С‘Р»С‚С‹Р№ С„РѕРЅ',
  );

  /* 3 С‚РѕРЅРєРёРµ РґРёР°РіРѕРЅР°Р»Рё РІ РїСЂР°РІРѕР№ С‚СЂРµС‚Рё */
  const angle = 55;
  const makeDiagonal = (x0: number): StampShapeItem => {
    const dx = Math.tan((angle * Math.PI) / 180) * H;
    return shape({
      kind: 'line',
      x: x0, y: 6,
      width: Math.abs(dx), height: H - 12,
      points: [0, H - 12, dx, 0],
      stroke: YELLOW, strokeWidth: 1.5, dash: 'solid',
    });
  };

  const d1 = makeDiagonal(W - 100);
  const d2 = makeDiagonal(W - 70);
  const d3 = makeDiagonal(W - 40);

  return {
    id: 'bank-striped',
    name: 'РџРѕР»РѕСЃР°С‚С‹Р№ РєР»РёС€-С€С‚Р°РјРї',
    description: 'РћС„РѕСЂРјРёС‚РµР»СЊСЃРєРёР№ С€С‚Р°РјРї СЃ РґРёР°РіРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹РјРё РїРѕР»РѕСЃР°РјРё',
    icon: 'рџЋ—',
    width: W, height: H,
    shapes: [innerFill, frame, inner, d1, d2, d3],
    texts: [
      text({
        text: 'РџР РРќРЇРўРћ Рљ РРЎРџРћР›РќР•РќРР®', mode: 'straight',
        x: 14, y: 30, width: W - 130,
        fontSize: 15, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: INK, align: 'left',
      }),
      text({
        text: 'Р”Р°С‚Р° Рё РїРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ РѕРїРµСЂР°С†РёРѕРЅРёСЃС‚Р°', mode: 'straight',
        x: 14, y: 58, width: W - 130,
        fontSize: 8, fill: SOFT, align: 'left',

/* ============================================================

function cashierSignature(): StampTemplate {
  const W = 240, H = 110;

  const frame = shape({
    kind: 'rect', x: 2, y: 2, width: W - 4, height: H - 4,

  });

  const sigZone = shape({
    kind: 'rect', x: 12, y: 62, width: W - 24, height: 38,
    cornerRadius: 3, strokeWidth: 1, stroke: SOFT, dash: 'dotted',
  });
  const sigFill = anchorFill(
    sigZone, BLUE_LIGHT,

  );

  return {
    id: 'bank-cashier-signature',
    name: 'РЁС‚Р°РјРї РєР°СЃСЃРёСЂР° (СЃ Р·РѕРЅРѕР№)',
    description: 'РљР°СЃСЃРѕРІС‹Р№ С€С‚Р°РјРї СЃ РїР»Р°С€РєРѕР№ РґР»СЏ РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРё',
    icon: 'рџ–‹',
    width: W, height: H,
    shapes: [sigFill, frame, sigZone],
    texts: [
      text({
        text: 'РћРџР•Р РђР¦РРћРќРќРђРЇ РљРђРЎРЎРђ', mode: 'straight',
        x: 12, y: 10, width: W - 24,
        fontSize: 12, fontStyle: 'bold', fill: INK,
      }),
      text({
        text: 'РћРїРµСЂР°С†РёСЏ в„–: ____________', mode: 'straight',
        x: 12, y: 34, width: W - 24,
        fontSize: 10, align: 'left', fill: INK,
      }),
      text({
        text: 'Р”Р°С‚Р°: В«___В» ________ 20___', mode: 'straight',
        x: 12, y: 48, width: W - 24,
        fontSize: 9, align: 'left', fill: SOFT,
      }),
      text({
        text: 'РџРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ РєР°СЃСЃРёСЂР°', mode: 'straight',
        x: 12, y: 70, width: W - 24,

   РІРЅРµС€РЅРёС… С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ. РљР°Р¶РґРѕРµ РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚
   shapes/texts/images, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РїРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚СЊ, Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РёР»Рё
   СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РІ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂРµ С€С‚Р°РјРїР°.

   РўРµС…РЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРё StampImageItem СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚:
     - src: data URL РёР»Рё http(s)
     - x, y, width, height
     - fit: 'contain' | 'cover' | 'fill'
     - rotation, name, locked, hidden

/* ------------------------------------------------------------

const STAR_SVG = `

        fill="${GOLD}" stroke="${WHITE}" stroke-width="1"/>
</svg>`;

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   QR-Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РєР° вЂ” РєРІР°РґСЂР°С‚ СЃ РїР°С‚С‚РµСЂРЅРѕРј
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */

function qrSvg(seed = 1): string {
  /* РџСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕР№ РїСЃРµРІРґРѕ-QR: 8x8 СЃРµС‚РєР°, РґРµС‚РµСЂРјРёРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕ seed */
  const cells: string[] = [];
  const size = 100;
  const n = 8;
  const cellSize = size / n;

  /* РџСЃРµРІРґРѕСЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№РЅС‹Р№ РґРµС‚РµСЂРјРёРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РіРµРЅРµСЂР°С‚РѕСЂ */
  let state = seed * 9301 + 49297;
  const rnd = () => {
    state = (state * 9301 + 49297) % 233280;
    return state / 233280;
  };

  for (let y = 0; y < n; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < n; x++) {
      /* РЈРіР»РѕРІС‹Рµ РјР°СЂРєРµСЂС‹ (РєР°Рє РІ РЅР°СЃС‚РѕСЏС‰РµРј QR) */
      const isCorner =
        (x < 3 && y < 3) ||
        (x >= n - 3 && y < 3) ||
        (x < 3 && y >= n - 3);
      /* Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРё СѓРіР»РѕРІС‹С… РјР°СЂРєРµСЂРѕРІ вЂ” С‚РѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЂРёСЃСѓРЅРѕРє */
      const inCornerFrame =
        (x === 0 || x === 2 || y === 0 || y === 2) && x < 3 && y < 3;
      const innerCorner = x === 1 && y === 1 && false;

      if (isCorner) {
        if (inCornerFrame || innerCorner) {
          cells.push(
            `<rect x="${x * cellSize}" y="${y * cellSize}" width="${cellSize}" height="${cellSize}" fill="${INK}"/>`,
          );
        } else if (x === 1 && y === 1) {
          cells.push(
            `<rect x="${x * cellSize}" y="${y * cellSize}" width="${cellSize}" height="${cellSize}" fill="${INK}"/>`,
          );
        }
      } else if (rnd() > 0.5) {
        cells.push(
          `<rect x="${x * cellSize}" y="${y * cellSize}" width="${cellSize}" height="${cellSize}" fill="${INK}"/>`,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  return `
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100">
  <rect width="100" height="100" fill="${WHITE}"/>
  ${cells.join('')}
</svg>`;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   РџРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ (С„Р°РєСЃРёРјРёР»Рµ) вЂ” СЃС‚РёР»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅР°СЏ В«Р·Р°РєРѕСЂСЋС‡РєР°В»
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */

        fill="none" stroke="${INK}" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"/>
</svg>`;

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Р›РѕРіРѕС‚РёРї Р±Р°РЅРєР° вЂ” С‚СЂРё РїРѕР»РѕСЃС‹ (СЃС‚РёР»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№)

   РљРѕРЅРІРµСЂС‚ вЂ” РґР»СЏ РІС…РѕРґСЏС‰РµР№ РєРѕСЂСЂРµСЃРїРѕРЅРґРµРЅС†РёРё

/* ============================================================
   РљРѕРґРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ SVG РІ data URL
   ============================================================ */

function svgToDataUrl(svg: string): string {
  const cleaned = svg.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  /* encodeURIComponent вЂ” РЅР°РґС‘Р¶РЅРµРµ base64 РґР»СЏ РёРЅР»Р°Р№РЅ SVG */
  return `data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,${encodeURIComponent(cleaned)}`;
}

   Р¤Р°Р±СЂРёРєРё
   ============================================================ */

const shape = (
  o: Partial<StampShapeItem> & { kind: StampShapeItem['kind'] },
): StampShapeItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100,
  rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
  stroke: INK, strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid',
  fill: undefined,
  ...o,
});

const text = (
  o: Partial<StampTextItem> & { text: string; mode: 'straight' | 'arc' },
): StampTextItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
  arcCx: 100, arcCy: 100, arcRadius: 60,
  arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
  arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
  fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
  textDecoration: '', fill: INK,
  ...o,
});

const image = (
  src: string,
  x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number,
  name = 'РР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµ',
): StampImageItem => ({
  id: uid(),
  src,
  x, y, width: w, height: h,
  rotation: 0,
  fit: 'contain',
  name,
});

  return {
    id: 'bank-img-qr-stamp',
    name: 'РЁС‚Р°РјРї СЃ QR-РєРѕРґРѕРј',
    description: 'РЁС‚Р°РјРї СЃ QR РґР»СЏ Р±С‹СЃС‚СЂРѕР№ РёРґРµРЅС‚РёС„РёРєР°С†РёРё РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°',

      text({

import type {
  StampElement, StampShape,
  StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem, StampShapeKind,
} from '../../shared/types';
import { uid } from '../uid';
import type { StampTemplate } from './types';
import {
  computeNewShapeGeometry,
  findHostShape,
  computeArcParams,
  DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
  DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
  type PlacementMode,
} from './geometry';
import { getShapeStrategy } from './shapes';

/* ============================================================
   РћР±С‘СЂС‚РєРё РґР»СЏ StampElement
   ============================================================ */

export function newStampFromTemplate(
  t: StampTemplate,
  x = 200,
  y = 200,
): StampElement {
  return {
    id: uid(),
    type: 'stamp',
    x, y,
    width: t.width,
    height: t.height,
    rotation: 0,
    libraryId: t.id,
    shapes: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.shapes)),
    texts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.texts)),
    images: t.images ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t.images)) : [],
  };
}

export function newLegacyStamp(shape: StampShape, x = 200, y = 200): StampElement {
  return {
    id: uid(), type: 'stamp', shape, x, y,
    width: shape === 'rect' ? 260 : shape === 'oval' ? 240 : 200,
    height: shape === 'rect' ? 120 : shape === 'oval' ? 150 : shape === 'triangle' ? 173 : 200,
    rotation: 0, stampColor: '#1a1a1a',
    strokeWidth: 3, strokeDash: 'solid',
    innerStrokeWidth: 1, innerStrokeDash: 'solid', showInner: true,
    topText: 'РќРђР—Р’РђРќРР•', topFontSize: 14, topFontFamily: 'Arial',
    topFontStyle: 'bold', topTextDecoration: '', topColor: '#1a1a1a',
    centerText: '2026', centerFontSize: 20, centerFontFamily: 'Arial',
    centerFontStyle: 'bold', centerTextDecoration: '', centerColor: '#1a1a1a',
    bottomText: 'Р“РћР РћР”', bottomFontSize: 12, bottomFontFamily: 'Arial',
    bottomFontStyle: 'normal', bottomTextDecoration: '', bottomColor: '#1a1a1a',
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹ С€С‚Р°РјРїР°
   ============================================================ */

export type { PlacementMode };

export interface FactoryContext {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedType: 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;
  stampW: number;
  stampH: number;
  placementMode: PlacementMode;
}

function resolveHost(ctx: FactoryContext): StampShapeItem | null {
  if (ctx.selectedType !== 'shape' || !ctx.selectedId) return null;
  return ctx.shapes.find((s) => s.id === ctx.selectedId) || null;
}

export function createShape(kind: StampShapeKind, ctx: FactoryContext): StampShapeItem {
  const strategy = getShapeStrategy(kind);
  const def = strategy.defaultSize;
  const host = resolveHost(ctx);
  const geom = computeNewShapeGeometry(
    host, def.w, def.h, ctx.stampW, ctx.stampH, ctx.placementMode, 12,
  );
  const normalized = strategy.normalizeGeometry(geom);

  const shape: StampShapeItem = {
    id: uid(), kind,
    x: normalized.x, y: normalized.y,
    width: normalized.width, height: normalized.height,
    rotation: 0,
    cornerRadius: def.corner,
    stroke: '#1a1a1a',
    strokeWidth: 1,
    dash: 'solid',
  };
  if (kind === 'line') shape.points = [0, normalized.height / 2, normalized.width, normalized.height / 2];
  return shape;
}

export function createStraightText(ctx: FactoryContext): StampTextItem {
  const host = resolveHost(ctx);
  const w = 180, h = 24;
  const pos = host
    ? { x: host.x + (host.width - w) / 2, y: host.y + (host.height - h) / 2 }
    : { x: (ctx.stampW - w) / 2, y: (ctx.stampH - h) / 2 };

  return {
    id: uid(), text: 'РњРѕР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚', mode: 'straight',
    x: Math.round(pos.x), y: Math.round(pos.y), width: w, align: 'center',
    arcCx: ctx.stampW / 2, arcCy: ctx.stampH / 2, arcRadius: 60,
    arcStartDeg: -30, arcEndDeg: 30, arcFlip: false,
    arcOffset: DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
    arcSpanDeg: DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
    fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
    textDecoration: '', fill: '#1a1a1a',
  };
}

export function createArcText(ctx: FactoryContext): StampTextItem {
  let host = resolveHost(ctx);
  if (!host) host = findHostShape(ctx.stampW / 2, ctx.stampH / 2, '', ctx.shapes);

  const fakeHost: StampShapeItem = host || {
    id: 'fake', kind: 'ellipse',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: ctx.stampW, height: ctx.stampH,
    rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0, stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
  };

  const params = computeArcParams(
    fakeHost, 14, 'РњРѕР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚'.length, true, 0,
    DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET, DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
  );

  return {
    id: uid(), text: 'РњРѕР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚', mode: 'arc',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, align: 'center',
    ...params,
    arcOffset: DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
    arcSpanDeg: DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
    arcHostId: host ? host.id : undefined,
    fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal',
    textDecoration: '', fill: '#1a1a1a',
  };
}

export function createImage(
  src: string, w: number, h: number,
  stampW: number, stampH: number,
): StampImageItem {
  return {
    id: uid(), src,
    x: Math.round((stampW - w) / 2),
    y: Math.round((stampH - h) / 2),
    width: w, height: h,
    fit: 'contain',
    name: 'РљР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєР°',
  };
}

export function createFillSubtract(
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  selectedId: string | null,
): StampShapeItem | null {
  let outer = selectedId
    ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId && s.kind !== 'fill') || null
    : null;

  if (!outer) {
    const candidates = shapes.filter(
      (s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden && s.kind !== 'line',
    );
    if (!candidates.length) return null;
    outer = candidates.reduce((a, b) =>
      a.width * a.height >= b.width * b.height ? a : b,
    );
  }

  const ox = outer.x, oy = outer.y, ow = outer.width, oh = outer.height;
  const holeIds: string[] = [];
  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === outer.id || s.kind === 'fill' || s.kind === 'line') continue;
    if (s.hidden) continue;
    const cx = s.x + s.width / 2, cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
    if (cx >= ox && cx <= ox + ow && cy >= oy && cy <= oy + oh) holeIds.push(s.id);
  }

  return {
    id: uid(), kind: 'fill',
    x: ox, y: oy, width: ow, height: oh,
    rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
    stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    fill: '#bfdbfe',
    name: 'Р’С‹С‡РёС‚Р°РЅРёРµ',
    fillMode: 'subtract',
    fillOuterId: outer.id,
    fillHoleIds: holeIds,
  };
}

export function createFillIntersect(
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  selectedId: string | null,
): StampShapeItem | null {
  let a = selectedId
    ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId && s.kind !== 'fill') || null
    : null;

  if (!a) {
    const candidates = shapes.filter(
      (s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden && s.kind !== 'line',
    );
    if (candidates.length < 2) return null;
    a = candidates[0];
  }

  const others = shapes.filter(
    (s) => s.id !== a!.id && s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden && s.kind !== 'line',
  );
  if (!others.length) return null;

  const interArea = (p: StampShapeItem, q: StampShapeItem) => {
    const x1 = Math.max(p.x, q.x), y1 = Math.max(p.y, q.y);
    const x2 = Math.min(p.x + p.width, q.x + q.width);
    const y2 = Math.min(p.y + p.height, q.y + q.height);
    return Math.max(0, x2 - x1) * Math.max(0, y2 - y1);
  };

  let b = others[0];
  let best = interArea(a, b);
  for (const o of others) {
    const v = interArea(a, o);
    if (v > best) { best = v; b = o; }
  }

  const minX = Math.min(a.x, b.x), minY = Math.min(a.y, b.y);
  const maxX = Math.max(a.x + a.width, b.x + b.width);
  const maxY = Math.max(a.y + a.height, b.y + b.height);

  return {
    id: uid(), kind: 'fill',
    x: minX, y: minY,
    width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
    height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
    rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
    stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    fill: '#fca5a5',
    name: 'РџРµСЂРµСЃРµС‡РµРЅРёРµ',
    fillMode: 'intersect',
    fillOuterId: a.id,
    fillHoleIds: [b.id],
  };
}

export function createFillBetween(
  outer: StampShapeItem,
  holes: StampShapeItem[],
  color: string,
  name = 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё',
): StampShapeItem {
  return {
    id: uid(), kind: 'fill',
    x: outer.x, y: outer.y,
    width: Math.max(1, outer.width),
    height: Math.max(1, outer.height),
    rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
    stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
    fill: color,
    name,
    fillMode: 'subtract',
    fillOuterId: outer.id,
    fillHoleIds: holes.map((h) => h.id),
  };
}

import type { StampShapeItem, StampFillMode } from '../../shared/types';
import { getShapeStrategy } from './shapes/registry';

export function shapeToPath2D(s: StampShapeItem): Path2D {
  return getShapeStrategy(s.kind).toPath(s);
}

export interface FillParticipants {
  outer: StampShapeItem;
  holes: StampShapeItem[];
  mode: StampFillMode;
}

export function paintFill(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  { outer, holes, mode }: FillParticipants,
  color: string,
): void {
  const all = [outer, ...holes];
  const minX = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.x));
  const minY = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.y));
  const maxX = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.x + p.width));
  const maxY = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.y + p.height));

  ctx.save();
  if (mode === 'intersect' && holes.length > 0) {
    ctx.clip(shapeToPath2D(outer));
    for (const h of holes) ctx.clip(shapeToPath2D(h));
  } else {
    const combined = new Path2D();
    combined.addPath(shapeToPath2D(outer));
    for (const h of holes) combined.addPath(shapeToPath2D(h));
    ctx.clip(combined, 'evenodd');
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = color;
  ctx.fillRect(minX, minY, maxX - minX, maxY - minY);
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ============================================================
   lib/stamps/fillRasterizer.ts

   Singleton, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№:
   - РґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ РћР”РРќ Worker РЅР° РІСЃС‘ РїСЂРёР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ;
   - РєСЌС€РёСЂСѓРµС‚ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹ РїРѕ signature (LRU, РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј 30);
   - СЃРµСЂРёР°Р»РёР·СѓРµС‚ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃС‹ С‡РµСЂРµР· seq;
   - РїСЂРё РїР°РґРµРЅРёРё РІРѕСЂРєРµСЂР° СЃР»РёРІР°РµС‚ РІРёСЃСЏС‰РёРµ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃС‹ РІ main thread;
   - fallback РЅР° main thread С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ В«envВ»-РѕС€РёР±РѕРє
     (no-canvas / no-2d-context / no-path2d / serialize-failed).
     Р”РµС‚РµСЂРјРёРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РѕС‚РєР°Р·С‹ (empty-target-mask, no-seed-pixel Рё С‚.Рї.)
     РІ main thread РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂСЏС‚СЊ Р±РµСЃСЃРјС‹СЃР»РµРЅРЅРѕ вЂ” РѕРЅРё РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРёСЃСЏС‚ РѕС‚ РѕРєСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРёСЏ.
   ============================================================ */

import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../shared/types';
import {
  rasterizeCompositeFills,
  type FillSpec,
  type FillError,
} from './rasterFill';
import type {
  FillWorkerRequest,
  FillWorkerResponse,
} from '../../workers/protocol';

const CACHE_LIMIT = 30;

const ENVIRONMENT_REASONS: ReadonlySet<FillError> = new Set<FillError>([
  'no-canvas',
  'no-2d-context',
  'no-path2d',
  'serialize-failed',
]);

export interface FillCacheEntry {
  image: HTMLImageElement | null;
  error: FillError | null;
}

export interface FillRequest {
  key: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

type Listener = (key: string, entry: FillCacheEntry) => void;

class FillRasterizer {
  private worker: Worker | null = null;
  private workerBroken = false;

  private cache = new Map<string, FillCacheEntry>();
  private inflight = new Set<string>();
  private seqByKey = new Map<number, FillRequest>();
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>();
  private nextSeq = 0;

  constructor() {
    this.createWorker();
  }

  private createWorker(): void {
    try {
      const worker = new Worker(
        new URL('../../workers/fillWorker.ts', import.meta.url),
        { type: 'module' },
      );

      worker.onmessage = (e: MessageEvent<FillWorkerResponse>) => {
        void this.handleWorkerResponse(e.data);
      };

      worker.onerror = (err) => {
        this.handleWorkerFailure('worker crashed', err);
        try { worker.terminate(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        this.worker = null;
      };

      this.worker = worker;
    } catch (err) {
      this.handleWorkerFailure('worker unavailable', err);
      this.worker = null;
    }
  }

  private handleWorkerFailure(reason: string, err: unknown): void {
    if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
      console.warn(`[fill] ${reason}, falling back to main thread`, err);
    }
    this.workerBroken = true;

    const pending = Array.from(this.seqByKey.values());
    this.seqByKey.clear();
    this.inflight.clear();

    for (const req of pending) {
      this.inflight.add(req.key);
      void this.rasterizeOnMainThread(req);
    }
  }

  private async handleWorkerResponse(res: FillWorkerResponse): Promise<void> {
    const req = this.seqByKey.get(res.seq);
    if (!req) return;
    this.seqByKey.delete(res.seq);

    if (!res.ok) {
      if (ENVIRONMENT_REASONS.has(res.reason)) {
        if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
          console.warn('[fill] worker env-failure:', res.reason, 'вЂ” retrying in main thread');
        }
        await this.rasterizeOnMainThread(req);
        return;
      }
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
        console.warn('[fill] fill rejected:', res.reason);
      }
      this.store(req.key, { image: null, error: res.reason });
      return;
    }

    const image = await loadImage(res.blob);
    this.store(
      req.key,
      image
        ? { image, error: null }
        : { image: null, error: 'serialize-failed' },
    );
  }

  private async rasterizeOnMainThread(req: FillRequest): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const result = await rasterizeCompositeFills({
        width: req.width,
        height: req.height,
        shapes: req.shapes,
        fills: req.fills,
      });

      if (!result.ok) {
        if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
          console.warn('[fill] main-thread rejected:', result.reason);
        }
        this.store(req.key, { image: null, error: result.reason });
        return;
      }

      const image = await loadImage(result.value);
      this.store(
        req.key,
        image
          ? { image, error: null }
          : { image: null, error: 'serialize-failed' },
      );
    } catch (err) {
      if (import.meta.env.DEV) {
        console.error('[fill] main-thread rasterize threw', err);
      }
      this.store(req.key, { image: null, error: 'all-fills-rejected' });
    }
  }

  private store(key: string, entry: FillCacheEntry): void {
    /* LRU: Map СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РІСЃС‚Р°РІРєРё; РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРЅС‹Р№ set РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµС‚ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ. */
    this.cache.delete(key);
    this.cache.set(key, entry);
    if (this.cache.size > CACHE_LIMIT) {
      const oldest = this.cache.keys().next().value;
      if (oldest !== undefined) this.cache.delete(oldest);
    }
    this.inflight.delete(key);
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener(key, entry);
  }

  subscribe(listener: Listener): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  }

  get(key: string): FillCacheEntry | undefined {
    return this.cache.get(key);
  }

  isInflight(key: string): boolean {
    return this.inflight.has(key);
  }

  request(req: FillRequest): void {
    if (this.cache.has(req.key)) return;
    if (this.inflight.has(req.key)) return;

    this.inflight.add(req.key);

    if (this.worker && !this.workerBroken) {
      const seq = ++this.nextSeq;
      this.seqByKey.set(seq, req);

      const wire: FillWorkerRequest = {
        seq,
        width: req.width,
        height: req.height,
        shapes: req.shapes,
        fills: req.fills,
      };

      try {
        this.worker.postMessage(wire);
        return;
      } catch (err) {
        this.handleWorkerFailure('postMessage failed', err);
        this.seqByKey.delete(seq);
        try { this.worker?.terminate(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        this.worker = null;
      }
    }

    void this.rasterizeOnMainThread(req);
  }

  dispose(): void {
    try { this.worker?.terminate(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    this.worker = null;
    this.cache.clear();
    this.inflight.clear();
    this.seqByKey.clear();
    this.listeners.clear();
  }
}

function loadImage(blob: Blob): Promise<HTMLImageElement | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const img = new window.Image();
    img.onload = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); resolve(img); };
    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); resolve(null); };
    img.src = url;
  });
}

let singleton: FillRasterizer | null = null;

export function getFillRasterizer(): FillRasterizer {
  if (!singleton) singleton = new FillRasterizer();
  return singleton;
}

/** РўРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІ Рё hot-reload. */
export function resetFillRasterizer(): void {
  singleton?.dispose();
  singleton = null;
}

import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';

export const DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG = 320;
export const MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG = 20;
export const MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG = 350;
export const DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET = 6;

export interface ArcParams {
  arcCx: number;
  arcCy: number;
  arcRadius: number;
  /* Р­Р»Р»РёРїС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёРµ СЂР°РґРёСѓСЃС‹, РµСЃР»Рё РґСѓРіР° РЅРµ РєСЂСѓРіРѕРІР°СЏ. */
  arcRx?: number;
  arcRy?: number;
  arcStartDeg: number;
  arcEndDeg: number;
  arcFlip: boolean;
}

function arcRadiusFor(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  fontSize: number,
  isOutside: boolean,
  arcOffset: number,
): number {
  const baseR = Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2;
  const safeOffset = Math.max(0, arcOffset);
  if (isOutside) return baseR + safeOffset;
  return Math.max(fontSize * 0.8, baseR - safeOffset);
}

function resolveSpanDeg(
  fontSize: number,
  textLength: number,
  radius: number,
  explicitSpan?: number,
): number {
  if (explicitSpan !== undefined && explicitSpan > 0) {
    return Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, explicitSpan));
  }
  const charWidth = fontSize * 0.6;
  const textWidth = Math.max(charWidth, textLength * charWidth);
  const raw = (textWidth / Math.max(1, radius)) * (180 / Math.PI);
  return Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, raw));
}

export function computeArcParams(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  fontSize: number,
  textLength: number,
  isTop: boolean,
  ringOffset = 0,
  arcOffset = DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
  arcSpanDeg = DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
): ArcParams {
  const cx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const cy = host.y + host.height / 2;
  const radius = arcRadiusFor(host, fontSize, false, arcOffset) - ringOffset * 12;

  const span = resolveSpanDeg(fontSize, textLength, radius, arcSpanDeg);
  const half = span / 2;

  if (isTop) {
    return {
      arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
      arcStartDeg: -90 - half,
      arcEndDeg: -90 + half,
      arcFlip: false,
    };
  }
  return {
    arcCx: cx, arcCy: cy, arcRadius: radius,
    arcStartDeg: 90 + half,
    arcEndDeg: 90 - half,
    arcFlip: true,
  };
}

export function computeArcParamsForAngle(
  host: StampShapeItem,
  angleRad: number,
  fontSize: number,
  isOutside: boolean,
  spanDeg: number,
  arcOffset: number = DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
): ArcParams {
  const cx = host.x + host.width / 2;
  const cy = host.y + host.height / 2;
  const radius = arcRadiusFor(host, fontSize, isOutside, arcOffset);

  const centerDeg = (angleRad * 180) / Math.PI;
  const isTop = Math.sin(angleRad) < 0;
  const clampedSpan = Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, spanDeg));
  const half = clampedSpan / 2;

  const startDeg = isTop ? centerDeg - half : centerDeg + half;
  const endDeg = isTop ? centerDeg + half : centerDeg - half;

  return {
    arcCx: cx,
    arcCy: cy,
    arcRadius: radius,
    arcStartDeg: startDeg,
    arcEndDeg: endDeg,
    arcFlip: !isTop,
  };
}

export interface ArcBBox {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/**
 * bbox РґР»СЏ arc-С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°.
 *
 * Р”Р»СЏ РєСЂСѓРіР° вЂ” СЃС‚Р°СЂР°СЏ Р»РѕРіРёРєР° (radius РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РґР»СЏ РѕР±РѕРёС… РѕСЃРµР№).
 * Р”Р»СЏ СЌР»Р»РёРїСЃР° вЂ” РєРѕРѕСЂРґРёРЅР°С‚С‹ С‚РѕС‡РµРє СЃС‡РёС‚Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕ rx Рё ry РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅРѕ.
 */
export function arcTextBBox(t: {
  text: string;
  arcCx: number; arcCy: number;
  arcRadius: number;
  arcRx?: number;
  arcRy?: number;
  arcStartDeg: number; arcEndDeg: number;
  fontSize: number;
}): ArcBBox {
  const rx = t.arcRx ?? t.arcRadius;
  const ry = t.arcRy ?? t.arcRadius;

  const chars = t.text.split('');
  if (chars.length === 0) {
    return { x: t.arcCx - 20, y: t.arcCy - 20, width: 40, height: 40 };
  }
  const start = (t.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const end = (t.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  const step = chars.length > 1 ? (end - start) / (chars.length - 1) : 0;

  let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
  chars.forEach((_, i) => {
    const a = start + i * step;
    const x = t.arcCx + Math.cos(a) * rx;
    const y = t.arcCy + Math.sin(a) * ry;
    if (x < minX) minX = x;
    if (y < minY) minY = y;
    if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
    if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
  });
  const pad = t.fontSize;
  return {
    x: minX - pad, y: minY - pad,
    width: (maxX - minX) + pad * 2,
    height: (maxY - minY) + pad * 2,
  };
}

import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';

export interface Edge {
  x1: number; y1: number;
  x2: number; y2: number;
  len: number;
  dirX: number; dirY: number;
}

export function makeEdge(ax: number, ay: number, bx: number, by: number): Edge {
  const dx = bx - ax;
  const dy = by - ay;
  const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  return { x1: ax, y1: ay, x2: bx, y2: by, len, dirX: dx / len, dirY: dy / len };
}

/** signedOffset > 0 вЂ” РІРЅСѓС‚СЂСЊ, < 0 вЂ” СЃРЅР°СЂСѓР¶Рё. */
export function buildRectEdges(w: number, h: number, signedOffset: number): Edge[] {
  const x1 = signedOffset, y1 = signedOffset;
  const x2 = w - signedOffset, y2 = h - signedOffset;
  return [
    makeEdge(x1, y1, x2, y1),
    makeEdge(x2, y1, x2, y2),
    makeEdge(x2, y2, x1, y2),
    makeEdge(x1, y2, x1, y1),
  ];
}

/** signedOffset > 0 вЂ” РІРЅСѓС‚СЂСЊ (Рє С†РµРЅС‚СЂРѕРёРґСѓ), < 0 вЂ” РЅР°СЂСѓР¶Сѓ. */
export function buildTriangleEdges(w: number, h: number, signedOffset: number): Edge[] {
  const cx = w / 2, cy = h / 2;
  const verts: [number, number][] = [[cx, 0], [w, h], [0, h]];
  const shrunk = verts.map(([x, y]) => {
    const dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const k = (dist - signedOffset) / dist;
    return [cx + dx * k, cy + dy * k] as [number, number];
  });
  return [
    makeEdge(shrunk[0][0], shrunk[0][1], shrunk[1][0], shrunk[1][1]),
    makeEdge(shrunk[1][0], shrunk[1][1], shrunk[2][0], shrunk[2][1]),
    makeEdge(shrunk[2][0], shrunk[2][1], shrunk[0][0], shrunk[0][1]),
  ];
}

export function buildPerimeterEdges(s: StampShapeItem, signedOffset: number): Edge[] {
  if (s.kind === 'rect') return buildRectEdges(s.width, s.height, signedOffset);
  if (s.kind === 'triangle') return buildTriangleEdges(s.width, s.height, signedOffset);
  return [];
}

FILE: src\lib\stamps\geometry\findShape.ts

import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { buildTriangleEdges } from './edges';
import { isPointInTriangle, pointToSegmentDistance } from './math';

export function shapeContainsPoint(s: StampShapeItem, px: number, py: number): boolean {
  if (px < s.x || px > s.x + s.width) return false;
  if (py < s.y || py > s.y + s.height) return false;
  if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
    const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
    const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
    const rx = s.width / 2;
    const ry = s.height / 2;
    const dx = (px - cx) / Math.max(1e-6, rx);
    const dy = (py - cy) / Math.max(1e-6, ry);
    return dx * dx + dy * dy <= 1;
  }
  if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
    return isPointInTriangle(
      px - s.x, py - s.y,
      [s.width / 2, 0], [s.width, s.height], [0, s.height],
    );
  }
  return true;
}

export function distanceToPerimeter(s: StampShapeItem, px: number, py: number): number {
  const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
  const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
  const rx = s.width / 2;
  const ry = s.height / 2;
  const dx = px - cx;
  const dy = py - cy;

  if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') {
    const angle = Math.atan2(dy / Math.max(1e-6, ry), dx / Math.max(1e-6, rx));
    const ex = cx + rx * Math.cos(angle);
    const ey = cy + ry * Math.sin(angle);
    return Math.hypot(px - ex, py - ey);
  }
  if (s.kind === 'triangle') {
    const edges = buildTriangleEdges(s.width, s.height, 0);
    let best = Infinity;
    for (const e of edges) {
      const d = pointToSegmentDistance(px - s.x, py - s.y, e.x1, e.y1, e.x2, e.y2).distance;
      if (d < best) best = d;
    }
    return best;
  }
  return Math.min(
    Math.abs(px - s.x), Math.abs(px - (s.x + s.width)),
    Math.abs(py - s.y), Math.abs(py - (s.y + s.height)),
  );
}

export interface FindShapeResult {
  host: StampShapeItem;
  angleRad: number;
  isOutside: boolean;
}

export function findSnapTarget(
  px: number, py: number,
  excludeId: string,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  maxDistance?: number,
): FindShapeResult | null {
  let best: StampShapeItem | null = null;
  let bestDist = Infinity;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === excludeId || s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') continue;
    if (s.width < 30 || s.height < 30) continue;

    const cx = s.x + s.width / 2;
    const cy = s.y + s.height / 2;
    const d = Math.hypot(px - cx, py - cy);
    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = s; }
  }

  if (!best) return null;

  const cx = best.x + best.width / 2;
  const cy = best.y + best.height / 2;
  const rHost = Math.min(best.width, best.height) / 2;
  if (maxDistance !== undefined && bestDist > rHost + maxDistance) return null;

  return {
    host: best,
    angleRad: Math.atan2(py - cy, px - cx),
    isOutside: !shapeContainsPoint(best, px, py),
  };
}

export function findNearestShapeByCursor(
  px: number, py: number,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
  maxDistance = 260,
): FindShapeResult | null {
  let best: StampShapeItem | null = null;
  let bestDist = Infinity;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.hidden || s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') continue;
    if (s.width < 20 || s.height < 20) continue;

    const d = distanceToPerimeter(s, px, py);
    if (d > maxDistance) continue;
    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = s; }
  }

  if (!best) return null;
  const cx = best.x + best.width / 2;
  const cy = best.y + best.height / 2;
  return {
    host: best,
    angleRad: Math.atan2(py - cy, px - cx),
    isOutside: !shapeContainsPoint(best, px, py),
  };
}

export function findHostShape(
  px: number, py: number,
  excludeId: string, shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem | null {
  const t = findSnapTarget(px, py, excludeId, shapes);
  return t ? t.host : null;
}

/** РЎР°РјР°СЏ РјР°Р»РµРЅСЊРєР°СЏ РїРѕ РїР»РѕС‰Р°РґРё С„РёРіСѓСЂР°, С‡РµР№ bbox СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ. */
export function findContainingShape(
  cx: number, cy: number,
  excludeId: string,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem | null {
  let best: StampShapeItem | null = null;
  let bestArea = Infinity;

  for (const s of shapes) {
    if (s.id === excludeId || s.hidden) continue;
    if (s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') continue;
    if (cx < s.x || cx > s.x + s.width) continue;
    if (cy < s.y || cy > s.y + s.height) continue;

    const area = Math.max(1, s.width * s.height);
    if (area < bestArea) { bestArea = area; best = s; }
  }
  return best;
}

/**
 * Р’СЃРµ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ (РЅРµ line/fill), СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶Р°С‰РёРµ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ P.
 * РџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє вЂ” РєР°Рє РІ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІРµ shapes (РѕРЅ Р¶Рµ z-order).
 */
export function findShapesContaining(
  px: number, py: number,
  shapes: StampShapeItem[],
): StampShapeItem[] {
  return shapes.filter((s) =>
    s.kind !== 'line' && s.kind !== 'fill'
    && !s.hidden
    && shapeContainsPoint(s, px, py),
  );
}

export function clamp(v: number, min: number, max: number): number {
  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v));
}

export function isPointInTriangle(
  px: number, py: number,
  a: [number, number], b: [number, number], c: [number, number],
): boolean {
  const sign = (p1: [number, number], p2: [number, number], p3: [number, number]) =>
    (p1[0] - p3[0]) * (p2[1] - p3[1]) - (p2[0] - p3[0]) * (p1[1] - p3[1]);
  const d1 = sign([px, py], a, b);
  const d2 = sign([px, py], b, c);
  const d3 = sign([px, py], c, a);
  const hasNeg = d1 < 0 || d2 < 0 || d3 < 0;
  const hasPos = d1 > 0 || d2 > 0 || d3 > 0;
  return !(hasNeg && hasPos);
}

export interface SegmentDistanceResult {
  distance: number;
  t: number;
  qx: number;
  qy: number;
}

export function pointToSegmentDistance(
  px: number, py: number,
  ax: number, ay: number, bx: number, by: number,
): SegmentDistanceResult {
  const ex = bx - ax;
  const ey = by - ay;
  const lenSq = ex * ex + ey * ey || 1;
  const t = clamp(((px - ax) * ex + (py - ay) * ey) / lenSq, 0, 1);
  const qx = ax + t * ex;
  const qy = ay + t * ey;
  return { distance: Math.hypot(px - qx, py - qy), t, qx, qy };
}
