import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Circle, Ellipse, Line, Text as KonvaText, Shape as KonvaShape, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampElement, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { drawArcText, canvasFont, konvaStyle, STAMP_DASH_MAP } from '../../../lib/stamps/render';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from '../stamps/CompositeFillLayer';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: StampElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<StampElement>) => void;
}

export const StampEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate: _onUpdate, onDblClick, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isSelected,
}) => {
  const shapes = el.shapes ?? [];
  const texts = el.texts ?? [];
  const images = el.images ?? [];

  const regularShapes = useMemo(() => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill'), [shapes]);
  const legacyFills = useMemo(() => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === 'fill' && !s.fillTargetShapeId), [shapes]);

  // === LEGACY STAMP ===
  if (shapes.length === 0 && el.shape) {
    const {
      shape, stampColor, strokeWidth, strokeDash, showInner, innerStrokeWidth, innerStrokeDash, topText, centerText, bottomText,
      topFontSize, topFontFamily, topFontStyle, topTextDecoration, topColor,
      centerFontSize, centerFontFamily, centerFontStyle, centerTextDecoration, centerColor,
      bottomFontSize, bottomFontFamily, bottomFontStyle, bottomTextDecoration, bottomColor,
    } = el;
    const w = el.width, h = el.height;
    const pad = 8;

    return (
      <Group
        id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
        onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
        onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
        onDragStart={onDragStart}
        onDragMove={onDragMove}
        onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
        onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
      >
        <Rect width={w} height={h} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />

        {/* Р’РЅРµС€РЅРёР№ РєРѕРЅС‚СѓСЂ */}
        {shape === 'circle' && <Circle x={w/2} y={h/2} radius={Math.min(w,h)/2} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {shape === 'oval' && <Ellipse x={w/2} y={h/2} radiusX={w/2} radiusY={h/2} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {(shape === 'rect') && <Rect width={w} height={h} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" cornerRadius={12} />}
        {(shape === 'triangle') && <Line points={[w/2, 0, w, h, 0, h]} closed stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={strokeWidth} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[strokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}

        {/* Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ РєРѕРЅС‚СѓСЂ */}
        {showInner && shape === 'circle' && <Circle x={w/2} y={h/2} radius={Math.min(w,h)/2 - pad} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {showInner && shape === 'oval' && <Ellipse x={w/2} y={h/2} radiusX={w/2 - pad} radiusY={h/2 - pad} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}
        {showInner && shape === 'rect' && <Rect x={pad} y={pad} width={w - pad*2} height={h - pad*2} stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" cornerRadius={6} />}
        {showInner && shape === 'triangle' && <Line points={[w/2, pad, w - pad, h - pad, pad, h - pad]} closed stroke={stampColor} strokeWidth={innerStrokeWidth || 1} dash={STAMP_DASH_MAP[innerStrokeDash || 'solid']} fill="transparent" />}

        {/* РўРµРєСЃС‚ РїРѕ РґСѓРіРµ */}
        {(shape === 'circle' || shape === 'oval') && topText && (
          <KonvaShape sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
            drawArcText((ctx as any)._context, { text: topText, cx: w/2, cy: h/2, radius: Math.min(w,h)/2 - 15, startDeg: -150, endDeg: -30, flip: false, font: canvasFont(topFontFamily||'Arial', topFontStyle||'bold', topFontSize||14), fill: topColor||stampColor||'#000' });
          }} listening={false} />
        )}
        {(shape === 'circle' || shape === 'oval') && bottomText && (
          <KonvaShape sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
            drawArcText((ctx as any)._context, { text: bottomText, cx: w/2, cy: h/2, radius: Math.min(w,h)/2 - 15, startDeg: 150, endDeg: 30, flip: true, font: canvasFont(bottomFontFamily||'Arial', bottomFontStyle||'normal', bottomFontSize||12), fill: bottomColor||stampColor||'#000' });
          }} listening={false} />
        )}

        {/* РџСЂСЏРјРѕР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚ */}
        {shape === 'rect' && topText && <KonvaText x={0} y={10} width={w} text={topText} fontSize={topFontSize||14} fontFamily={topFontFamily||'Arial'} fontStyle={topFontStyle||'bold'} textDecoration={topTextDecoration||''} fill={topColor||stampColor||'#000'} align="center" />}
        {shape === 'rect' && bottomText && <KonvaText x={0} y={h - (bottomFontSize||12) - 10} width={w} text={bottomText} fontSize={bottomFontSize||12} fontFamily={bottomFontFamily||'Arial'} fontStyle={bottomFontStyle||'normal'} textDecoration={bottomTextDecoration||''} fill={bottomColor||stampColor||'#000'} align="center" />}
        {centerText && <KonvaText x={0} y={h/2 - (centerFontSize||20)/2} width={w} text={centerText} fontSize={centerFontSize||20} fontFamily={centerFontFamily||'Arial'} fontStyle={centerFontStyle||'bold'} textDecoration={centerTextDecoration||''} fill={centerColor||stampColor||'#000'} align="center" />}

        {isSelected && <Rect width={w} height={h} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />}
      </Group>
    );
  }

  // === NEW STAMP ===
  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation} draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDblClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDblTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick?.(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart}
      onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Rect width={Math.max(1, el.width)} height={Math.max(1, el.height)} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening />
      <CompositeFillLayer width={el.width} height={el.height} shapes={shapes} />
      {legacyFills.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : <LegacyFillShape key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} />)}
      {regularShapes.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : <ShapeNode key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} />)}
      {images.map((im) => im.hidden ? null : <ImageNode key={im.id} im={im} />)}
      {texts.map((t) => t.hidden ? null : <TextNode key={t.id} t={t} />)}
      {isSelected && <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} stroke="#6366f1" strokeWidth={1.5} dash={[6, 4]} listening={false} />}
    </Group>
  );
};

/* ---------- Р’СЃРїРѕРјРѕРіР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚С‹ ---------- */

/**
 * allShapes РїСЂРёСЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ РІ props РґР»СЏ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё СЃ LegacyFillShape-РїРѕРґРѕР±РЅС‹Рј
 * API, РЅРѕ РґР»СЏ СЂРµРЅРґРµСЂР° РѕРґРЅРѕР№ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ РЅРµ РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ. РџСЂРµС„РёРєСЃ `_` вЂ” РјР°СЂРєРµСЂ РЅР°РјРµСЂРµРЅРЅРѕ
 * РЅРµРёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРјРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂР°.
 */
const ShapeNode: React.FC<{ shape: StampShapeItem; allShapes: StampShapeItem[] }> = ({ shape, allShapes: _allShapes }) => {
  const cx = shape.x + shape.width / 2;
  const cy = shape.y + shape.height / 2;
  const common = { stroke: shape.stroke, strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth, dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[shape.dash], fill: shape.fill || 'transparent', listening: false };
  if (shape.kind === 'rect') return <Rect x={shape.x} y={shape.y} width={shape.width} height={shape.height} cornerRadius={shape.cornerRadius || 0} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'circle') return <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={Math.min(shape.width, shape.height)/2} radiusY={Math.min(shape.width, shape.height)/2} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'ellipse') return <Ellipse x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={shape.width/2} radiusY={shape.height/2} {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'triangle') return <Line points={[cx, shape.y, shape.x + shape.width, shape.y + shape.height, shape.x, shape.y + shape.height]} closed lineJoin="round" {...common} />;
  if (shape.kind === 'line') {
    const pts = shape.points && shape.points.length >= 4 ? shape.points.slice() : [0, shape.height / 2, shape.width, shape.height / 2];
    return <Line x={shape.x} y={shape.y} points={pts} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" {...common} />;
  }
  return null;
};

const LegacyFillShape: React.FC<{ shape: StampShapeItem; allShapes: StampShapeItem[] }> = ({ shape, allShapes }) => {
  const outer = shape.fillOuterId ? allShapes.find((x) => x.id === shape.fillOuterId) : null;
  if (!outer) return null;
  const holes = (shape.fillHoleIds || []).map((id) => allShapes.find((x) => x.id === id)).filter((x): x is StampShapeItem => !!x && !x.hidden);
  const mode = shape.fillMode || 'subtract';
  return (
    <KonvaShape listening={false} sceneFunc={(c) => {
      const raw = (c as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
      const all = [outer, ...holes];
      const minX = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.x));
      const minY = Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.y));
      const maxX = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.x + p.width));
      const maxY = Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.y + p.height));
      raw.save();
      if (mode === 'intersect' && holes.length > 0) {
        const op = (s: StampShapeItem) => {
          const path = new Path2D();
          const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
          if (s.kind === 'rect') path.rect(x, y, w, h);
          else if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') path.ellipse(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, w / 2, h / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          else if (s.kind === 'triangle') { path.moveTo(x + w / 2, y); path.lineTo(x + w, y + h); path.lineTo(x, y + h); path.closePath(); }
          return path;
        };
        raw.clip(op(outer));
        for (const h of holes) raw.clip(op(h));
      } else {
        const combined = new Path2D();
        const addPath = (s: StampShapeItem) => {
          const { x, y, width: w, height: h } = s;
          if (s.kind === 'rect') combined.rect(x, y, w, h);
          else if (s.kind === 'circle' || s.kind === 'ellipse') combined.ellipse(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, w / 2, h / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          else if (s.kind === 'triangle') { combined.moveTo(x + w / 2, y); combined.lineTo(x + w, y + h); combined.lineTo(x, y + h); combined.closePath(); }
        };
        addPath(outer);
        for (const h of holes) addPath(h);
        raw.clip(combined, 'evenodd');
      }
      raw.fillStyle = shape.fill || '#bfdbfe';
      raw.fillRect(minX, minY, maxX - minX, maxY - minY);
      raw.restore();
    }} />
  );
};

const ImageNode: React.FC<{ im: StampImageItem }> = ({ im }) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!im.src) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
    i.src = im.src;
  }, [im.src]);
  if (!img) return null;
  return <KImage x={im.x} y={im.y} width={im.width} height={im.height} image={img} listening={false} />;
};

/**
 * props.shapes СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РїРµСЂРµРґР°РІР°Р»СЃСЏ РґР»СЏ РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅРѕРіРѕ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° host-С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹ (arc-text
 * С…РѕС‚РµР» СЃРјРѕС‚СЂРµС‚СЊ РЅР° СЃРѕСЃРµРґРЅРёРµ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹). РЎРµР№С‡Р°СЃ РЅРµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ, РЅРѕ РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»РµРЅ РІ
 * С‚РёРїРµ РґР»СЏ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕР№ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё СЃ РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°СЋС‰РёРј РєРѕРґРѕРј.
 */
const TextNode: React.FC<{ t: StampTextItem }> = ({ t }) => {
  if (t.mode === 'straight') {
    return <KonvaText x={t.x} y={t.y} width={t.width} text={t.text} fontSize={t.fontSize} fontFamily={t.fontFamily} fontStyle={konvaStyle(t.fontStyle || 'normal')} textDecoration={t.textDecoration} fill={t.fill} align={t.align} listening={false} />;
  }
  const rx = t.arcRx ?? t.arcRadius;
  const ry = t.arcRy ?? t.arcRadius;
  return (
    <KonvaShape listening={false} sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
      const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
      drawArcText(c, { text: t.text, cx: t.arcCx, cy: t.arcCy, radius: t.arcRadius, radiusX: rx, radiusY: ry, startDeg: t.arcStartDeg, endDeg: t.arcEndDeg, flip: t.arcFlip, font: canvasFont(t.fontFamily, t.fontStyle, t.fontSize), fill: t.fill });
    }} />
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text } from 'react-konva';
import type { TableElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: TableElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TableElement>) => void;
}

export const TableEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate: _onUpdate, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu,
}) => {
  const cellW = el.width / el.cols;
  const cellH = el.height / el.rows;
  const cells: React.ReactNode[] = [];
  const texts: React.ReactNode[] = [];

  for (let r = 0; r < el.rows; r++) {
    for (let c = 0; c < el.cols; c++) {
      const bg = r === 0 ? el.headerBg : '#fff';
      cells.push(
        <Rect key={`r-${r}-${c}`} x={c * cellW} y={r * cellH} width={cellW} height={cellH} fill={bg} stroke={el.borderColor} strokeWidth={1} />
      );
      const cellText = el.data[r]?.[c] ?? '';
      const isPh = /\{\{[^}]+\}\}/.test(cellText);
      texts.push(
        <Text key={`t-${r}-${c}`} x={c * cellW + 6} y={r * cellH + 6} width={Math.max(1, cellW - 12)} height={Math.max(1, cellH - 12)} text={cellText} fontSize={el.fontSize} fill={isPh ? '#2563eb' : '#111'} fontStyle={isPh ? 'italic' : 'normal'} wrap="word" ellipsis={true} listening={false} />
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" />
      {cells}
      {texts}
    </Group>
  );
};

/**
 * @deprecated РРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓР№С‚Рµ РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ РёР· '@/lib/richText/tableLayout'.
 * Р¤Р°Р№Р» РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»РµРЅ РґР»СЏ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕР№ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РёС… РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚РѕРІ

FILE: src\presentation\components\elements\TextEl.tsx

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Text as KonvaText } from 'react-konva';
import type { TextElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import type { CommonProps } from './types';
import { parseHtmlToBlocks, layoutRuns, plainToHtml, type PositionedRun, type RichTableData } from '../../../lib/richText';
import { RichTable, measureTableHeight } from './RichTable';
import { TEXT_PLACEHOLDER } from '../../../shared/constants';

const BLOCK_MARGIN = 0;

interface Props extends CommonProps {
  el: TextElement;
  onUpdate: (p: Partial<TextElement>) => void;
  isEditing?: boolean;
}

type PreparedBlock = { kind: 'text'; layout: any; height: number } | { kind: 'table'; data: RichTableData; height: number };

export const TextEl: React.FC<Props> = ({
  el, onSelect, onUpdate: _onUpdate, onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd, onContextMenu, isEditing,
}) => {
  const padding = el.padding || { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 };
  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, el.width - padding.left - padding.right);
  const innerHeight = Math.max(1, el.height - padding.top - padding.bottom);

  const isPlaceholder = useMemo(
    () => !el.html && (el.text === TEXT_PLACEHOLDER || !el.text?.trim()),
    [el.html, el.text],
  );

  const blocks = useMemo(() => {
    const baseFontStyle = el.fontStyle || 'normal';
    const resolvedFontStyle = isPlaceholder ? `${baseFontStyle} italic` : baseFontStyle;
    const resolvedFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : (el.fill || '#000000');
    const sourceHtml = el.html && el.html.trim() ? el.html : plainToHtml(el.text || '');
    return parseHtmlToBlocks(sourceHtml, {
      fontSize: el.fontSize,
      fontFamily: el.fontFamily,
      fontStyle: resolvedFontStyle,
      textDecoration: el.textDecoration || '',
      fill: resolvedFill,
    });
  }, [el.html, el.text, el.fontSize, el.fontFamily, el.fontStyle, el.textDecoration, el.fill, isPlaceholder]);

  const lineHeightCoef = el.lineHeight || 1.5;

  const prepared = useMemo(() => {
    const items: PreparedBlock[] = [];
    let totalHeight = 0;
    for (const block of blocks) {
      if (block.kind === 'text') {
        const layout = layoutRuns(block.runs, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef);
        if (layout.lines.length === 0) continue;
        items.push({ kind: 'text', layout, height: layout.totalHeight });
        totalHeight += layout.totalHeight;
      } else {
        const h = measureTableHeight(block.data, innerWidth, el.fontSize, lineHeightCoef);
        items.push({ kind: 'table', data: block.data, height: h });
        totalHeight += h;
      }
    }
    if (items.length > 1 && BLOCK_MARGIN > 0) totalHeight += (items.length - 1) * BLOCK_MARGIN;
    return { items, totalHeight };
  }, [blocks, innerWidth, lineHeightCoef, el.fontSize]);

  let offsetY = padding.top;
  if (el.verticalAlign === 'middle') offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, (innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight) / 2);
  else if (el.verticalAlign === 'bottom') offsetY = padding.top + Math.max(0, innerHeight - prepared.totalHeight);

  const effectiveFill = isPlaceholder ? '#9ca3af' : (el.fill || '#000000');
  const bg = el.background && el.background !== 'transparent' ? el.background : 'transparent';
  const hitFill = bg === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.001)' : bg;
  let cursorY = offsetY;

  return (
    <Group
      id={el.id} name="element" x={el.x} y={el.y} rotation={el.rotation}
      draggable
      onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(e); }}
      onDragStart={onDragStart} onDragMove={onDragMove}
      onDragEnd={(e) => { onDragEnd?.(e); }}
      onContextMenu={onContextMenu}
    >
      <Rect width={el.width} height={el.height} fill={hitFill} listening={true} />
      {!isEditing && prepared.items.map((item, bi) => {
        const blockTop = cursorY;
        cursorY += item.height;
        if (bi < prepared.items.length - 1) cursorY += BLOCK_MARGIN;

        if (item.kind === 'text') {
          return (
            <React.Fragment key={`b-${bi}`}>
              {item.layout.lines.map((line: PositionedRun[], i: number) => {
                const lw = line.reduce((acc: number, r: PositionedRun) => acc + r.width, 0);
                let lineOffsetX = 0;
                if (el.align === 'center') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, (innerWidth - lw) / 2);
                else if (el.align === 'right') lineOffsetX = Math.max(0, innerWidth - lw);
                return line.map((run, j) => {
                  const x = padding.left + lineOffsetX + run.x;
                  const y = blockTop + run.y;
                  const resolvedFontStyle = run.bold && run.italic ? 'italic bold' : run.bold ? 'bold' : run.italic ? 'italic' : isPlaceholder ? 'italic' : 'normal';
                  return (
                    <React.Fragment key={`${i}-${j}`}>
                      {run.bgColor && <Rect x={x} y={y} width={run.width} height={run.actualFontSize * 1.2} fill={run.bgColor} listening={false} />}
                      <KonvaText x={x} y={y} text={run.text} fontSize={run.actualFontSize} fontFamily={run.fontFamily || el.fontFamily} fontStyle={resolvedFontStyle} textDecoration={run.underline && run.strike ? 'underline line-through' : run.underline ? 'underline' : run.strike ? 'line-through' : ''} fill={run.color || effectiveFill} listening={false} />
                    </React.Fragment>
                  );
                });
              })}
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        }
        return <RichTable key={`table-${bi}`} data={item.data} x={padding.left} y={blockTop} width={innerWidth} fontFamily={el.fontFamily} fallbackFontSize={el.fontSize} fallbackFill={el.fill} lineHeightCoef={lineHeightCoef} />;
      })}
    </Group>
  );
};

export interface CommonProps {
  onSelect: (e?: any) => void;
  onUpdate: (p: any) => void;
  onDragStart?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragMove?: (e: any) => void;
  onDragEnd?: (e: any) => void;
  onContextMenu?: (e: any) => void;

/* ============================================================
   ErrorBoundary.tsx
   NFR-OBS-01: РџРµСЂРµС…РІР°С‚ РѕС€РёР±РѕРє РЅР° СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµ App Рё РїР°РЅРµР»РµР№.
   ============================================================ */

import React, { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
  fallback?: ReactNode;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  public override state: State = { hasError: false, error: null };

  public static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  public override componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo): void {
    // NFR-OBS-02: РІ prod вЂ” Sentry.captureException(error, { extra: errorInfo })
    console.error('[ErrorBoundary] Uncaught error:', error, errorInfo);
  }

  public override render(): ReactNode {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return this.props.fallback || (
        <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: '#dc2626', fontFamily: 'sans-serif' }}>
          <h2>Р§С‚Рѕ-С‚Рѕ РїРѕС€Р»Рѕ РЅРµ С‚Р°Рє</h2>
          <p style={{ color: '#64748b' }}>РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№С‚Рµ РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ.</p>
          <pre style={{ marginTop: 20, padding: 12, background: '#fef2f2', borderRadius: 6, textAlign: 'left', fontSize: 12, overflowX: 'auto' }}>
            {this.state.error?.message}
          </pre>

            onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
            style={{ marginTop: 20, padding: '8px 16px', background: '#6366f1', color: '#fff', border: 'none', borderRadius: 6, cursor: 'pointer' }}

            РћР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ
          </button>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';

interface FloatingPropsProps {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  element: CanvasElement;
  onUpdate: (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onBringForward: () => void;
  onSendBackward: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

const normalizeColor = (c: string | undefined | null): string => {
  if (!c || c === 'transparent') return '#ffffff';
  if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(c)) {
    return '#' + c[1] + c[1] + c[2] + c[2] + c[3] + c[3];
  }
  return c;
};

export const FloatingProps: React.FC<FloatingPropsProps> = ({
  x, y, element, onUpdate, onClose, onBringForward, onSendBackward, onDelete,
}) => {
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState({ x, y });

  useEffect(() => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    if (!panel) return;
    const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
    let newX = x, newY = y - 50;
    if (newX + rect.width > window.innerWidth) newX = window.innerWidth - rect.width - 10;
    if (newX < 10) newX = 10;
    if (newY < 10) newY = y + element.height + 10;
    if (newY + rect.height > window.innerHeight) newY = window.innerHeight - rect.height - 10;
    setPosition({ x: newX, y: newY });
  }, [x, y, element.height]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const down = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (panelRef.current && !panelRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    };
    setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener('mousedown', down), 0);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', down);
  }, [onClose]);

  return (
    <div ref={panelRef} className="floating-props" style={{ left: position.x, top: position.y }}>
      <div className="fp-group">
        <button onClick={onBringForward} title="Р’С‹С€Рµ">в¬†</button>
        <button onClick={onSendBackward} title="РќРёР¶Рµ">в¬‡</button>
        <button onClick={onDelete} title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ">рџ—‘</button>
      </div>
      {element.type === 'shape' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</label>
            <input type="color" value={normalizeColor(element.fill)} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })} />
            <button onClick={() => onUpdate({ fill: 'transparent' })}>в€…</button>
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>РћР±РІРѕРґРєР°</label>
            <input type="color" value={normalizeColor(element.stroke)} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ stroke: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
      {element.type === 'text' && (
        <>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Р¦РІРµС‚</label>
            <input type="color" value={normalizeColor(element.fill)} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fill: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="fp-group">
            <label>Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ</label>
            <input type="number" value={element.fontSize} onChange={(e) => onUpdate({ fontSize: Number(e.target.value) })} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

/**
 * Grid Layer Component - Presentation Layer
 * Renders canvas grid
 */

import React from 'react';
import { Shape } from 'react-konva';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../../shared/constants';

interface GridLayerProps {
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export const GridLayer: React.FC<GridLayerProps> = ({ width, height }) => {
  return (
    <Shape
      listening={false}
      sceneFunc={(ctx, shape) => {
        const context = ctx._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        context.beginPath();

        // Vertical lines
        for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(x, 0);
          context.lineTo(x, height);
        }

        // Horizontal lines
        for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE) {
          context.moveTo(0, y);
          context.lineTo(width, y);
        }

        context.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
        context.lineWidth = 1;
        context.stroke();

        ctx.strokeShape(shape);
      }}
    />
  );
};

GridLayer.displayName = 'GridLayer';

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react';

interface InlineEditorProps {
  x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number;
  value: string; html?: string;
  fontSize: number; fontFamily: string; fontStyle: string; textDecoration: string;
  fill: string; align: 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify';
  verticalAlign: 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom';
  lineHeight: number; padding: { top: number; right: number; bottom: number; left: number };
  background: string; isOpen: boolean;
  onChange: (value: { value?: string; html?: string }) => void;
  onCommit: () => void; onCancel: () => void;
}

export const InlineEditor: React.FC<InlineEditorProps> = ({
  x, y, width, height, value, html,
  fontSize, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration,
  fill, align, verticalAlign, lineHeight, padding, background,
  isOpen, onChange, onCommit, onCancel,
}) => {
  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [isComposing, setIsComposing] = useState(false);
  const committedRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !editorRef.current) return;
    committedRef.current = false;
    const editor = editorRef.current;
    editor.innerHTML = html || value || '<p><br></p>';
    editor.focus();

    const range = document.createRange();
    range.selectNodeContents(editor);
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    if (selection) {
      selection.removeAllRanges();
      selection.addRange(range);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  const handleInput = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editorRef.current) return;
    onChange({ value: editorRef.current.innerText, html: editorRef.current.innerHTML });
  }, [onChange]);

  const handleCommit = useCallback(() => {
    if (committedRef.current) return;
    committedRef.current = true;
    onCommit();
  }, [onCommit]);

  const handleKeyDown = useCallback((e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (isComposing) return;
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); handleCommit(); }
    else if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); onCancel(); }
  }, [isComposing, handleCommit, onCancel]);

  const handleBlur = useCallback(() => setTimeout(() => handleCommit(), 100), [handleCommit]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="inline-editor-overlay" style={{ position: 'fixed', left: x, top: y, width, height, zIndex: 1000 }}>
      <div
        ref={editorRef}
        className="inline-editor"
        contentEditable
        suppressContentEditableWarning
        onInput={handleInput}
        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
        onBlur={handleBlur}
        onCompositionStart={() => setIsComposing(true)}
        onCompositionEnd={() => setIsComposing(false)}
        style={{
          width: '100%', height: '100%', padding: `${padding.top}px ${padding.right}px ${padding.bottom}px ${padding.left}px`,
          fontSize: `${fontSize}px`, fontFamily, fontStyle, textDecoration, color: fill,
          backgroundColor: background === 'transparent' ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)' : background,
          textAlign: align, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column',
          justifyContent: verticalAlign === 'middle' ? 'center' : verticalAlign === 'bottom' ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start',
          lineHeight, outline: 'none', border: '2px solid #3b82f6', borderRadius: '4px',
          boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)', overflow: 'auto', wordWrap: 'break-word', whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\InlineEditorHost.tsx
SIZE: 1937 bytes

import React, { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { RichTextEditor } from './RichTextEditor';
import { InlineEditor } from './InlineEditor';
import type { EditingState } from '../stores/ModalsStore';

interface Props {
  editing: EditingState;
  onClose: () => void;
  onCommit: (html: string | undefined, text: string) => void;
}

export const InlineEditorHost: React.FC<Props> = ({ editing, onClose, onCommit }) => {
  const valueRef = useRef(editing.value || '');

  const handlePlainCommit = useCallback(() => {
    onCommit(undefined, valueRef.current);
  }, [onCommit]);

  if (editing.isRich) {
    return (
      <RichTextEditor
        isOpen
        initialHtml={editing.html ?? `<p>${escapeHtml(editing.value ?? '')}</p>`}
        onClose={onClose}
        onSave={(html) => {
          const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
          let text = doc.body.textContent ?? '';
          text = text.replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ');
          onCommit(html, text);
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <InlineEditor
      isOpen
      x={editing.x} y={editing.y}
      width={editing.width} height={editing.height}
      value={editing.value}
      html={editing.html}
      fontSize={editing.fontSize} fontFamily={editing.fontFamily}
      fontStyle={editing.fontStyle} textDecoration={editing.textDecoration}
      fill={editing.fill} align={editing.align} verticalAlign={editing.verticalAlign}
      lineHeight={editing.lineHeight} padding={editing.padding} background={editing.background}
      onChange={(val) => {
        if (val.value !== undefined) valueRef.current = val.value;
      }}
      onCommit={handlePlainCommit}
      onCancel={onClose}
    />
  );
};

function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) =>
    ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]!));
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';

const Section: React.FC<{
  title: string;
  icon?: string;
  defaultOpen?: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}> = ({ title, icon, defaultOpen = false, children }) => {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen);
  return (
    <div className={`tool-section ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="section-head"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
      >
        <span className="section-ico">{icon}</span>
        <span className="section-title">{title}</span>
        <span className="section-chev">{open ? 'в–ѕ' : 'в–ё'}</span>
      </button>
      {open && <div className="section-body">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );

const PaddingEditor: React.FC<{
  value: Padding;
  onChange: (p: Padding) => void;
}> = ({ value, onChange }) => {
  const keys: Array<{ k: keyof Padding; label: string }> = [
    { k: 'top', label: 'Р’РµСЂС…' },
    { k: 'bottom', label: 'РќРёР·' },
    { k: 'left', label: 'РЎР»РµРІР°' },
    { k: 'right', label: 'РЎРїСЂР°РІР°' },

      {keys.map(({ k, label }) => (
        <label key={k} className="field-label">
          {label}
          <input
            type="number"
            min={0}
            value={value[k]}
            onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, [k]: +e.target.value })}
            className="field"
          />
        </label>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

type ServerExportKind = null | 'svg' | 'pdfa';

export const LeftPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();
  const fileIO = useFileIO();

  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<ServerExportKind>(null);

  const activeSlide = useStore(docStore, (s) =>
    s.slides.find((x) => x.id === s.activeId),
  );
  const fillMode = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillMode);
  const fillColor = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.fillColor);
  const showGrid = useStore(modalsStore, (s) => s.showGrid);

  const canUndo = docStore.canUndo;
  const canRedo = docStore.canRedo;

  if (!activeSlide) return null;

  /* ---------- Р±Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Рµ ---------- */

  const add = (el: CanvasElement) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.elements.push(el);
    }, 'add-element');
    selStore.select(el.id);
  };

  const resetAfterLoad = () => {
    selStore.clear();
    modalsStore.closeEditor();
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
  };

  /* ---------- server export ---------- */

  const handleSvgServer = async () => {
    setBusy('svg');
    try {
      await fileIO.exportSvgServer();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(`SVG (server) РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»СЃСЏ: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const handlePdfaServer = async () => {
    setBusy('pdfa');
    try {
      await fileIO.exportPdfaServer();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(`PDF/A (server) РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»СЃСЏ: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  /* ---------- С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ ---------- */

  const handleFormat = (format: DocFormat) => {
    if (format === activeSlide.format) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const i = draft.slides.findIndex((s) => s.id === draft.activeId);
      if (i < 0) return;
      draft.slides[i] = changeFormat(draft.slides[i], format);
    }, 'change-format');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const handleOrientation = (orientation: Orientation) => {
    if (orientation === activeSlide.orientation) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const i = draft.slides.findIndex((s) => s.id === draft.activeId);
      if (i < 0) return;
      draft.slides[i] = changeOrientation(draft.slides[i], orientation);
    }, 'change-orientation');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const updatePadding = (padding: Padding) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.padding = padding;
    }, 'padding');
  };

  const changeCustomSize = (w: number, h: number) => {
    if (activeSlide.format !== 'custom') return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      const newW = s.orientation === 'portrait' ? w : h;
      const newH = s.orientation === 'portrait' ? h : w;
      s.width = newW;
      s.height = newH;
    }, 'custom-size');
  };

  /* ---------- С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅС‹ ---------- */

  const applyLayout = (layoutId: string) => {
    const layout = TYPICAL_LAYOUTS.find((l) => l.id === layoutId);
    if (!layout) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const slide = layout.build();
      draft.slides = [slide];
      draft.activeId = slide.id;
    }, 'apply-layout');
    resetAfterLoad();
  };

  const applyBundle = (bundleId: string) => {
    const bundle = BUNDLES.find((b) => b.id === bundleId);
    if (!bundle) return;
    const { slide, data: bundleData } = bundle.build();
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      draft.slides = [slide];
      draft.activeId = slide.id;
      draft.data = bundleData;
    }, 'apply-bundle');
    resetAfterLoad();
  };

  /* ---------- drag ---------- */

  const startDragNew = (e: React.DragEvent, elementType: string) => {
    e.dataTransfer.setData(
      'application/json',
      JSON.stringify({ kind: 'new-element', elementType }),
    );
    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy';
  };

  return (
    <aside className="left-panel">
      {/* 1. Р¤РђР™Р› */}
      <Section title="Р¤Р°Р№Р»" icon="рџ“Ѓ" defaultOpen>
        <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={fileIO.importJson}>
          рџ“‚ РРјРїРѕСЂС‚ JSON
        </button>
        <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={fileIO.exportJson}>
          рџ’ѕ Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ JSON
        </button>
        <div style={{ height: 10 }} />
        <button
          type="button"
          className="tool-btn"
          onClick={handleSvgServer}
          disabled={busy !== null}
        >
          {busy === 'svg' ? 'вЏі Р“РµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёСЏ SVGвЂ¦' : 'рџ–ј SVG (server)'}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="tool-btn"
          onClick={handlePdfaServer}
          disabled={busy !== null}
        >
          {busy === 'pdfa' ? 'вЏі Р“РµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёСЏ PDF/AвЂ¦' : 'рџ“„ PDF/A (server)'}
        </button>
        <div style={{ height: 10 }} />
        <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={fileIO.exportSvgLocal}>
          рџ–ј SVG (Р»РѕРєР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ, fallback)
        </button>
      </Section>

      {/* 2. РњРђРљР•РўР« */}
      <Section title="РњР°РєРµС‚С‹" icon="рџЋЁ">
        {TYPICAL_LAYOUTS.map((l) => (
          <button
            key={l.id}
            type="button"
            className="tool-btn"
            title={l.description}
            onClick={() => applyLayout(l.id)}
          >
            {l.icon} {l.name}
          </button>
        ))}
      </Section>

      {/* 3. Р”РћРљРЈРњР•РќРўР« */}
      <Section title="Р”РѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚С‹" icon="рџ“љ">
        {BUNDLES.map((b) => (
          <button
            key={b.id}
            type="button"
            className="tool-btn"
            title={b.description}
            onClick={() => applyBundle(b.id)}
          >
            {b.icon} {b.name}
          </button>
        ))}
      </Section>

      {/* 4. Р¤РћР РњРђРў */}
      <Section title="Р¤РѕСЂРјР°С‚" icon="рџ“ђ" defaultOpen>
        <label className="field-label">Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ</label>
        <select
          className="field"
          value={activeSlide.format}
          onChange={(e) => handleFormat(e.target.value as DocFormat)}
        >
          {Object.entries(FORMATS).map(([k, v]) => (
            <option key={k} value={k}>{v.label}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <label className="field-label">РћСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёСЏ</label>
        <div className="segmented">
          <button
            type="button"
            className={activeSlide.orientation === 'portrait' ? 'active' : ''}
            onClick={() => handleOrientation('portrait')}
          >РљРЅРёР¶РЅР°СЏ</button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={activeSlide.orientation === 'landscape' ? 'active' : ''}
            onClick={() => handleOrientation('landscape')}
          >РђР»СЊР±РѕРјРЅР°СЏ</button>
        </div>

        {activeSlide.format === 'custom' && (
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6, marginTop: 8 }}>
            <label className="field-label">РЁРёСЂРёРЅР°
              <input
                className="field" type="number" min={50}
                value={activeSlide.width}
                onChange={(e) => changeCustomSize(+e.target.value, activeSlide.height)}
              />
            </label>
            <label className="field-label">Р’С‹СЃРѕС‚Р°
              <input
                className="field" type="number" min={50}
                value={activeSlide.height}
                onChange={(e) => changeCustomSize(activeSlide.width, +e.target.value)}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="hint-sm">
          {activeSlide.width} Г— {activeSlide.height} px
        </div>

        <h4 style={{ margin: '10px 0 4px', fontSize: 11, color: '#666', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>

import React from 'react';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { useSelectedElement } from '../hooks/useSelectedElement';
import { getElementStrategy } from './elements/registry';
import { Group, L, inputStyle } from './elements/properties/ui';

export const PropertiesPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const el = useSelectedElement();

  if (!el) {
    return <div style={{ padding: 20, color: '#94a3b8', textAlign: 'center' }}>Р’С‹РґРµР»РёС‚Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚</div>;
  }

  const update = (patch: Partial<CanvasElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const target = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === el.id);
      if (target) Object.assign(target, patch);
    }, `update-${el.id}`);
  };

  const strategy = getElementStrategy(el.type);
  const PropsComponent = strategy?.PropertiesPanel;

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 12, overflowY: 'auto', height: '100%' }}>
      {/* РћР±С‰РёРµ СЃРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° РґР»СЏ РІСЃРµС… СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ */}
      <Group title="РџРѕР»РѕР¶РµРЅРёРµ">
        <L label="X"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.x)} onChange={(e) => update({ x: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Y"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.y)} onChange={(e) => update({ y: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="РЁРёСЂРёРЅР°"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.width)} onChange={(e) => update({ width: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="Р’С‹СЃРѕС‚Р°"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.height)} onChange={(e) => update({ height: +e.target.value })} /></L>
        <L label="РџРѕРІРѕСЂРѕС‚"><input style={inputStyle} type="number" value={Math.round(el.rotation)} onChange={(e) => update({ rotation: +e.target.value })} /></L>
      </Group>

      {/* РЎРїРµС†РёС„РёС‡РЅС‹Рµ СЃРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Р° С‡РµСЂРµР· Strategy */}
      {PropsComponent && <PropsComponent el={el as any} update={update as any} />}
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

interface Props {
  title: string;
  colors: string[];
  onPick: (color: string) => void;
  onReset?: () => void;
  resetLabel?: string;
}

export const ColorPickerPopover: React.FC<Props> = ({
  title, colors, onPick, onReset, resetLabel = 'РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ',
}) => (
  <div className="rt-popover">
    <div className="rt-popover-title">{title}</div>
    <div className="rt-color-grid">
      {colors.map((c) => (
        <button
          key={c}
          type="button"
          className="rt-color-cell"
          style={{ background: c }}
          title={c}
          onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onPick(c); }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
    {onReset && (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="rt-popover-action"
        onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onReset(); }}
      >
        {resetLabel}
      </button>
    )}
  </div>
);

FILE: src\presentation\components\richText\extensions.ts
SIZE: 4683 bytes

import { Extension } from '@tiptap/core';
import TableCell from '@tiptap/extension-table-cell';
import TableHeader from '@tiptap/extension-table-header';

declare module '@tiptap/core' {
  interface Commands<ReturnType> {
    indent: {
      indent: () => ReturnType;
      outdent: () => ReturnType;
    };
  }
}

export const FontSize = Extension.create({
  name: 'fontSize',
  addOptions() {
    return { types: ['textStyle'] };
  },
  addGlobalAttributes() {
    return [{
      types: this.options.types,
      attributes: {
        fontSize: {
          default: null,
          parseHTML: (el) => el.style.fontSize?.replace(/['"]+/g, '') || null,
          renderHTML: (attrs) =>
            attrs.fontSize ? { style: `font-size: ${attrs.fontSize}` } : {},
        },
      },
    }];
  },
  addCommands() {
    return {
      setFontSize: (fontSize: string) => ({ chain }: any) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize }).run(),
      unsetFontSize: () => ({ chain }: any) =>
        chain().setMark('textStyle', { fontSize: null }).removeEmptyTextStyle().run(),
    } as any;
  },
});

const CELL_ATTRIBUTES = {
  bgColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.backgroundColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) => attrs.bgColor ? { style: `background-color: ${attrs.bgColor}` } : {},
  },
  borderColor: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderColor || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) => attrs.borderColor ? { style: `border-color: ${attrs.borderColor}` } : {},
  },
  borderWidth: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderWidth || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) => attrs.borderWidth ? { style: `border-width: ${attrs.borderWidth}` } : {},
  },
  borderStyle: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.borderStyle || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) => attrs.borderStyle ? { style: `border-style: ${attrs.borderStyle}` } : {},
  },
  cellPadding: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.padding || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) => attrs.cellPadding ? { style: `padding: ${attrs.cellPadding}` } : {},
  },
  textAlign: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.textAlign || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) => attrs.textAlign ? { style: `text-align: ${attrs.textAlign}` } : {},
  },
  verticalAlign: {
    default: null,
    parseHTML: (el: HTMLElement) => el.style.verticalAlign || null,
    renderHTML: (attrs: any) => attrs.verticalAlign ? { style: `vertical-align: ${attrs.verticalAlign}` } : {},
  },
};

export const CustomTableCell = TableCell.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

export const CustomTableHeader = TableHeader.extend({
  addAttributes() {
    return { ...this.parent?.(), ...CELL_ATTRIBUTES };
  },
});

export const Indent = Extension.create({
  name: 'indent',
  addOptions() {
    return { types: ['paragraph', 'heading'] };
  },
  addGlobalAttributes() {
    return [
      {
        types: this.options.types,
        attributes: {
          indent: {
            default: 0,
            parseHTML: (element) => parseInt(element.style.paddingLeft) / 20 || 0,
            renderHTML: (attributes) => {
              if (!attributes.indent) return {};
              return { style: `padding-left: ${attributes.indent * 20}px` };
            },
          },
        },
      },
    ];
  },
  addCommands() {
    return {
      indent: () => ({ tr, state, dispatch }: any) => {
        const { selection } = state;
        const { from, to } = selection;
        state.doc.nodesBetween(from, to, (node: any, pos: number) => {
          if (this.options.types.includes(node.type.name)) {
            const indent = (node.attrs.indent || 0) + 1;
            tr.setNodeMarkup(pos, undefined, { ...node.attrs, indent: Math.min(indent, 10) });
          }
        });
        if (dispatch) dispatch(tr);
        return true;
      },
      outdent: () => ({ tr, state, dispatch }: any) => {
        const { selection } = state;
        const { from, to } = selection;
        state.doc.nodesBetween(from, to, (node: any, pos: number) => {
          if (this.options.types.includes(node.type.name)) {
            const indent = (node.attrs.indent || 0) - 1;
            tr.setNodeMarkup(pos, undefined, { ...node.attrs, indent: Math.max(indent, 0) });
          }
        });
        if (dispatch) dispatch(tr);
        return true;
      },
    } as any;
  },
});

FILE: src\presentation\components\richText\TableOperationsToolbar.tsx

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Editor } from '@tiptap/react';
import { TableSizePicker } from './TableSizePicker';
import { CellFillPopover } from './TableCellPopovers';

export const TableOperationsToolbar: React.FC<{ editor: Editor }> = ({ editor }) => {
  const [pop, setPop] = useState<null | 'size' | 'fill'>(null);
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const toggle = (k: 'size' | 'fill') => setPop((v) => (v === k ? null : k));
  const insideTable = editor.isActive('table');

  /* Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РїР°Р»РёС‚СЂС‹ РїРѕ РєР»РёРєСѓ РІРЅРµ РµС‘ (F-TT-07) */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pop) return;
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setPop(null);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, [pop]);

  const setCellAttrs = (attrs: Record<string, any>) => {
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableCell', attrs).run();
    editor.chain().focus().updateAttributes('tableHeader', attrs).run();
  };

  const handleInsertTable = (r: number, c: number) => {
    editor.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows: r, cols: c, withHeaderRow: true }).run();

    const { doc } = editor.state;
    const lastNode = doc.lastChild;
    if (lastNode && lastNode.type.name === 'table') {
      editor.chain().focus().insertContentAt(doc.content.size, '<p></p>').run();
    }

    setPop(null);
  };

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} style={{ display: 'contents' }}>
      {/* Р’РЎРўРђР’РљРђ РўРђР‘Р›РР¦Р« */}
      <div className="rt-sep" />
      <div className="rt-popover-wrap">

import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface Props {
  onPick: (rows: number, cols: number) => void;
}

const MAX = 6;

export const TableSizePicker: React.FC<Props> = ({ onPick }) => {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState({ r: -1, c: -1 });

  return (
    <div className="rt-popover">
      <div className="rt-popover-title">Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†С‹</div>
      <div className="rt-table-grid">
        {Array.from({ length: MAX }, (_, r) =>
          Array.from({ length: MAX }, (_, c) => {
            const active = r <= hover.r && c <= hover.c && hover.r >= 0 && hover.c >= 0;
            return (
              <button
                key={`${r}-${c}`}
                type="button"
                className={`rt-table-cell ${active ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHover({ r, c })}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHover({ r: -1, c: -1 })}
                onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onPick(r + 1, c + 1); }}
              />
            );
          })
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="rt-popover-hint">
        {hover.r >= 0 ? `${hover.r + 1} Г— ${hover.c + 1}` : 'РќР°РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂ'}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

interface Props {
  isOpen: boolean;
  initialHtml: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (html: string) => void;
}

type PopKind = null | 'color' | 'bg';

export const RichTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({ isOpen, initialHtml, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const [pop, setPop] = useState<PopKind>(null);
  const toolbarRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const isPlaceholder = initialHtml.includes('Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ С‚РµРєСЃС‚') || !initialHtml.trim();
  let cleanHtml = isPlaceholder ? '<p></p>' : initialHtml;

  if (cleanHtml.includes('<table')) {
    if (!cleanHtml.startsWith('<p>') && !cleanHtml.startsWith('<div>')) {
      cleanHtml = '<p><br></p>' + cleanHtml;
    }
    if (!cleanHtml.endsWith('</p>') && !cleanHtml.endsWith('</div>')) {
      cleanHtml += '<p><br></p>';
    }
  }

  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: [
      StarterKit.configure({ heading: { levels: [1, 2, 3] } }),
      Underline,
      TextAlign.configure({ types: ['heading', 'paragraph'] }),
      TextStyle,
      Color,
      FontFamily,
      FontSize,
      Highlight.configure({ multicolor: true }),
      Link.configure({ openOnClick: false }),
      Table.configure({ resizable: true }),
      TableRow,
      CustomTableHeader,
      CustomTableCell,
      Gapcursor,
      Indent, // <--- Р”РћР‘РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ Р’ РЎРџРРЎРћРљ Р РђРЎРЁРР Р•РќРР™
    ],
    content: cleanHtml,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && editor) {
      editor.commands.setContent(cleanHtml);
      setTimeout(() => { try {
  editor.commands.focus('end');
} catch {
  /* noop: С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РЅРµ РєСЂРёС‚РёС‡РµРЅ, РµСЃР»Рё editor СѓР¶Рµ СЂР°Р·РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ */
} }, 50);
    }
  }, [isOpen, cleanHtml, editor]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (toolbarRef.current && !toolbarRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setPop(null);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const handleSave = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editor) return;
    const html = editor.getHTML();
    const isEmpty = html === '<p></p>' || html === '<p><br></p>';
    onSave(isEmpty ? '' : html);
    onClose();
  }, [editor, onSave, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen || !editor) return null;

  const currentFontSize = (editor.getAttributes('textStyle').fontSize || '16px').replace('px', '');
  const currentColor = editor.getAttributes('textStyle').color || '#000000';
  const currentHighlight = editor.getAttributes('highlight').color || null;

  const Btn = ({ onClick, active, title, children }: any) => (
    <button type="button" className={`rt-btn ${active ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={onClick} title={title}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );

  return (
    <div className="rt-modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={handleSave}>
      <div className="rt-modal" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="rt-header">
          <h3>Р РµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°</h3>
          <button type="button" className="rt-close" onClick={handleSave}>Г—</button>
        </div>

        <div className="rt-toolbar" ref={toolbarRef}>
          <div className="rt-group">
            <select className="rt-select" value={editor.isActive('heading', { level: 1 }) ? 'h1' : editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }) ? 'h2' : editor.isActive('heading', { level: 3 }) ? 'h3' : 'p'} onChange={(e) => { const v = e.target.value; if (v === 'p') editor.chain().focus().setParagraph().run(); else editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: +v[1] as any }).run(); }}>
              <option value="p">РћР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Р№</option>
              <option value="h1">Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє 1</option>
              <option value="h2">Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє 2</option>
              <option value="h3">Р—Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє 3</option>
            </select>
            <select className="rt-select" value={editor.getAttributes('textStyle').fontFamily || 'Arial'} onChange={(e) => editor.chain().focus().setFontFamily(e.target.value).run()}>
              {FONT_FAMILIES.map(f => <option key={f.value} value={f.value}>{f.label}</option>)}
            </select>
            <select className="rt-select" style={{ width: 60 }} value={currentFontSize} onChange={(e) => (editor.chain().focus() as any).setFontSize(`${e.target.value}px`).run()}>
              {FONT_SIZES.map(s => <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()} active={editor.isActive('bold')} title="Р–РёСЂРЅС‹Р№"><b>B</b></Btn>
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()} active={editor.isActive('italic')} title="РљСѓСЂСЃРёРІ"><i>I</i></Btn>
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()} active={editor.isActive('underline')} title="РџРѕРґС‡РµСЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"><u>U</u></Btn>
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()} active={editor.isActive('strike')} title="Р—Р°С‡РµСЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"><s>S</s></Btn>
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setPop(p => p === 'color' ? null : 'color'); }}>
              <span className="rt-letter" style={{ borderBottomColor: currentColor }}>A</span>
            </button>
            {pop === 'color' && <ColorPickerPopover title="Р¦РІРµС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°" colors={TEXT_COLORS.flat()} onPick={(c) => { editor.chain().focus().setColor(c).run(); setPop(null); }} onReset={() => { editor.chain().focus().unsetColor().run(); setPop(null); }} />}
          </div>
          <div className="rt-popover-wrap">
            <button type="button" className="rt-btn" title="Р¤РѕРЅ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°" onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setPop(p => p === 'bg' ? null : 'bg'); }}>
              <span className="rt-letter rt-bg-letter" style={{ background: currentHighlight || 'transparent', borderBottomColor: currentHighlight || '#9ca3af' }}>A</span>
            </button>
            {pop === 'bg' && <ColorPickerPopover title="Р¤РѕРЅ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°" colors={BG_COLORS.flat()} onPick={(c) => { editor.chain().focus().setHighlight({ color: c }).run(); setPop(null); }} onReset={() => { editor.chain().focus().unsetHighlight().run(); setPop(null); }} />}
          </div>

          <div className="rt-sep" />
          <div className="rt-group">
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('left').run()} active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'left' })} title="РџРѕ Р»РµРІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ">в—§</Btn>
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('center').run()} active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'center' })} title="РџРѕ С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ">в—«</Btn>
            <Btn onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('right').run()} active={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'right' })} title="РџРѕ РїСЂР°РІРѕРјСѓ РєСЂР°СЋ">в—Ё</Btn>

FILE: src\presentation\components\shapes\PolylineRenderer.tsx

import React from 'react';
import { Line, Circle } from 'react-konva';
import { DASH_MAP } from '../elements/dashMap';
import type { DashType } from '../../../shared/types';

export interface PolylineRendererProps {
  points: number[];
  stroke: string;
  strokeWidth: number;
  dash?: DashType;
  isArrow?: boolean;
  isSelected?: boolean;
  hitStrokeWidth?: number;
  handleRadius?: number;
  onPointMove?: (index: number, x: number, y: number) => void;
  onPointMoveEnd?: (index: number, x: number, y: number) => void;
  onPointContextMenu?: (index: number, e: any) => void;
}

export const PolylineRenderer: React.FC<PolylineRendererProps> = ({
  points,
  stroke,
  strokeWidth,
  dash = 'solid',
  isArrow = false,
  isSelected = false,
  hitStrokeWidth = 20,
  handleRadius = 6,
  onPointMove,
  onPointMoveEnd,
  onPointContextMenu,
}) => {
  if (!points || points.length < 4) return null;

  const dashArr = DASH_MAP[dash];

  const arrowNode = (() => {
    if (!isArrow || points.length < 4) return null;
    const n = points.length;
    const x1 = points[n - 4], y1 = points[n - 3];
    const x2 = points[n - 2], y2 = points[n - 1];
    const angle = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
    const len = 14;
    const a1 = angle + Math.PI - 0.35;
    const a2 = angle + Math.PI + 0.35;
    return (
      <Line
        points={[
          x2 + len * Math.cos(a1), y2 + len * Math.sin(a1),
          x2, y2,
          x2 + len * Math.cos(a2), y2 + len * Math.sin(a2),
        ]}
        stroke={stroke}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={false}
      />
    );
  })();

  const pointCount = points.length / 2;

  return (
    <>
      <Line
        points={points}
        stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)"
        strokeWidth={hitStrokeWidth}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={true}
      />
      <Line
        points={points}
        stroke={stroke}
        strokeWidth={strokeWidth}
        dash={dashArr}
        lineCap="round"
        lineJoin="round"
        listening={false}
      />
      {arrowNode}
      {isSelected && Array.from({ length: pointCount }).map((_, i) => {
        const px = points[i * 2];
        const py = points[i * 2 + 1];
        return (
          <Circle
            key={i}
            x={px}
            y={py}
            radius={handleRadius}
            fill="#fbbf24"
            stroke="#f59e0b"
            strokeWidth={1.5}
            draggable
            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; }}
            onDragMove={(e) => {
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              onPointMove?.(i, e.target.x(), e.target.y());
            }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => {
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              onPointMoveEnd?.(i, e.target.x(), e.target.y());
            }}
            onContextMenu={(e) => {
              if (!onPointContextMenu) return;
              e.evt.preventDefault();
              e.cancelBubble = true;
              onPointContextMenu(i, e);
            }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\SlidesBar.tsx
SIZE: 2383 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { createSlide, cloneSlide } from '../../domain/slide/operations';

export const SlidesBar: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);

  const addSlide = () => {
    const current = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);
    const next = createSlide(
      slides.length + 1,
      current?.format ?? 'a4',
      current?.orientation ?? 'portrait',
      undefined,
      current?.padding,
    );
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      draft.slides.push(next);
      draft.activeId = next.id;
    }, 'add-slide');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const duplicateSlide = (id: string) => {
    const src = slides.find((s) => s.id === id);
    if (!src) return;
    const clone = cloneSlide(src);
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      draft.slides.push(clone);
      draft.activeId = clone.id;
    }, 'dup-slide');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const removeSlide = (id: string) => {
    if (slides.length <= 1) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const i = draft.slides.findIndex((s) => s.id === id);
      if (i < 0) return;
      draft.slides.splice(i, 1);
      if (draft.activeId === id) draft.activeId = draft.slides[0].id;
    }, 'remove-slide');
    selStore.clear();
  };

  const setActive = (id: string) => {
    docStore.setState((s) => ({ ...s, activeId: id }));
    selStore.clear();
  };

  return (
    <footer className="slides-panel">
      {slides.map((s, i) => (
        <div
          key={s.id}
          className={`slide-thumb ${s.id === activeId ? 'active' : ''}`}
          onClick={() => setActive(s.id)}
          onDoubleClick={() => duplicateSlide(s.id)}
        >
          {i + 1}
          {slides.length > 1 && (
            <button
              onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); removeSlide(s.id); }}
              style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 2, right: 4, border: 'none', background: 'none', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#94a3b8' }}
            >Г—</button>
          )}
        </div>
      ))}
      <button className="add-slide" onClick={addSlide}>+ РЎР»Р°Р№Рґ</button>
    </footer>
  );
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
