import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Stage, Layer, Rect, Circle, Ellipse, Line,
  Text as KonvaText, Shape as KonvaShape, Image as KonvaImage, Transformer,
} from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampElement, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';
import { useStampFillMode } from '../hooks/useStampFillMode';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from './stamps/CompositeFillLayer';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP, konvaStyle } from '../../lib/stamps/render';

interface StampEditorProps {
  stamp: StampElement;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (stamp: StampElement) => void;
}

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image';
interface Snapshot { shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[] }

const SNAP_TO_GRID = 10;
const GRID_SNAP_DIST = 2;
const MIN_SIZE = 8;
const HISTORY_LIMIT = 60;

export const StampEditor: React.FC<StampEditorProps> = ({ stamp, isOpen, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const [shapes, setShapes] = useState<StampShapeItem[]>(stamp.shapes ?? []);
  const [texts, setTexts] = useState<StampTextItem[]>(stamp.texts ?? []);
  const [images, setImages] = useState<StampImageItem[]>(stamp.images ?? []);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<SelectionType | null>(null);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [gridOn, setGridOn] = useState(true);

  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const historyRef = useRef<Snapshot[]>([]);
  const futureRef = useRef<Snapshot[]>([]);

  const pushHistory = () => {
    historyRef.current.push({
      shapes: shapes.map((s) => ({ ...s })),
      texts: texts.map((t) => ({ ...t })),
      images: images.map((im) => ({ ...im })),
    });
    if (historyRef.current.length > HISTORY_LIMIT) historyRef.current.shift();
    futureRef.current = [];
  };

  const setFullState = (next: Snapshot) => {
    setShapes(next.shapes);
    setTexts(next.texts);
    setImages(next.images);
  };

  const undo = () => {
    const prev = historyRef.current.pop();
    if (!prev) return;
    futureRef.current.push({ shapes, texts, images });
    setFullState(prev);
  };

  const redo = () => {
    const next = futureRef.current.pop();
    if (!next) return;
    historyRef.current.push({ shapes, texts, images });
    setFullState(next);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    setShapes(stamp.shapes ?? []);
    setTexts(stamp.texts ?? []);
    setImages(stamp.images ?? []);
    setSelectedId(null);
    setSelectedType(null);
    setZoom(1);
    historyRef.current = [];
    futureRef.current = [];
  }, [isOpen, stamp]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!tr || !stage) return;
    const isFill = shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId)?.kind === 'fill';
    if (!selectedId || isFill) { tr.nodes([]); tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw(); return; }
    const node = stage.findOne('#' + selectedId);
    if (node) tr.nodes([node]);
    tr.getLayer()?.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedId, shapes, texts, images]);

  const handleWheel = (e: any) => {
    e.evt.preventDefault();
    const dir = e.evt.deltaY > 0 ? -1 : 1;
    setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.4, Math.min(6, z + dir * 0.08)));
  };

  const patchShape = (id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => {
    pushHistory();
    setShapes((prev) => prev.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...patch } : s)));
  };
  const patchText = (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => {
    pushHistory();
    setTexts((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, ...patch } : t)));
  };
  const patchImage = (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => {
    pushHistory();
    setImages((prev) => prev.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, ...patch } : im)));
  };

  const addShape = (kind: StampShapeItem['kind']) => {
    const isCircle = kind === 'circle';
    const w = isCircle ? 120 : kind === 'rect' ? 160 : 140;
    const h = isCircle ? 120 : kind === 'rect' ? 100 : 120;
    const s: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(), kind,
      x: Math.round((stamp.width - w) / 2),
      y: Math.round((stamp.height - h) / 2),
      width: w, height: h,
      cornerRadius: kind === 'rect' ? 12 : 0,
      stroke: '#000', strokeWidth: 2, dash: 'solid',
    };
    pushHistory();
    setShapes((prev) => [...prev, s]);
    setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape');
  };

  const addText = (mode: 'straight' | 'arc') => {
    const t: StampTextItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      text: mode === 'arc' ? 'РўР•РљРЎРў РџРћ Р”РЈР“Р•' : 'РќРѕРІС‹Р№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚',
      mode,
      x: Math.round(stamp.width / 2 - 75),
      y: Math.round(stamp.height / 2 - 10),
      width: 150, align: 'center',
      arcCx: stamp.width / 2, arcCy: stamp.height / 2,
      arcRadius: Math.min(stamp.width, stamp.height) / 2 - 8,
      arcStartDeg: -90, arcEndDeg: 90, arcFlip: false,
      arcOffset: 6, arcSpanDeg: 140,
      fontSize: 16, fontFamily: 'Arial',
      fontStyle: 'bold', textDecoration: '', fill: '#000',
    };
    pushHistory();
    setTexts((prev) => [...prev, t]);
    setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text');
  };

  const deleteSelected = () => {
    if (!selectedId) return;
    pushHistory();
    if (selectedType === 'shape') setShapes((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== selectedId));
    if (selectedType === 'text') setTexts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== selectedId));
    if (selectedType === 'image') setImages((prev) => prev.filter((im) => im.id !== selectedId));
    setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null);
  };

  const fillApi = useStampFillMode({
    shapes, texts, images,
    width: stamp.width, height: stamp.height,
    commit: (s, t, i) => { pushHistory(); setShapes(s); setTexts(t); setImages(i); },
    select: (id, type) => { setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType(id ? (type as SelectionType) : null); },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
      const tag = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName;
      const isField = tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA';
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        if (isField) return;
        e.preventDefault(); deleteSelected();
      } else if (ctrl && e.code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); undo(); }
      else if (ctrl && (e.code === 'KeyY' || (e.code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey))) { e.preventDefault(); redo(); }
      else if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [isOpen, shapes, texts, images, selectedId, selectedType]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const selectedShape = selectedType === 'shape' ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) : null;
  const selectedText = selectedType === 'text' ? texts.find((t) => t.id === selectedId) : null;
  const selectedImage = selectedType === 'image' ? images.find((im) => im.id === selectedId) : null;

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-backdrop">
      <div className="stamp-editor-modal">
        <div className="stamp-editor-head">
          <input type="text" defaultValue="Custom Stamp" />
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={undo}>в†¶</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={redo}>в†·</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={onClose}>РћС‚РјРµРЅР°</button>
            <button className="stamp-btn primary" onClick={() => onSave({ ...stamp, shapes, texts, images })}>РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="stamp-toolbar">
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('circle')}>в—Ї РљСЂСѓРі</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('ellipse')}>в¬­ Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('rect')}>в–­ РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРі.</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('triangle')}>в–і РўСЂРµСѓРі.</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addShape('line')}>в•± Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('straight')}>T РўРµРєСЃС‚</button>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => addText('arc')}>в‰ Р”СѓРіР°</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className={`stamp-btn ${fillApi.fillMode ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => fillApi.setFillMode(!fillApi.fillMode)}>рџ–Њ Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР°</button>
          {fillApi.fillMode && (
            <input type="color" value={fillApi.fillColor} onChange={(e) => fillApi.setFillColor(e.target.value)} style={{ width: 32, height: 26 }} />
          )}
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setGridOn((v) => !v)}>{gridOn ? 'в‘' : 'вђ'} РЎРµС‚РєР°</button>
          <span style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', height: 20, margin: '0 8px' }} />
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(6, z + 0.2))}>+</button>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 12, width: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
          <button className="stamp-btn" onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.4, z - 0.2))}>в€’</button>
        </div>

        <div className="stamp-editor-body">
          <div className="stamp-editor-left">
            <div className="stamp-layers-head">РЎР»РѕРё</div>
            <div className="stamp-layers-list">
              {texts.map((t) => (
                <div key={t.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === t.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">T</span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{t.text || '(РїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ)'}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); pushHistory(); setTexts((p) => p.filter((x) => x.id !== t.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
              {images.map((im) => (
                <div key={im.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === im.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => { setSelectedId(im.id); setSelectedType('image'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">рџ–ј</span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{im.name || 'Image'}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); pushHistory(); setImages((p) => p.filter((x) => x.id !== im.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
              {shapes.map((s) => (
                <div key={s.id} className={`stamp-layer ${selectedId === s.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => { setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape'); }}>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-icon">
                    {s.kind === 'circle' ? 'в—Ї' : s.kind === 'ellipse' ? 'в¬­' : s.kind === 'rect' ? 'в–­' : s.kind === 'triangle' ? 'в–і' : s.kind === 'line' ? 'в•±' : 'рџЋЁ'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="stamp-layer-name">{s.name || s.kind}</span>
                  <button className="stamp-layer-btn" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); pushHistory(); setShapes((p) => p.filter((x) => x.id !== s.id)); }}>вњ•</button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="stamp-editor-canvas">
            <Stage
              ref={stageRef}
              width={stamp.width * zoom}
              height={stamp.height * zoom}
              scaleX={zoom} scaleY={zoom}
              onWheel={handleWheel}
              onMouseDown={(e) => {
                if (fillApi.fillMode && e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
                  const pos = e.target.getStage()?.getRelativePointerPosition();
                  if (pos) fillApi.handleCanvasClick(pos.x, pos.y);
                } else if (e.target === e.target.getStage()) {
                  setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null);
                }
              }}
              style={{ background: '#fff', boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)' }}
            >
              <Layer>
                <Rect width={stamp.width} height={stamp.height} fill="#fff" listening={false} />
                {gridOn && (
                  <KonvaShape
                    listening={false}
                    sceneFunc={(ctx, shape) => {
                      const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
                      c.beginPath();
                      for (let x = 0; x <= stamp.width; x += 10) { c.moveTo(x, 0); c.lineTo(x, stamp.height); }
                      for (let y = 0; y <= stamp.height; y += 10) { c.moveTo(0, y); c.lineTo(stamp.width, y); }
                      c.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7';
                      c.lineWidth = 1;
                      c.stroke();
                      ctx.strokeShape(shape);
                    }}
                  />
                )}

                <CompositeFillLayer width={stamp.width} height={stamp.height} shapes={shapes} />

                {shapes.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill').map((s) => (
                  <ShapeNode
                    key={s.id}
                    shape={s}
                    draggable={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    snapEnabled={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(s.id); setSelectedType('shape'); }}
                    onPatch={(p) => patchShape(s.id, p)}
                  />
                ))}

                {images.map((im) => (
                  <ImageNode
                    key={im.id}
                    im={im}
                    draggable={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(im.id); setSelectedType('image'); }}
                    onPatch={(p) => patchImage(im.id, p)}
                  />
                ))}

                {texts.map((t) => (
                  <TextNode
                    key={t.id}
                    t={t}
                    draggable={!fillApi.fillMode}
                    onSelect={() => { setSelectedId(t.id); setSelectedType('text'); }}
                    onPatch={(p) => patchText(t.id, p)}
                  />
                ))}

                {!fillApi.fillMode && (
                  <Transformer
                    ref={trRef}
                    rotateEnabled
                    keepRatio={false}
                    anchorSize={8}
                    onTransformEnd={(e) => {
                      const node = e.target as Konva.Node;
                      const id = node.id();
                      const sx = node.scaleX();
                      const sy = node.scaleY();
                      node.scaleX(1); node.scaleY(1);

                      const shp = shapes.find((s) => s.id === id);
                      if (shp) {
                        patchShape(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x() - (shp.width * sx) / 2),
                          y: Math.round(node.y() - (shp.height * sy) / 2),
                          width: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(shp.width * sx)),
                          height: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(shp.height * sy)),
                          rotation: Math.round(node.rotation()),
                        });
                        return;
                      }
                      const txt = texts.find((t) => t.id === id);
                      if (txt) {
                        patchText(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x()),
                          y: Math.round(node.y()),
                          width: Math.max(20, Math.round((txt.width || 200) * sx)),

                        });
                        return;
                      }
                      const img = images.find((im) => im.id === id);
                      if (img) {
                        patchImage(id, {
                          x: Math.round(node.x() - (img.width * sx) / 2),
                          y: Math.round(node.y() - (img.height * sy) / 2),
                          width: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(img.width * sx)),
                          height: Math.max(MIN_SIZE, Math.round(img.height * sy)),
                          rotation: Math.round(node.rotation()),
                        });
                      }
                    }}
                  />
                )}
              </Layer>
            </Stage>
          </div>

          <div className="stamp-editor-right">
            {selectedShape && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹</h4>
                <label>РћР±РІРѕРґРєР° <input type="color" value={selectedShape.stroke || '#000000'} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { stroke: e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>РўРѕР»С‰РёРЅР° <input type="number" min={0} value={selectedShape.strokeWidth} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° <input type="color" value={selectedShape.fill || '#ffffff'} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>РЎРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ <input type="number" min={0} value={selectedShape.cornerRadius} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { cornerRadius: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>РЈРіРѕР» <input type="number" value={selectedShape.rotation ?? 0} onChange={(e) => patchShape(selectedShape.id, { rotation: +e.target.value })} /></label>
              </>
            )}
            {selectedText && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р°</h4>
                <label>РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ
                  <textarea value={selectedText.text} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { text: e.target.value })} rows={3} />
                </label>
                <label>Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ <input type="number" value={selectedText.fontSize} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { fontSize: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ <input type="color" value={selectedText.fill} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
                {selectedText.mode === 'arc' && (
                  <>
                    <h4 style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>Р”СѓРіР°</h4>
                    <label>Р Р°РґРёСѓСЃ <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcRadius} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { arcRadius: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                    <label>РќР°С‡Р°Р»Рѕ (В°) <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcStartDeg} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { arcStartDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                    <label>РљРѕРЅРµС† (В°) <input type="number" value={selectedText.arcEndDeg} onChange={(e) => patchText(selectedText.id, { arcEndDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
                  </>
                )}
              </>
            )}
            {selectedImage && (
              <>
                <h4>РЎРІРѕР№СЃС‚РІР° РёР·РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёСЏ</h4>
                <img src={selectedImage.src} alt="" style={{ width: '100%', maxHeight: 120, objectFit: 'contain' }} />
              </>
            )}
            {!selectedShape && !selectedText && !selectedImage && (
              <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 40 }}>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

/* ============================================================
   Sub-components
   ============================================================ */

interface ShapeNodeProps {
  shape: StampShapeItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  snapEnabled: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onPatch: (p: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => void;
}

const ShapeNode: React.FC<ShapeNodeProps> = ({ shape, draggable, snapEnabled, onSelect, onPatch }) => {
  const cx = shape.x + shape.width / 2;
  const cy = shape.y + shape.height / 2;

  const common = {
    stroke: shape.stroke,
    strokeWidth: shape.strokeWidth,
    dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[shape.dash],
    fill: shape.fill || 'transparent',
    listening: true,
    draggable,
    onClick: onSelect,
    onTap: onSelect,
    onDragEnd: (e: any) => {
      let nx = e.target.x() - shape.width / 2;
      let ny = e.target.y() - shape.height / 2;
      if (snapEnabled && !e.evt?.ctrlKey && !e.evt?.metaKey) {
        const gx = Math.round(nx / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
        const gy = Math.round(ny / SNAP_TO_GRID) * SNAP_TO_GRID;
        if (Math.abs(gx - nx) <= GRID_SNAP_DIST) nx = gx;
        if (Math.abs(gy - ny) <= GRID_SNAP_DIST) ny = gy;
      }
      onPatch({ x: Math.round(nx), y: Math.round(ny) });
    },
  };

  switch (shape.kind) {
    case 'circle':
      return <Circle id={shape.id} name="stamp-element" x={cx} y={cy} radius={Math.min(shape.width, shape.height) / 2} {...common} />;
    case 'ellipse':
      return <Ellipse id={shape.id} name="stamp-element" x={cx} y={cy} radiusX={shape.width / 2} radiusY={shape.height / 2} {...common} />;
    case 'rect':
      return (
        <Rect
          id={shape.id}
          name="stamp-element"
          x={cx}
          y={cy}
          offsetX={shape.width / 2}
          offsetY={shape.height / 2}
          width={shape.width}
          height={shape.height}
          cornerRadius={shape.cornerRadius || 0}
          {...common}
        />
      );
    case 'triangle':
      return (
        <Line
          id={shape.id}
          name="stamp-element"
          points={[cx, shape.y, shape.x + shape.width, shape.y + shape.height, shape.x, shape.y + shape.height]}
          closed
          {...common}
        />
      );
    case 'line':
      return (
        <Line
          id={shape.id}
          name="stamp-element"
          x={shape.x}
          y={shape.y}
          points={shape.points && shape.points.length >= 4 ? shape.points.slice() : [0, shape.height / 2, shape.width, shape.height / 2]}
          {...common}
        />
      );
    default:
      return null;
  }
};

interface TextNodeProps {
  t: StampTextItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onPatch: (p: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
}

const TextNode: React.FC<TextNodeProps> = ({ t, draggable, onSelect, onPatch }) => {
  if (t.mode === 'straight') {
    return (
      <KonvaText
        id={t.id}
        name="stamp-element"
        x={t.x} y={t.y} width={t.width}
        text={t.text}
        fontSize={t.fontSize}
        fontFamily={t.fontFamily}
        fontStyle={konvaStyle(t.fontStyle)}
        textDecoration={t.textDecoration}
        fill={t.fill}
        align={t.align}
        draggable={draggable}
        onClick={onSelect}
        onTap={onSelect}
        onDragEnd={(e) => onPatch({ x: Math.round(e.target.x()), y: Math.round(e.target.y()) })}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <KonvaShape
      id={t.id}
      name="stamp-element"
      draggable={draggable}
      onClick={onSelect}
      onTap={onSelect}
      onDragEnd={(e) => onPatch({ x: Math.round(e.target.x()), y: Math.round(e.target.y()) })}
      sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
        const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        const parts: string[] = [];
        if (t.fontStyle.includes('italic')) parts.push('italic');
        if (t.fontStyle.includes('bold')) parts.push('bold');
        parts.push(`${t.fontSize}px`, `${t.fontFamily}, Arial`);
        c.font = parts.join(' ');
        c.fillStyle = t.fill;
        c.textAlign = 'center';
        c.textBaseline = 'middle';
        const chars = Array.from(t.text);
        const start = (t.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
        const end = (t.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
        const step = chars.length > 1 ? (end - start) / (chars.length - 1) : 0;
        const rx = t.arcRx ?? t.arcRadius;
        const ry = t.arcRy ?? t.arcRadius;
        chars.forEach((ch, i) => {
          const a = start + i * step;
          const x = t.arcCx + Math.cos(a) * rx;
          const y = t.arcCy + Math.sin(a) * ry;
          const tx = -rx * Math.sin(a);
          const ty = ry * Math.cos(a);
          const rot = Math.atan2(ty, tx);
          c.save();
          c.translate(x, y);
          c.rotate(rot);
          c.fillText(ch, 0, 0);
          c.restore();
        });
      }}
    />
  );
};

interface ImageNodeProps {
  im: StampImageItem;
  draggable: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void;
  onPatch: (p: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
}

const ImageNode: React.FC<ImageNodeProps> = ({ im, draggable, onSelect, onPatch }) => {
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!im.src) { setImg(null); return; }
    const i = new window.Image();
    i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    i.onload = () => setImg(i);
    i.onerror = () => setImg(null);
    i.src = im.src;
  }, [im.src]);
  if (!img) return null;
  return (
    <KonvaImage
      id={im.id}
      name="stamp-element"
      x={im.x} y={im.y}
      width={im.width} height={im.height}
      image={img}
      draggable={draggable}
      onClick={onSelect}
      onTap={onSelect}
      onDragEnd={(e) => onPatch({ x: Math.round(e.target.x()), y: Math.round(e.target.y()) })}
    />
  );
};

interface EditingState {
  mode: 'create' | 'edit';
  sourceId?: string;
  sourceIsBuiltin?: boolean;
  stamp: StampElement;
}

export const StampLibraryPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<EditingState | null>(null);

  const stamps = useMemo(() => getAllStamps(), [version]);

  const addElement = (el: StampElement) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      (s as any).elements = [...(s.elements as any), el];
    }, 'add-stamp');
    selStore.select(el.id);
  };

  const insertTemplate = (tpl: StampTemplate) =>
    addElement(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));

  const openCreate = () => {
    setEditing({
      mode: 'create',
      stamp: {
        id: generateId(),
        type: 'stamp',
        x: 200,
        y: 200,
        width: 200,
        height: 200,
        rotation: 0,
        shapes: [],
        texts: [],
        images: [],
      },
    });
  };

  const openEdit = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    const builtin = isBuiltinStamp(tpl.id);
    setEditing({
      mode: 'edit',
      sourceId: tpl.id,
      sourceIsBuiltin: builtin,
      stamp: {
        id: generateId(),
        type: 'stamp',
        x: 200,
        y: 200,
        width: tpl.width,
        height: tpl.height,
        rotation: 0,
        shapes: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.shapes)),
        texts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.texts)),
        images: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.images || [])),
      },
    });
  };

  /* В§F-SLIB-05..08 */
  const handleSave = (saved: StampElement) => {
    if (!editing) return;
    const shapes = saved.shapes ?? [];
    const texts = saved.texts ?? [];
    const images = saved.images ?? [];

    if (editing.mode === 'create') {
      const tpl: StampTemplate = {
        id: `custom-${Date.now()}`,
        name: saved.name || 'РњРѕР№ С€С‚Р°РјРї',
        width: saved.width,
        height: saved.height,
        shapes: [...shapes],
        texts: [...texts],
        images: [...images],
        custom: true,
      };
      addCustomStamp(tpl);
      addElement({ ...newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200) });
    } else if (editing.sourceIsBuiltin) {
      /* В§F-SLIB-08: builtin РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ вЂ” С„РѕСЂРєР°РµРј РІ custom. */
      const source = stamps.find((s) => s.id === editing.sourceId);
      if (source) {
        const copy = forkStampAsCustom({
          ...source,
          width: saved.width,
          height: saved.height,
          shapes: [...shapes],
          texts: [...texts],
          images: [...images],
        });
        addCustomStamp(copy);
        addElement({ ...newStampFromTemplate(copy, 200, 200) });
      }
    } else if (editing.sourceId) {
      /* В§F-SLIB-07: РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РµРіРѕ custom С€С‚Р°РјРїР°. */
      updateCustomStamp(editing.sourceId, {
        width: saved.width,
        height: saved.height,
        shapes: [...shapes],
        texts: [...texts],
        images: [...images],
      });
    }

    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
    setEditing(null);
  };

  const deleteStamp = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    if (!tpl.custom) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРї В«${tpl.name}В» РёР· Р±РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєРё?`)) return;
    removeCustomStamp(tpl.id);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="tool-btn"
        onClick={openCreate}
        style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}
      >
        + РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРївЂ¦
      </button>

      <div className="tool-grid">
        {stamps.map((s) => (
          <div key={s.id} style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="tool-tile"
              title={s.description || s.name}
              onClick={() => insertTemplate(s)}
              style={{ width: '100%' }}
            >
              <span className="tile-ico">{s.icon || 'рџ–ј'}</span>

              type="button"
              title="Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ"

FILE: src\presentation\components\stamps\canvas\CompositeFillLayer.tsx
SIZE: 3394 bytes

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../../shared/types'
import type { FillSpec } from '../../../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import { useFillWorker } from '../../../../hooks/useFillWorker';

interface Props {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  enabled?: boolean;
}

function anchorToWorld(
  target: StampShapeItem,
  local: { x: number; y: number },
): { x: number; y: number } {
  const lx = target.x + local.x * target.width;
  const ly = target.y + local.y * target.height;
  const cx = target.x + target.width / 2;
  const cy = target.y + target.height / 2;
  const rot = ((target.rotation || 0) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const dx = lx - cx;
  const dy = ly - cy;
  return {
    x: cx + dx * Math.cos(rot) - dy * Math.sin(rot),
    y: cy + dx * Math.sin(rot) + dy * Math.cos(rot),
  };
}

export const CompositeFillLayer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  width,
  height,
  shapes,
  enabled = true,
}) => {
  const { result, rasterize } = useFillWorker();
  const [img, setImg] = useState<HTMLImageElement | null>(null);
  const debounceRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const fills: FillSpec[] = useMemo(() => {
    const out: FillSpec[] = [];
    for (const s of shapes) {
      if (s.kind !== 'fill') continue;
      if (s.hidden) continue;
      if (!s.fillTargetShapeId) continue;

      const target = shapes.find((t) => t.id === s.fillTargetShapeId);
      if (!target) continue;

      const local = s.fillAnchorLocal ?? { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 };
      const { x, y } = anchorToWorld(target, local);

      out.push({
        px: x,
        py: y,
        color: s.fill || '#fbbf24',
        target,
      });
    }
    return out;
  }, [shapes]);

  const signature = useMemo(() => {
    const geo = shapes
      .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
      .map(
        (s) =>
          `${s.kind}:${Math.round(s.x)}|${Math.round(s.y)}|${Math.round(s.width)}|${Math.round(s.height)}|${Math.round(s.rotation || 0)}|${(s.points || []).map((v) => Math.round(v)).join(',')}|${s.strokeWidth}`,
      )
      .join('||');
    const f = fills
      .map((x) => `${Math.round(x.px)},${Math.round(x.py)},${x.color},${x.target?.id ?? '-'}`)
      .join(';');
    return geo + '###' + f;
  }, [shapes, fills]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || !fills.length) {
      setImg(null);
      return;
    }
    if (debounceRef.current) window.clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);

    debounceRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      rasterize(signature, width, height, shapes, fills);
    }, 80);

    return () => {
      if (debounceRef.current) window.clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
    };
  }, [signature, width, height, enabled, fills, shapes, rasterize]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!result || result.key !== signature) return;
    if (!result.url) { setImg(null); return; }
    const next = new window.Image();
    next.onload = () => setImg(next);
    next.onerror = () => setImg(null);
    next.src = result.url;
  }, [result, signature]);

  if (!img) return null;

  return (
    <KImage
      x={0}
      y={0}
      width={width}
      height={height}
      image={img}
      listening={false}
    />
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Layer, Rect, Line, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import { GRID_SIZE } from '../../../../shared/constants';

interface Props { width: number; height: number; zoom: number; showGrid: boolean; }

export const StampBackgroundLayer: React.FC<Props> = ({ width, height, zoom, showGrid }) => (
  <Layer>
    <Rect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={height} fill="#ffffff" shadowColor="#000" shadowBlur={14} shadowOpacity={0.12} listening={false} />
    {showGrid && (
      <Shape listening={false} sceneFunc={(ctx) => {
        const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D;
        c.beginPath();
        for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += GRID_SIZE) { c.moveTo(x, 0); c.lineTo(x, height); }
        for (let y = 0; y <= height; y += GRID_SIZE) { c.moveTo(0, y); c.lineTo(width, y); }
        c.strokeStyle = '#eef2f7'; c.lineWidth = 1 / zoom; c.stroke();
      }} />
    )}
    <Line points={[width / 2, 0, width / 2, height]} stroke="#dbeafe" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
    <Line points={[0, height / 2, width, height / 2]} stroke="#dbeafe" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} dash={[4 / zoom, 4 / zoom]} listening={false} />
    <Rect x={0} y={0} width={width} height={height} stroke="#cbd5e1" strokeWidth={1 / zoom} listening={false} />
  </Layer>
);

import React from 'react';
import { Layer, Image as KImage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../../shared/types';
import { StampShapeView } from '../StampShapeView';
import { StampStraightTextView } from '../StampStraightTextView';
import { StampArcTextView } from '../StampArcTextView';
import { StampTransformer } from './StampTransformer';
import { CompositeFillLayer } from './CompositeFillLayer';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;
interface Props {
  layerRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Layer>; trRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Transformer>;
  stampW: number; stampH: number; shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; imageCache: Record<string, HTMLImageElement>;
  selectedId: string | null; selectedType: SelectionType; selectedShape: StampShapeItem | null;
  snap: boolean; listening?: boolean;
  onSelect: (id: string, type: Exclude<SelectionType, null>) => void;
  onShapeDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onTextDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, text: StampTextItem) => void;
  onImageDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, image: StampImageItem) => void;
  onTransformEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<Event>) => void;
  onOpenTextEditor: (id: string) => void;
  onUpdateText: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
  onPointsChange?: (id: string, points: number[]) => void;
}

export const StampElementsLayer: React.FC<Props> = ({
  layerRef, trRef, stampW, stampH, shapes, texts, images, imageCache,
  selectedId, selectedType, selectedShape, snap, listening = true,
  onSelect, onShapeDragEnd, onTextDragEnd, onImageDragEnd, onTransformEnd, onOpenTextEditor, onUpdateText, onPointsChange,
}) => {
  const regularShapes = React.useMemo(() => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill'), [shapes]);
  const legacyFills = React.useMemo(() => shapes.filter((s) => s.kind === 'fill' && !s.fillTargetShapeId), [shapes]);

  return (
    <Layer ref={layerRef as any} listening={listening}>
      <CompositeFillLayer width={stampW} height={stampH} shapes={shapes} />
      {legacyFills.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : (
        <StampShapeView key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} draggable={false} onSelect={() => onSelect(s.id, 'shape')} onDragEnd={() => {}} />
      ))}
      {regularShapes.map((s) => s.hidden ? null : (
        <StampShapeView key={s.id} shape={s} allShapes={shapes} draggable={!s.locked} isSelected={selectedId === s.id && selectedType === 'shape'}
          onSelect={() => onSelect(s.id, 'shape')} onDragEnd={(e) => onShapeDragEnd(s.id, e)}
          onPointsChange={onPointsChange ? (points) => onPointsChange(s.id, points) : undefined} />
      ))}
      {images.map((im) => {
        if (im.hidden) return null; const img = imageCache[im.src]; if (!img) return null;
        return (
          <KImage key={im.id} id={im.id} name="image" x={im.x} y={im.y} width={im.width} height={im.height} image={img} draggable={!im.locked}
            onClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(im.id, 'image'); }} onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(im.id, 'image'); }}
            onDragEnd={(e) => onImageDragEnd(im.id, e, im)} />
        );
      })}
      {texts.map((t) => t.hidden ? null : t.mode === 'straight' ? (
        <StampStraightTextView key={t.id} text={t} draggable={!t.locked} onSelect={() => onSelect(t.id, 'text')}
          onDblClick={() => { onSelect(t.id, 'text'); onOpenTextEditor(t.id); }} onDragEnd={(e) => onTextDragEnd(t.id, e, t)} />
      ) : (
        <StampArcTextView key={t.id} text={t} shapes={shapes} snapEnabled={snap} draggable={!t.locked}
          onSelect={() => onSelect(t.id, 'text')} onDblClick={() => { onSelect(t.id, 'text'); onOpenTextEditor(t.id); }}
          onUpdate={(patch) => onUpdateText(t.id, patch)} />
      ))}
      {listening && <StampTransformer trRef={trRef} selectedShape={selectedShape} onTransformEnd={onTransformEnd} />}
    </Layer>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

  name: string; width: number; height: number;
  onChangeName: (v: string) => void; onChangeWidth: (v: number) => void; onChangeHeight: (v: number) => void;
  onSave: () => void; onCancel: () => void;
}

export const StampHead: React.FC<Props> = ({ name, width, height, onChangeName, onChangeWidth, onChangeHeight, onSave, onCancel }) => (
  <div className="stamp-editor-head">
    <input className="stamp-editor-name" value={name} onChange={(e) => onChangeName(e.target.value)} placeholder="РќР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ С€С‚Р°РјРїР°" />

import React from 'react';
import type { StampTextItem } from '../../../../shared/types';
import { arcTextBBox } from '../../../../lib/stamps/geometry';

interface Props { text: StampTextItem; zoom: number; pan: { x: number; y: number }; onChange: (v: string) => void; onClose: () => void; }

export const StampInlineTextEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({ text, zoom, pan, onChange, onClose }) => {
  let left = 0, top = 0, width = 200, height = 30;
  if (text.mode === 'straight') {
    left = text.x * zoom + pan.x; top = text.y * zoom + pan.y;
    width = Math.max(80, text.width * zoom); height = Math.max(20, text.fontSize * 1.5 * zoom);
  } else {
    const rx = text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius; const ry = text.arcRy ?? text.arcRadius;
    const bb = arcTextBBox({ text: text.text, arcCx: text.arcCx, arcCy: text.arcCy, arcRadius: text.arcRadius, arcRx: rx, arcRy: ry, arcStartDeg: text.arcStartDeg, arcEndDeg: text.arcEndDeg, fontSize: text.fontSize });
    const cx = bb.x + bb.width / 2; const cy = bb.y + bb.height / 2;
    width = Math.max(140, Math.min(280, bb.width * zoom)); height = Math.max(24, text.fontSize * 1.6 * zoom);
    left = cx * zoom + pan.x - width / 2; top = cy * zoom + pan.y - height / 2;
  }
  return (
    <textarea autoFocus value={text.text} onFocus={(e) => e.currentTarget.select()} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} onBlur={onClose}
      onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); onClose(); } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); (e.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).blur(); } }}
      onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      style={{ position: 'absolute', left, top, width, minHeight: height, zIndex: 50, boxSizing: 'border-box', padding: '2px 6px', border: '2px solid #6366f1', borderRadius: 4, outline: 'none', resize: 'none', background: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.98)', boxShadow: '0 0 0 4px rgba(99,102,241,0.15)', fontSize: Math.max(10, text.fontSize * zoom), fontFamily: text.fontFamily, fontWeight: text.fontStyle.includes('bold') ? 700 : 400, fontStyle: text.fontStyle.includes('italic') ? 'italic' : 'normal', textDecoration: text.textDecoration, color: text.fill, textAlign: text.align as any, lineHeight: 1.3, whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap', wordBreak: 'break-word' }}
    />
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { StampShapeKind } from '../../../../shared/types';
import type { ShapeViewStrategy, ShapePanelContext } from './types';
import { RectView, CircleView, EllipseView, TriangleView, LineView, FillView } from './ShapeViews';
import { ClosedShapePanel, LinePanel, FillPanel } from './ShapePanels';

const RectPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = (props) => React.createElement(ClosedShapePanel, { ...props, showCornerRadius: true });

export const shapeViews: Record<StampShapeKind, ShapeViewStrategy> = {
  rect:     { kind: 'rect',     View: RectView,     Panel: RectPanel },
  circle:   { kind: 'circle',   View: CircleView,   Panel: ClosedShapePanel },
  ellipse:  { kind: 'ellipse',  View: EllipseView,  Panel: ClosedShapePanel },
  triangle: { kind: 'triangle', View: TriangleView, Panel: ClosedShapePanel },
  line:     { kind: 'line',     View: LineView,     Panel: LinePanel },
  fill:     { kind: 'fill',     View: FillView,     Panel: FillPanel },
};

export function getShapeView(kind: StampShapeKind): ShapeViewStrategy {
  return shapeViews[kind];
}

export const LinePanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = ({ shape, onChange }) => {
  const points = shape.points && shape.points.length >= 4 ? shape.points : [0, shape.height / 2, shape.width, shape.height / 2];
  const pointCount = points.length / 2;
  const setPoint = (index: number, x: number, y: number) => { const next = [...points]; next[index * 2] = x; next[index * 2 + 1] = y; onChange({ points: next }); };
  const addPointAtEnd = () => { const lastX = points[points.length - 2]; const lastY = points[points.length - 1]; const prevX = points[points.length - 4]; const prevY = points[points.length - 3]; onChange({ points: [...points, lastX + (lastX - prevX), lastY + (lastY - prevY)] }); };
  const removePointAt = (index: number) => { if (pointCount <= 2) return; const next = points.slice(); next.splice(index * 2, 2); onChange({ points: next }); };
  return (
    <>
      <h4>Р›РёРЅРёСЏ</h4>
      <label>РРјСЏ <input value={shape.name || ''} placeholder="Р›РёРЅРёСЏ" onChange={(e) => onChange({ name: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <h4>РўРѕС‡РєРё ({pointCount})</h4>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4, marginBottom: 8 }}>
        {Array.from({ length: pointCount }).map((_, i) => (
          <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, alignItems: 'center' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 10, color: '#6b7280', width: 20 }}>#{i + 1}</span>
            <input type="number" value={Math.round(points[i * 2])} onChange={(e) => setPoint(i, +e.target.value, points[i * 2 + 1])} style={{ width: 60, padding: '4px 6px', fontSize: 11 }} />
            <input type="number" value={Math.round(points[i * 2 + 1])} onChange={(e) => setPoint(i, points[i * 2], +e.target.value)} style={{ width: 60, padding: '4px 6px', fontSize: 11 }} />
            <button type="button" disabled={pointCount <= 2} onClick={() => removePointAt(i)} style={{ padding: '4px 8px', fontSize: 11, color: pointCount <= 2 ? '#9ca3af' : '#dc2626', border: '1px solid #e5e7eb', borderRadius: 4, background: '#fff' }}>Г—</button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <button type="button" className="tool-btn" onClick={addPointAtEnd}>+ Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ</button>
      <h4>РћР±РІРѕРґРєР°</h4>
      <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ <input type="color" value={shape.stroke || '#000000'} onChange={(e) => onChange({ stroke: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>РўРѕР»С‰РёРЅР° <input type="number" min="0.25" max="40" step="0.25" value={shape.strokeWidth} onChange={(e) => onChange({ strokeWidth: +e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>РЎС‚РёР»СЊ
        <select value={shape.dash} onChange={(e) => onChange({ dash: e.target.value as DashType })}>
          <option value="solid">РЎРїР»РѕС€РЅР°СЏ</option><option value="dashed">РЁС‚СЂРёС…</option><option value="dotted">РўРѕС‡РєРё</option><option value="dashdot">РЁС‚СЂРёС…-РїСѓРЅРєС‚РёСЂ</option><option value="longdash">Р”Р»РёРЅРЅС‹Р№ С€С‚СЂРёС…</option>
        </select>
      </label>
    </>
  );
};

const AnchorFillPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = ({ shape, allShapes, onChange }) => {
  const target = shape.fillTargetShapeId ? allShapes.find((s) => s.id === shape.fillTargetShapeId) || null : null;
  return (
    <>
      <h4>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё</h4>

      <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ <input type="color" value={(shape.fill && shape.fill.startsWith('#')) ? shape.fill : '#fbbf24'} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 8 }}>
        <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}><input type="checkbox" checked={!!shape.locked} onChange={(e) => onChange({ locked: e.target.checked })} /> Р—Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ</label>
        <label className="check" style={{ flex: 1 }}><input type="checkbox" checked={!!shape.hidden} onChange={(e) => onChange({ hidden: e.target.checked })} /> РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ</label>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

const LegacyPathFillPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = ({ shape, allShapes, onChange }) => {
  const fillMode: StampFillMode = shape.fillMode || 'subtract';
  const outer = shape.fillOuterId ? allShapes.find((s) => s.id === shape.fillOuterId) || null : null;
  const holeIds = shape.fillHoleIds || [];
  const candidates = allShapes.filter((s) => s.id !== shape.id && s.kind !== 'fill');
  const toggleHole = (id: string) => { const next = holeIds.includes(id) ? holeIds.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...holeIds, id]; onChange({ fillHoleIds: next }); };
  return (
    <>
      <h4>Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё</h4>
      <label>Р РµР¶РёРј
        <select value={fillMode} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fillMode: e.target.value as StampFillMode })}>
          <option value="subtract">Р’С‹С‡РµСЃС‚СЊ</option><option value="intersect">РџРµСЂРµСЃРµС‡РµРЅРёРµ</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ <input type="color" value={(shape.fill && shape.fill.startsWith('#')) ? shape.fill : '#bfdbfe'} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <h4>Р¤РёРіСѓСЂР°-РѕСЃРЅРѕРІР°</h4>
      <select value={shape.fillOuterId || ''} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fillOuterId: e.target.value || undefined })}>
        <option value="">-- РЅРµ РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅР° --</option>
        {candidates.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.name || KIND_LABEL[s.kind]} ({s.id.slice(0, 4)}вЂ¦)</option>)}
      </select>
      {!outer && <p className="hint-sm" style={{ color: '#c00' }}>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ С„РёРіСѓСЂСѓ.</p>}
      <h4>{fillMode === 'intersect' ? 'РџРµСЂРµСЃРµС‡РµРЅРёРµ СЃ' : 'Р’С‹С‡РµСЃС‚СЊ'}</h4>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 4 }}>
        {candidates.filter((s) => s.id !== shape.fillOuterId).map((s) => (
          <label key={s.id} className="check" style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>
            <input type="checkbox" checked={holeIds.includes(s.id)} onChange={() => toggleHole(s.id)} />
            <span>{s.name || KIND_LABEL[s.kind]} <span style={{ color: '#999', fontSize: 10 }}>{s.id.slice(0, 4)}вЂ¦</span></span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

export const FillPanel: React.FC<ShapePanelContext> = (props) => {
  if (props.shape.fillTargetShapeId) return <AnchorFillPanel {...props} />;
  return <LegacyPathFillPanel {...props} />;
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Ellipse, Rect, Line, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../../shared/types';
import { STAMP_DASH_MAP } from '../../../../lib/stamps/render';
import { paintFill } from '../../../../lib/stamps/fillGeometry';
import type { ShapeViewContext } from './types';
import { PolylineRenderer } from '../../../shapes/PolylineRenderer';
import { ensureMinimalPoints, parsePoints, serializePoints, insertPoint } from '../../../../lib/polyline';

function useGroupProps(ctx: ShapeViewContext) {
  const { shape: s } = ctx; const w = Math.max(1, s.width); const h = Math.max(1, s.height);
  return {
    group: { id: s.id, name: 'shape', x: s.x + w / 2, y: s.y + h / 2, offsetX: w / 2, offsetY: h / 2, rotation: s.rotation || 0, draggable: ctx.draggable, onDragEnd: ctx.onDragEnd, onClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; ctx.onSelect(); }, onTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; ctx.onSelect(); } },
    w, h,
  };
}
function useBaseStyle(s: StampShapeItem) {
  return { stroke: s.stroke, strokeWidth: s.strokeWidth, dash: STAMP_DASH_MAP[s.dash], fill: s.fill || 'transparent', listening: true };
}

export const RectView: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx); const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape);
  return <Group {...group}><Rect x={0} y={0} width={w} height={h} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening /><Rect x={0} y={0} width={w} height={h} cornerRadius={ctx.shape.cornerRadius} {...base} /></Group>;
};
export const CircleView: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx); const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape); const r = Math.max(1, Math.min(w, h) / 2);
  return <Group {...group}><Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={r} radiusY={r} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening /><Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={r} radiusY={r} {...base} /></Group>;
};
export const EllipseView: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx); const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape); const rx = Math.max(1, w / 2); const ry = Math.max(1, h / 2);
  return <Group {...group}><Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={rx} radiusY={ry} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening /><Ellipse x={w / 2} y={h / 2} radiusX={rx} radiusY={ry} {...base} /></Group>;
};
export const TriangleView: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx); const base = useBaseStyle(ctx.shape); const points = [w / 2, 0, w, h, 0, h, w / 2, 0];
  return <Group {...group}><Line points={points} closed fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening /><Line points={points} closed lineJoin="round" {...base} /></Group>;
};

export const LineView: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => {
  const { group, w, h } = useGroupProps(ctx); const s = ctx.shape; const points = ensureMinimalPoints(s.points, w, h);
  const handleDblClick = (e: any) => {
    if (!ctx.onPointsChange) return; e.cancelBubble = true;
    const stage = e.target.getStage(); const pointer = stage?.getPointerPosition(); if (!stage || !pointer) return;
    let node: any = e.target; while (node && typeof node.name === 'function' && node.name() !== 'shape') { node = node.getParent?.(); }
    if (!node) return;
    const inv = node.getAbsoluteTransform().copy().invert(); const local = inv.point(pointer);
    const zoom = stage.scaleX() || 1; const snapRadius = 5 / zoom;
    const pts = parsePoints(points); const next = insertPoint(pts, local.x, local.y, snapRadius); if (next === pts) return;
    ctx.onPointsChange(serializePoints(next));
  };
  const handlePointMove = (index: number, x: number, y: number) => { if (!ctx.onPointsChange) return; const next = [...points]; next[index * 2] = x; next[index * 2 + 1] = y; ctx.onPointsChange(next); };
  const handlePointContextMenu = (index: number) => { if (!ctx.onPointsChange) return; const pts = parsePoints(points); if (pts.length <= 2) return; const next = pts.slice(); next.splice(index, 1); ctx.onPointsChange(serializePoints(next)); };
  return (
    <Group {...group} onDblClick={handleDblClick} onDblTap={handleDblClick}>
      <PolylineRenderer points={points} stroke={s.stroke} strokeWidth={s.strokeWidth} dash={s.dash} isArrow={!!s.points && (s as any).isArrow} isSelected={!!ctx.isSelected} onPointMove={handlePointMove} onPointContextMenu={handlePointContextMenu} />
    </Group>
  );
};

const PathFill: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = ({ shape: s, allShapes, onSelect }) => {
  const outer = s.fillOuterId ? allShapes.find((x) => x.id === s.fillOuterId) || null : null; if (!outer) return null;
  const holes = (s.fillHoleIds || []).map((id) => allShapes.find((x) => x.id === id)).filter((x): x is StampShapeItem => !!x && !x.hidden && x.kind !== 'fill');
  const mode = s.fillMode || 'subtract';
  return (
    <Shape id={s.id} name="shape" listening draggable={false} onClick={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); }} onTap={(e) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); }}
      sceneFunc={(c) => { const raw = (c as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D; paintFill(raw, { outer, holes, mode }, s.fill || '#bfdbfe'); }}
      hitFunc={(c) => { const raw = (c as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D; paintFill(raw, { outer, holes, mode }, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.001)'); }}
    />
  );
};
export const FillView: React.FC<ShapeViewContext> = (ctx) => { if (ctx.shape.fillTargetShapeId) return null; return <PathFill {...ctx} />; };

import React, { useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Group, Rect, Line, Shape } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampTextItem, StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { canvasFont, drawArcText } from '../../../lib/stamps/render';
import {
  arcTextBBox, findNearestShapeByCursor, computeArcParamsForAngle,
  closestPointOnHostPerimeter, drawPerimeterText, DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET, DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG,
} from './stampSnap';

interface Props { text: StampTextItem; shapes: StampShapeItem[]; snapEnabled: boolean; draggable: boolean; onSelect: () => void; onDblClick: () => void; onUpdate: (patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void; }

function resolveArcRadii(text: StampTextItem, host: StampShapeItem | null, arcOffset: number): { rx: number; ry: number } {
  if (text.arcRx != null || text.arcRy != null) return { rx: text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius, ry: text.arcRy ?? text.arcRadius };
  if (host && host.kind === 'ellipse' && host.width !== host.height) {
    const sign = text.arcOutside ? 1 : -1; const minR = text.fontSize * 0.8;
    return { rx: Math.max(minR, host.width / 2 + sign * arcOffset), ry: Math.max(minR, host.height / 2 + sign * arcOffset) };
  }
  return { rx: text.arcRadius, ry: text.arcRadius };
}

export const StampArcTextView: React.FC<Props> = ({ text, shapes, snapEnabled, draggable, onSelect, onDblClick, onUpdate }) => {
  const groupRef = useRef<Konva.Group>(null);
  const host = text.arcHostId ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === text.arcHostId) || null : shapes.find((s) => { if (s.hidden || s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') return false; return Math.abs(s.x + s.width / 2 - text.arcCx) < 1 && Math.abs(s.y + s.height / 2 - text.arcCy) < 1; }) || null;
  const arcOffset = text.arcOffset ?? DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET;
  const isOutside = !!text.arcOutside;
  const spanDeg = text.arcSpanDeg ?? Math.abs(text.arcEndDeg - text.arcStartDeg) ?? DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG;
  const centerDeg = (text.arcStartDeg + text.arcEndDeg) / 2;
  const onPerimeter = !!host && (host.kind === 'rect' || host.kind === 'triangle');
  const { rx, ry } = resolveArcRadii(text, host, arcOffset);
  const bbox = arcTextBBox({ text: text.text, arcCx: text.arcCx, arcCy: text.arcCy, arcRadius: text.arcRadius, arcRx: rx, arcRy: ry, arcStartDeg: text.arcStartDeg, arcEndDeg: text.arcEndDeg, fontSize: text.fontSize });

  const arcHitPoints = useMemo(() => {
    if (onPerimeter) return [] as number[];
    const steps = 64; const start = (text.arcStartDeg * Math.PI) / 180; const end = (text.arcEndDeg * Math.PI) / 180; const out: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) { const a = start + (end - start) * (i / steps); out.push(text.arcCx + Math.cos(a) * rx, text.arcCy + Math.sin(a) * ry); }
    return out;
  }, [onPerimeter, text.arcCx, text.arcCy, rx, ry, text.arcStartDeg, text.arcEndDeg]);

  const handlers = { onClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); }, onTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); }, onDblClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }, onDblTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); } };

  const handleDragEnd = (e: any) => {
    const g = groupRef.current; if (!g) return; const dx = g.x(); const dy = g.y(); g.x(0); g.y(0);
    const nx = text.arcCx + dx; const ny = text.arcCy + dy;
    if (snapEnabled) {
      const stage = e.target.getStage(); const pos = stage?.getRelativePointerPosition?.();
      if (pos) {
        const target = findNearestShapeByCursor(pos.x, pos.y, shapes, 260);
        if (target) {
          const signed = target.isOutside ? -arcOffset : arcOffset;
          const [cpx, cpy] = closestPointOnHostPerimeter(target.host, signed, pos.x, pos.y);
          const hostCx = target.host.x + target.host.width / 2; const hostCy = target.host.y + target.host.height / 2;
          const angleRad = Math.atan2(cpy - hostCy, cpx - hostCx);
          const params = computeArcParamsForAngle(target.host, angleRad, text.fontSize, target.isOutside, spanDeg, arcOffset);
          onUpdate({ ...params, arcHostId: target.host.id, arcOutside: target.isOutside });
          return;
        }
      }
    }
    onUpdate({ arcCx: Math.round(nx), arcCy: Math.round(ny) });
  };

  let hitPoints: number[] = [];
  if (host) {
    if (host.kind === 'rect') hitPoints = [host.x, host.y, host.x + host.width, host.y, host.x + host.width, host.y + host.height, host.x, host.y + host.height];
    else if (host.kind === 'triangle') hitPoints = [host.x + host.width / 2, host.y, host.x + host.width, host.y + host.height, host.x, host.y + host.height];
  }

  return (
    <Group ref={groupRef} id={text.id} name="text-arc" x={0} y={0} draggable={draggable} onDragEnd={handleDragEnd} {...handlers}>
      {onPerimeter && host ? (
        <>
          <Group x={host.x} y={host.y} listening={false}>
            <Shape listening={false} sceneFunc={(ctx) => { const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D; drawPerimeterText(c, { host, arcOffset, isOutside, centerDeg, text: text.text, fontSize: text.fontSize, fontFamily: text.fontFamily, fontStyle: text.fontStyle, fill: text.fill }); }} />
          </Group>
          <Line points={hitPoints} closed stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" strokeWidth={1} hitStrokeWidth={Math.max(12, text.fontSize * 1.2)} lineJoin="round" lineCap="round" listening={true} />
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Shape listening={false} sceneFunc={(ctx) => { const c = (ctx as any)._context as CanvasRenderingContext2D; drawArcText(c, { text: text.text, cx: text.arcCx, cy: text.arcCy, radius: text.arcRadius, radiusX: rx, radiusY: ry, startDeg: text.arcStartDeg, endDeg: text.arcEndDeg, flip: text.arcFlip, font: canvasFont(text.fontFamily, text.fontStyle, text.fontSize), fill: text.fill }); }} />
          <Line points={arcHitPoints} stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" strokeWidth={1} hitStrokeWidth={Math.max(14, text.fontSize * 1.5)} lineCap="round" lineJoin="round" listening={true} />
          <Rect x={bbox.x + bbox.width / 2 - 8} y={bbox.y + bbox.height / 2 - 8} width={16} height={16} fill="rgba(0,0,0,0.001)" listening={true} />
        </>
      )}
    </Group>
  );
};

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Stage } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { StampBackgroundLayer } from './canvas/StampBackgroundLayer';
import { StampElementsLayer } from './canvas/StampElementsLayer';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Props {
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
  stageRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  trRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Transformer>;
  layerRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Layer>;
  wrapSize: { w: number; h: number };
  width: number;
  height: number;
  zoom: number;
  pan: { x: number; y: number };
  onWheel: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<WheelEvent>) => void;
  onStageMouseDown: () => void;
  showGrid: boolean;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  imageCache: Record<string, HTMLImageElement>;
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedType: SelectionType;
  selectedShape: StampShapeItem | null;
  snap: boolean;
  fillMode: boolean;
  onFillClick: (px: number, py: number) => void;
  onSelect: (id: string, type: Exclude<SelectionType, null>) => void;
  onShapeDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onTextDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, text: StampTextItem) => void;
  onImageDragEnd: (id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, image: StampImageItem) => void;
  onTransformEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<Event>) => void;
  onOpenTextEditor: (id: string) => void;
  onUpdateText: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
  onPointsChange?: (id: string, points: number[]) => void;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const StampCanvas: React.FC<Props> = ({
  wrapRef, stageRef, trRef, layerRef, wrapSize, width, height, zoom, pan,
  onWheel, onStageMouseDown, showGrid, shapes, texts, images, imageCache,
  selectedId, selectedType, selectedShape, snap, fillMode, onFillClick,
  onSelect, onShapeDragEnd, onTextDragEnd, onImageDragEnd, onTransformEnd,
  onOpenTextEditor, onUpdateText, onPointsChange, children,
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = wrapRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const prev = el.style.cursor;
    el.style.cursor = fillMode ? 'crosshair' : '';
    return () => { el.style.cursor = prev; };
  }, [fillMode, wrapRef]);

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-canvas" ref={wrapRef}>
      <Stage
        ref={stageRef as any}
        width={wrapSize.w} height={wrapSize.h}
        x={pan.x} y={pan.y} scaleX={zoom} scaleY={zoom}
        onWheel={onWheel}
        onMouseDown={(e) => {
          if (e.target !== e.target.getStage()) return;
          if (fillMode) {
            const pos = e.target.getStage()?.getRelativePointerPosition();
            if (pos) onFillClick(pos.x, pos.y);
            return;
          }
          onStageMouseDown();
        }}
      >
        <StampBackgroundLayer width={width} height={height} zoom={zoom} showGrid={showGrid} />
        <StampElementsLayer
          layerRef={layerRef} trRef={trRef} stampW={width} stampH={height}
          shapes={shapes} texts={texts} images={images} imageCache={imageCache}
          selectedId={selectedId} selectedType={selectedType} selectedShape={selectedShape}
          snap={snap} listening={!fillMode}
          onSelect={onSelect} onShapeDragEnd={onShapeDragEnd} onTextDragEnd={onTextDragEnd}
          onImageDragEnd={onImageDragEnd} onTransformEnd={onTransformEnd}
          onOpenTextEditor={onOpenTextEditor} onUpdateText={onUpdateText}
          onPointsChange={onPointsChange}
        />
      </Stage>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { StampHead } from './panels/StampHead';
import { StampToolbar } from './panels/StampToolbar';
import { StampInlineTextEditor } from './panels/StampInlineTextEditor';
import { StampCanvas } from './StampCanvas';
import { StampLayersPanel } from './StampLayersPanel';
import { ShapePropsPanel } from './ShapePropsPanel';
import { TextPropsPanel } from './TextPropsPanel';
import { StampImagePropsPanel } from './StampImagePropsPanel';

import { useStampHistory } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampHistory';
import { useStampUndoRedo } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampUndoRedo';
import { useStampZoomPan } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampZoomPan';
import { useStampShortcuts } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampShortcuts';
import { useStampCanvasWrap } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampCanvasWrap';
import { useStampImageCache } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampImageCache';

import { useStampDragHandlers } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampDragHandlers';

import { useStampTransform } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampTransform';
import { useStampLayerActions } from '../../hooks/stamps/useStampLayerActions';

import type { PlacementMode } from '../../../lib/stamps/geometry';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Props {
  initialWidth: number; initialHeight: number;
  initialShapes: StampShapeItem[]; initialTexts: StampTextItem[]; initialImages?: StampImageItem[];
  initialName?: string;
  onSave: (data: { name: string; width: number; height: number; shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; }) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

export const StampEditor: React.FC<Props> = ({ initialWidth, initialHeight, initialShapes, initialTexts, initialImages, initialName, onSave, onCancel }) => {
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialName || 'РњРѕР№ С€С‚Р°РјРї');
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(initialWidth);
  const [height, setHeight] = useState(initialHeight);
  const [placementMode, setPlacementMode] = useState<PlacementMode>('inside');
  const [showGrid, setShowGrid] = useState(true);
  const [snap, setSnap] = useState(true);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<SelectionType>(null);

  const select = useCallback((id: string | null, type: SelectionType) => { setSelectedId(id); setSelectedType(type); }, []);
  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => { setSelectedId(null); setSelectedType(null); }, []);

  const stageWrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stageRef = useRef<Konva.Stage>(null);
  const trRef = useRef<Konva.Transformer>(null);
  const layerRef = useRef<Konva.Layer>(null);

  const history = useStampHistory({ shapes: initialShapes, texts: initialTexts, images: initialImages || [] });
  const { shapes, texts, images } = history.state;

  const zp = useStampZoomPan(stageRef);
  const wrapSize = useStampCanvasWrap(stageWrapRef as React.RefObject<HTMLElement>);

  useEffect(() => { zp.zoomFit(wrapSize.w, wrapSize.h, width, height); }, [width, height, wrapSize.w, wrapSize.h]);

  const imageCache = useStampImageCache(images);

  const commit = useCallback((s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => history.push({ shapes: s, texts: t, images: i }), [history]);

  const updateShape = useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, ...patch } : s)), texts, images), [shapes, texts, images, commit]);
  const updateImage = useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => commit(shapes, texts, images.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, ...patch } : im))), [shapes, texts, images, commit]);
  const updateText = useStampTextUpdate({ shapes, texts, images, commit });

  const textEditor = useStampTextEditor({ shapes, texts, images, commit, replace: history.replace });
  const drag = useStampDragHandlers({ shapes, width, height, snapEnabled: snap, updateShape, updateText, updateImage });
  const handleTransform = useStampTransform({ shapes, images, selectedType, updateShape, updateImage });
  const layerActions = useStampLayerActions({ shapes, texts, images, selectedId, commit, clearSelection });
  const toolbar = useStampToolbarActions({ shapes, texts, images, width, height, placementMode, selectedId, selectedType, commit, select });
  const crud = useStampCRUD({ shapes, texts, images, selectedId, selectedType, commit, select, clearSelection });
  const uploadImage = useStampImageUpload({ shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select });
  const replaceImage = useStampImageReplace({ updateImage, stampW: width, stampH: height });
  const fill = useStampFillMode({ shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select });

  const withFillOff = useCallback(<A extends any[]>(fn: (...args: A) => void) => (...args: A) => { fill.setFillMode(false); fn(...args); }, [fill]);
  const addShape = useCallback(withFillOff(toolbar.addShape), [toolbar, withFillOff]);
  const addStraightText = useCallback(withFillOff(toolbar.addStraightText), [toolbar, withFillOff]);
  const addArcText = useCallback(withFillOff(toolbar.addArcText), [toolbar, withFillOff]);
  const addImage = useCallback(withFillOff(uploadImage), [uploadImage, withFillOff]);

  const { undo: handleUndo, redo: handleRedo } = useStampUndoRedo({ history, clearSelection });

  useStampShortcuts({ editingTextId: textEditor.editingTextId, selectedId, onCloseTextEditor: textEditor.closeTextEditor, onClearSelection: clearSelection, onDelete: crud.deleteSelected, onDuplicate: crud.duplicateSelected, onUndo: handleUndo, onRedo: handleRedo, onCloseEditor: onCancel });

  useEffect(() => {
    const tr = trRef.current; const layer = layerRef.current;
    if (!tr || !layer) return;
    const isFill = selectedType === 'shape' && shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId)?.kind === 'fill';
    if (!selectedId || isFill || (selectedType !== 'shape' && selectedType !== 'image')) { tr.nodes([]); layer.batchDraw(); return; }
    const node = layer.findOne('#' + selectedId);
    if (node) tr.nodes([node]); else tr.nodes([]);
    layer.batchDraw();
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, images]);

  const selectedShape = selectedType === 'shape' ? shapes.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) || null : null;
  const selectedText = selectedType === 'text' ? texts.find((t) => t.id === selectedId) || null : null;
  const selectedImage = selectedType === 'image' ? images.find((im) => im.id === selectedId) || null : null;
  const editingText = textEditor.editingTextId ? texts.find((t) => t.id === textEditor.editingTextId) || null : null;

  const handleSave = () => onSave({ name, width, height, shapes, texts, images });

  return (
    <div className="stamp-editor-backdrop" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
      <div className="stamp-editor-modal" onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <StampHead name={name} width={width} height={height} onChangeName={setName} onChangeWidth={setWidth} onChangeHeight={setHeight} onSave={handleSave} onCancel={onCancel} />
        <StampToolbar placementMode={placementMode} zoom={zp.zoom} minZoom={zp.MIN_ZOOM} maxZoom={zp.MAX_ZOOM} canUndo={history.canUndo} canRedo={history.canRedo} showGrid={showGrid} snap={snap} fillMode={fill.fillMode} fillColor={fill.fillColor} onChangePlacement={setPlacementMode} onAddShape={addShape} onAddStraightText={addStraightText} onAddArcText={addArcText} onAddImage={addImage} onToggleFillMode={() => fill.setFillMode((v) => !v)} onFillColorChange={fill.setFillColor} onUndo={handleUndo} onRedo={handleRedo} onZoomIn={zp.zoomIn} onZoomOut={zp.zoomOut} onZoomFit={() => zp.zoomFit(wrapSize.w, wrapSize.h, width, height)} onToggleGrid={setShowGrid} onToggleSnap={setSnap} />
        <div className="stamp-editor-body">
          <div className="stamp-editor-left">
            <StampLayersPanel shapes={shapes} texts={texts} images={images} selectedId={selectedId} selectedType={selectedType} onSelect={select} onToggleHidden={layerActions.toggleHidden} onToggleLocked={layerActions.toggleLocked} onDelete={layerActions.removeLayer} onReorder={layerActions.reorder} />
          </div>
          <StampCanvas wrapRef={stageWrapRef} stageRef={stageRef} trRef={trRef} layerRef={layerRef} wrapSize={wrapSize} width={width} height={height} zoom={zp.zoom} pan={zp.pan} onWheel={zp.onWheel} onStageMouseDown={() => { if (textEditor.editingTextId) textEditor.closeTextEditor(); clearSelection(); }} showGrid={showGrid} shapes={shapes} texts={texts} images={images} imageCache={imageCache} selectedId={selectedId} selectedType={selectedType} selectedShape={selectedShape} snap={snap} fillMode={fill.fillMode} onFillClick={fill.handleCanvasClick} onSelect={(id, type) => select(id, type)} onShapeDragEnd={drag.handleShapeDragEnd} onTextDragEnd={drag.handleTextDragEnd} onImageDragEnd={drag.handleImageDragEnd} onTransformEnd={handleTransform} onOpenTextEditor={textEditor.openTextEditor} onUpdateText={updateText} onPointsChange={(id, points) => updateShape(id, { points })}>
            {editingText && <StampInlineTextEditor text={editingText} zoom={zp.zoom} pan={zp.pan} onChange={textEditor.changeEditingText} onClose={textEditor.closeTextEditor} />}
            <div className="stamp-editor-info">{width}Г—{height} В· {Math.round(zp.zoom * 100)}% В· Р­Р»РµРјРµРЅС‚РѕРІ: {shapes.length + texts.length + images.length}</div>
          </StampCanvas>
          <div className="stamp-editor-right">
            {selectedShape && <ShapePropsPanel shape={selectedShape} allShapes={shapes} onChange={(p) => updateShape(selectedShape.id, p)} />}
            {selectedText && <TextPropsPanel text={selectedText} onChange={(p) => updateText(selectedText.id, p)} />}
            {selectedImage && <StampImagePropsPanel image={selectedImage} onChange={(p) => updateImage(selectedImage.id, p)} onReplace={() => replaceImage(selectedImage.id)} />}
            {!selectedShape && !selectedText && !selectedImage && <p className="stamp-editor-empty"><b>Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚</b>, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ.<br /><br /><b>Ctrl</b> РїСЂРё РїРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёРё/РјР°СЃС€С‚Р°Р±РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё вЂ” СЃРІРѕР±РѕРґРЅРѕ.<br /><b>Р”РІРѕР№РЅРѕР№ РєР»РёРє РїРѕ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Сѓ</b> вЂ” СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ.</p>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';

type LayerKind = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image';
interface LayerItem { id: string; type: LayerKind; name: string; icon: string; hidden?: boolean; locked?: boolean; }
interface Props {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images?: StampImageItem[];
  selectedId: string | null; selectedType: LayerKind | null;
  onSelect: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void; onToggleHidden: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void;
  onToggleLocked: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void; onDelete: (id: string, type: LayerKind) => void; onReorder: (id: string, type: LayerKind, dir: 1 | -1) => void;
}

export const StampLayersPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ shapes, texts, images = [], selectedId, selectedType, onSelect, onToggleHidden, onToggleLocked, onDelete, onReorder }) => {
  const items: LayerItem[] = [
    ...texts.map((t) => ({ id: t.id, type: 'text' as const, name: t.name || (t.mode === 'arc' ? 'РўРµРєСЃС‚ (РґСѓРіР°)' : 'РўРµРєСЃС‚'), icon: t.mode === 'arc' ? 'в—Ў' : 'T', hidden: t.hidden, locked: t.locked })),
    ...images.map((im) => ({ id: im.id, type: 'image' as const, name: im.name || 'РљР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєР°', icon: 'рџ–ј', hidden: im.hidden, locked: im.locked })),
    ...shapes.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, type: 'shape' as const, name: s.name || ({ ellipse: 'Р­Р»Р»РёРїСЃ', rect: 'РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє', triangle: 'РўСЂРµСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅРёРє', line: 'Р›РёРЅРёСЏ', circle: 'РљСЂСѓРі' } as any)[s.kind] || 'Р¤РёРіСѓСЂР°', icon: s.kind === 'ellipse' || s.kind === 'circle' ? 'в—Ї' : s.kind === 'rect' ? 'в–­' : s.kind === 'triangle' ? 'в–і' : 'в•±', hidden: s.hidden, locked: s.locked })),
  ];

  return (
    <div className="stamp-layers">
      <div className="stamp-layers-head">РЎР»РѕРё ({items.length})</div>
      <div className="stamp-layers-list">
        {items.length === 0 && <p className="stamp-editor-empty">РџСѓСЃС‚Рѕ. Р”РѕР±Р°РІСЊС‚Рµ С„РёРіСѓСЂС‹, С‚РµРєСЃС‚ РёР»Рё РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєСѓ.</p>}
        {items.map((item) => {
          const active = item.id === selectedId && item.type === selectedType;
          return (
            <div key={item.id} className={`stamp-layer ${active ? 'active' : ''} ${item.hidden ? 'hidden' : ''}`} onClick={() => onSelect(item.id, item.type)}>
              <span className="stamp-layer-icon">{item.icon}</span>
              <span className="stamp-layer-name">{item.name}</span>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title={item.hidden ? 'РџРѕРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ' : 'РЎРєСЂС‹С‚СЊ'} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleHidden(item.id, item.type); }}>{item.hidden ? 'в—Њ' : 'в—Џ'}</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title={item.locked ? 'Р Р°Р·Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ' : 'Р—Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ'} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onToggleLocked(item.id, item.type); }}>{item.locked ? 'рџ”’' : 'рџ”“'}</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title="Р’С‹С€Рµ" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReorder(item.id, item.type, 1); }}>в–І</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn" title="РќРёР¶Рµ" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onReorder(item.id, item.type, -1); }}>в–ј</button>
              <button className="stamp-layer-btn danger" title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onDelete(item.id, item.type); }}>Г—</button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

FILE: src\presentation\components\stamps\StampLibraryPanel.tsx
