import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { StampShape, StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import {
  getAllStamps, addCustomStamp, updateCustomStamp, removeCustomStamp, isBuiltinStamp, forkStampAsCustom, type StampTemplate,
} from '../../../lib/stamps/library';
import { newStampFromTemplate, newLegacyStamp } from '../../../lib/stamps/factories';
import { StampEditor } from './StampEditor';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../../providers/EditorProvider';

const LEGACY_QUICK: { shape: StampShape; label: string; icon: string }[] = [
  { shape: 'circle',   label: 'РљСЂСѓРіР»Р°СЏ',       icon: 'в¬¤' },
  { shape: 'oval',     label: 'РћРІР°Р»',          icon: 'в¬­' },
  { shape: 'rect',     label: 'РџСЂСЏРјРѕСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅР°СЏ', icon: 'в–­' },
  { shape: 'triangle', label: 'РўСЂРµСѓРіРѕР»СЊРЅР°СЏ',   icon: 'в–і' },
];

interface EditorState {
  mode: 'create' | 'edit'; sourceId?: string; sourceIsBuiltin?: boolean; customId?: string;
  width: number; height: number; shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; name?: string;
}

function toDragPayload(tpl: StampTemplate) {
  return JSON.stringify({ kind: 'stamp-template', template: { id: tpl.id, name: tpl.name, description: tpl.description, icon: tpl.icon, width: tpl.width, height: tpl.height, shapes: tpl.shapes, texts: tpl.texts, images: tpl.images || [] } });
}

export const StampLibraryPanel: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);
  const [editorState, setEditorState] = useState<EditorState | null>(null);

  const stamps = useMemo(() => getAllStamps(), [version]);
  const generalStamps = useMemo(() => stamps.filter((s) => !s.custom && !s.banking), [stamps]);
  const bankingStamps = useMemo(() => stamps.filter((s) => !s.custom && s.banking), [stamps]);
  const customStamps  = useMemo(() => stamps.filter((s) => s.custom), [stamps]);

  const addElement = (el: StampShapeItem | StampTextItem | StampImageItem | any) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.elements.push(el);
    }, 'add-stamp-element');
    selStore.select(el.id);
  };

  const applyStamp = (tpl: StampTemplate) => addElement(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));
  const addLegacy = (shape: StampShape) => addElement(newLegacyStamp(shape, 200, 200));

  const openNew = () => { setEditorState({ mode: 'create', width: 200, height: 200, shapes: [], texts: [], images: [], name: 'РњРѕР№ С€С‚Р°РјРї' }); };
  const openEdit = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    const builtin = isBuiltinStamp(tpl.id);
    setEditorState({ mode: 'edit', sourceId: tpl.id, sourceIsBuiltin: builtin, customId: builtin ? undefined : tpl.id, width: tpl.width, height: tpl.height, shapes: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.shapes)), texts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.texts)), images: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(tpl.images || [])), name: tpl.name });
  };

  const handleSave = (data: { name: string; width: number; height: number; shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; }) => {
    if (!editorState) return;
    if (editorState.mode === 'edit') {
      if (editorState.sourceIsBuiltin) {
        const source = stamps.find((s) => s.id === editorState.sourceId);
        if (source) addCustomStamp(forkStampAsCustom({ ...source, name: data.name, width: data.width, height: data.height, shapes: data.shapes, texts: data.texts, images: data.images }));
      } else if (editorState.customId) {
        updateCustomStamp(editorState.customId, { name: data.name, width: data.width, height: data.height, shapes: data.shapes, texts: data.texts, images: data.images });
      }
      setVersion((v) => v + 1); setEditorState(null); return;
    }
    const tpl: StampTemplate = { id: `custom-${Date.now()}`, name: data.name, icon: 'рџ–ј', width: data.width, height: data.height, shapes: data.shapes, texts: data.texts, images: data.images, custom: true };
    addCustomStamp(tpl); setVersion((v) => v + 1); setEditorState(null); addElement(newStampFromTemplate(tpl, 200, 200));
  };

  const deleteStamp = (tpl: StampTemplate) => {
    if (!tpl.custom) return;
    if (!window.confirm(`РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРї В«${tpl.name}В»?`)) return;
    removeCustomStamp(tpl.id); setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  };

  const renderItem = (s: StampTemplate, opts: { editable: boolean; deletable: boolean }) => (
    <div key={s.id} style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
      <button className="tool-tile" title={s.description || s.name} draggable onDragStart={(e) => { e.dataTransfer.setData('application/json', toDragPayload(s)); e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'copy'; }} onClick={() => applyStamp(s)} style={{ width: '100%' }}>
        <span className="tile-ico">{s.icon || 'рџ–ј'}</span><span>{s.name}</span>
      </button>
      {opts.editable && <button title="Р РµРґР°РєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); openEdit(s); }} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 2, right: 2, width: 18, height: 18, padding: 0, fontSize: 10, lineHeight: '16px', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 3, background: 'var(--panel)', color: 'var(--text)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>вњЋ</button>}
      {opts.deletable && <button title="РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); deleteStamp(s); }} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 2, right: 22, width: 18, height: 18, padding: 0, fontSize: 11, lineHeight: '16px', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 3, background: 'var(--panel)', color: 'var(--danger, #ef4444)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>Г—</button>}
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <>
      <label className="field-label">Р‘С‹СЃС‚СЂС‹Рµ (СѓСЃС‚Р°СЂРµРІС€РёРµ)</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">{LEGACY_QUICK.map((q) => <button key={q.shape} className="tool-tile" onClick={() => addLegacy(q.shape)}><span className="tile-ico">{q.icon}</span><span>{q.label}</span></button>)}</div>
      <label className="field-label" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Р’СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">{generalStamps.map((s) => renderItem(s, { editable: true, deletable: false }))}</div>
      <label className="field-label" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Р‘Р°РЅРєРѕРІСЃРєРёРµ</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">{bankingStamps.map((s) => renderItem(s, { editable: true, deletable: false }))}</div>
      <label className="field-label" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>РњРѕРё С€С‚Р°РјРїС‹</label>
      <div className="tool-grid">
        {customStamps.map((s) => renderItem(s, { editable: true, deletable: true }))}
        {customStamps.length === 0 && <p className="hint-sm" style={{ gridColumn: '1 / -1', margin: 0 }}>РџРѕРєР° РїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ.</p>}
      </div>
      <button className="tool-btn" style={{ marginTop: 8 }} onClick={openNew}>+ РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ С€С‚Р°РјРївЂ¦</button>
      {editorState && <StampEditor initialWidth={editorState.width} initialHeight={editorState.height} initialShapes={editorState.shapes} initialTexts={editorState.texts} initialImages={editorState.images} initialName={editorState.name} onSave={handleSave} onCancel={() => setEditorState(null)} />}
    </>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { getShapeView } from './shapes';

interface Props {
  shape: StampShapeItem; allShapes?: StampShapeItem[]; draggable: boolean; isSelected?: boolean;
  onSelect: () => void; onDragEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void;
  onPointsChange?: (points: number[]) => void;
}

export const StampShapeView: React.FC<Props> = ({ shape, allShapes = [], draggable, isSelected, onSelect, onDragEnd, onPointsChange }) => {
  const strategy = getShapeView(shape.kind); const View = strategy.View;
  return <View shape={shape} allShapes={allShapes} draggable={draggable} isSelected={isSelected} onSelect={onSelect} onDragEnd={onDragEnd} onPointsChange={onPointsChange} />;
};

import React from 'react';
import { Group, Text } from 'react-konva';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampTextItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { konvaStyle } from '../../../lib/stamps/render';

interface Props { text: StampTextItem; draggable: boolean; onSelect: () => void; onDblClick: () => void; onDragEnd: (e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => void; }

export const StampStraightTextView: React.FC<Props> = ({ text, draggable, onSelect, onDblClick, onDragEnd }) => {
  const handlers = { onClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); }, onTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onSelect(); }, onDblClick: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); }, onDblTap: (e: any) => { e.cancelBubble = true; onDblClick(); } };
  return (
    <Group id={text.id} name="text" x={text.x} y={text.y} draggable={draggable} onDragEnd={onDragEnd} {...handlers}>
      <Text x={0} y={0} width={text.width} height={text.fontSize * 1.4} text={text.text} fontSize={text.fontSize} fontFamily={text.fontFamily} fontStyle={konvaStyle(text.fontStyle)} textDecoration={text.textDecoration} fill={text.fill} align={text.align} listening={true} hitStrokeWidth={0} />
    </Group>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { StampTextItem, TextAlign } from '../../../shared/types';
import { MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG } from './stampSnap';

interface Props { text: StampTextItem; onChange: (p: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void; }

export const TextPropsPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({ text, onChange }) => {
  const hasBold = (text.fontStyle || '').includes('bold'); const hasItalic = (text.fontStyle || '').includes('italic'); const hasUnderline = (text.textDecoration || '').includes('underline');
  const toggle = (flag: 'bold' | 'italic') => { const parts = (text.fontStyle || '').split(' ').filter(Boolean); const next = parts.includes(flag) ? parts.filter((p) => p !== flag) : [...parts, flag]; onChange({ fontStyle: next.join(' ') || 'normal' }); };
  const rx = text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius; const ry = text.arcRy ?? text.arcRadius;
  return (
    <>
      <h4>РўРµРєСЃС‚</h4>

      <label>РЎРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРµ <textarea value={text.text} onChange={(e) => onChange({ text: e.target.value })} /></label>
      <label>Р РµР¶РёРј
        <select value={text.mode} onChange={(e) => onChange({ mode: e.target.value as any })}>
          <option value="straight">РџСЂСЏРјРѕР№</option><option value="arc">РџРѕ РґСѓРіРµ</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      {text.mode === 'straight' ? (
        <>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}><label>X <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.x)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ x: +e.target.value })} /></label><label>Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.y)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ y: +e.target.value })} /></label></div>
          <label>РЁРёСЂРёРЅР° <input type="number" min="10" value={Math.round(text.width)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ width: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label>Р’С‹СЂР°РІРЅРёРІР°РЅРёРµ <select value={text.align} onChange={(e) => onChange({ align: e.target.value as TextAlign })}><option value="left">Р’Р»РµРІРѕ</option><option value="center">РџРѕ С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ</option><option value="right">Р’РїСЂР°РІРѕ</option></select></label>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <h4>Р”СѓРіР°</h4>
          <label>РЈРіРѕР»: {Math.round(text.arcSpanDeg ?? 320)}В°<input type="range" min={MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG} max={MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG} value={text.arcSpanDeg ?? 320} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcSpanDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>

          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>

            <label>Р Р°РґРёСѓСЃ Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(ry)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcRy: +e.target.value, arcRx: text.arcRx ?? text.arcRadius })} /></label>
          </div>
          <label className="check" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}><input type="checkbox" checked={!!text.arcOutside} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcOutside: e.target.checked })} /> РЎРЅР°СЂСѓР¶Рё</label>
          <h4>Р¦РµРЅС‚СЂ РґСѓРіРё</h4>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
            <label>X <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.arcCx)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcCx: +e.target.value })} /></label>
            <label>Y <input type="number" value={Math.round(text.arcCy)} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcCy: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
            <label>РќР°С‡Р°Р»РѕВ° <input type="number" value={text.arcStartDeg} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcStartDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
            <label>РљРѕРЅРµС†В° <input type="number" value={text.arcEndDeg} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcEndDeg: +e.target.value })} /></label>
          </div>
          <label className="check" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}><input type="checkbox" checked={!!text.arcFlip} onChange={(e) => onChange({ arcFlip: e.target.checked })} /> РџРµСЂРµРІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ</label>
        </>
      )}
      <h4>РЎС‚РёР»СЊ</h4>
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: 6 }}>
        <label>Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ <input type="number" min="6" value={text.fontSize} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontSize: +e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label>РЁСЂРёС„С‚ <select value={text.fontFamily} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontFamily: e.target.value })}><option value="Arial">Arial</option><option value="Times New Roman">Times New Roman</option><option value="Courier New">Courier New</option><option value="Georgia">Georgia</option><option value="Verdana">Verdana</option></select></label>
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginTop: 6 }}>
        <button className={hasBold ? 'active' : ''} style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => toggle('bold')}><b>B</b></button>
        <button className={hasItalic ? 'active' : ''} style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => toggle('italic')}><i>I</i></button>

      </div>
      <label>Р¦РІРµС‚ <input type="color" value={text.fill} onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })} /></label>
    </>

// src/presentation/components/TextToolbar.tsx
import React from 'react';
import type { TextAlign } from '../../shared/types';

/**
 * РўРёРїРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїР°С‚С‡ СЃС‚РёР»РµР№, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РїСЂРёРЅРёРјР°РµС‚ TextToolbar.
 * РќРµ `any` вЂ” РєРѕРЅРєСЂРµС‚РЅС‹Р№ РёРЅС‚РµСЂС„РµР№СЃ (CQ-TS-04).
 */
export interface TextStylePatch {
  fontSize?: number;
  fontFamily?: string;
  fontStyle?: string;
  textDecoration?: string;
  fill?: string;
  align?: TextAlign;
}

interface Props {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  fontSize: number;
  fontFamily: string;
  fontStyle: string;
  textDecoration: string;
  fill: string;
  align: TextAlign;
  onChange: (patch: TextStylePatch) => void;
  onCommit: () => void;
}

const FONTS = [
  'Arial',
  'Times New Roman',
  'Courier New',
  'Georgia',
  'Verdana',
  'Comic Sans MS',
];

const SIZES = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 24, 28, 32, 36, 48, 64, 72];

export const TextToolbar: React.FC<Props> = ({
  x,
  y,
  fontSize,
  fontFamily,
  fontStyle,
  textDecoration,
  fill,
  align,
  onChange,
  onCommit,
}) => {
  const isBold = fontStyle.includes('bold');
  const isItalic = fontStyle.includes('italic');
  const isUnderline = textDecoration.includes('underline');

  const toggleStyle = (flag: 'bold' | 'italic') => {
    const parts = fontStyle.split(' ').filter(Boolean);
    const idx = parts.indexOf(flag);
    if (idx >= 0) parts.splice(idx, 1);
    else parts.push(flag);
    onChange({ fontStyle: parts.join(' ') || 'normal' });
  };

  const toggleUnderline = () => {
    onChange({ textDecoration: isUnderline ? '' : 'underline' });
  };

  const keepFocus = (e: React.MouseEvent) => e.preventDefault();
  const stopBubble = (e: React.MouseEvent) => e.stopPropagation();

  return (
    <div
      className="text-toolbar"
      style={{ left: x, top: y }}
      onMouseDown={stopBubble}
    >
      <select
        value={fontFamily}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontFamily: e.target.value })}
        className="tt-select"
        title="РЁСЂРёС„С‚"
      >
        {FONTS.map((f) => (
          <option key={f} value={f}>{f}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <select
        value={Math.round(fontSize)}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fontSize: +e.target.value })}
        className="tt-select tt-size"
        title="Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂ"
      >
        {SIZES.map((s) => (
          <option key={s} value={s}>{s}</option>
        ))}
      </select>

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isBold ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => toggleStyle('bold')}
          title="Р–РёСЂРЅС‹Р№"
        >
          <b>B</b>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isItalic ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => toggleStyle('italic')}
          title="РљСѓСЂСЃРёРІ"
        >
          <i>I</i>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={isUnderline ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={toggleUnderline}
          title="РџРѕРґС‡С‘СЂРєРЅСѓС‚С‹Р№"
        >
          <u>U</u>
        </button>
      </div>

      <input
        type="color"
        value={fill}
        onChange={(e) => onChange({ fill: e.target.value })}
        className="tt-color"
        title="Р¦РІРµС‚"
      />

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'left' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'left' })}
          title="Р’Р»РµРІРѕ"
        >в—§</button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'center' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'center' })}
          title="РџРѕ С†РµРЅС‚СЂСѓ"
        >в—«</button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          className={align === 'right' ? 'active' : ''}
          onClick={() => onChange({ align: 'right' })}
          title="Р’РїСЂР°РІРѕ"
        >в—Ё</button>
      </div>

      <div className="tt-group">
        <button
          type="button"
          onMouseDown={keepFocus}
          onClick={onCommit}
          title="Р“РѕС‚РѕРІРѕ (Ctrl+Enter)"
          className="tt-done"
        >вњ“</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';
import { download } from '../../lib/download';
import { serializeSlideToSvg } from '../../lib/svg/serialize';

export const Toolbar: React.FC = () => {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const data = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.data);
  // РўСЂРёРіРіРµСЂ РїРµСЂРµСЂРёСЃРѕРІРєРё РїСЂРё РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё:
  useStore(docStore, (s) => s.historyVersion);

  const canUndo = docStore.canUndo;
  const canRedo = docStore.canRedo;

  const activeSlide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);

  const handleExportJson = () => {
    const payload = { version: '2.0', exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), slides, data };
    download(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`, 'application/json');
  };

  const handleExportSvg = () => {
    if (!activeSlide) return;
    const svgString = serializeSlideToSvg(activeSlide);
    download(svgString, `slide-${activeSlide.id}.svg`, 'image/svg+xml');
  };

  return (
    <header className="toolbar">
      <h1>Р РµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅРѕРІ</h1>
      <div className="toolbar-actions">
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.undo()} disabled={!canUndo}>в†¶ РћС‚РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ</button>
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={() => docStore.redo()} disabled={!canRedo}>в†· РџРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРёС‚СЊ</button>
        <div style={{ width: 16 }} />
        <button className="toolbar-btn" onClick={handleExportSvg}>рџ–ј Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ SVG</button>
        <button className="toolbar-btn primary" onClick={handleExportJson}>рџ“Ґ Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ JSON</button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

  selectedCount?: number;

  fillMode?: boolean;
}

export const ZoomBar: React.FC<Props> = ({ zoom, onChange, selectedCount, fillMode }) => (
  <div className="zoom-bar">
    <button onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(0.2, +(zoom - 0.1).toFixed(2)))}>в€’</button>
    <span>{Math.round(zoom * 100)}%</span>
    <button onClick={() => onChange(Math.min(3, +(zoom + 0.1).toFixed(2)))}>+</button>

      <span className="style-badge">Р’С‹Р±СЂР°РЅРѕ: {selectedCount}</span>

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasConnectorDraft.ts
SIZE: 3933 bytes

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { Slide, AnchorSide, ConnectorElement, CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import {
  findNearestAnchor, computeGhostRect, cloneForConnector,
  oppositeAnchor, type Rect
} from '../../../lib/connectors';
import { generateId } from '../../../shared/utils/id';

const ANCHOR_HIT_RADIUS = 30;

interface Draft {
  fromId: string;
  fromSide: AnchorSide;
  toX: number;
  toY: number;
}

export function useCanvasConnectorDraft(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, slide } = params;
  const [draft, setDraftState] = useState<Draft | null>(null);
  const [ghostRect, setGhostRect] = useState<Rect | null>(null);
  const draftRef = useRef<Draft | null>(null);

  const setDraft = useCallback((d: Draft | null) => {
    draftRef.current = d;
    setDraftState(d);
    if (!d) setGhostRect(null);
  }, []);

  const startDraft = useCallback((fromId: string, fromSide: AnchorSide, wx: number, wy: number) => {
    setDraft({ fromId, fromSide, toX: wx, toY: wy });
    const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === fromId);
    if (source) {
      const rect = computeGhostRect(source, fromSide, wx, wy);
      setGhostRect(rect);
    }
  }, [setDraft, slide]);

  const update = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const d = draftRef.current;
    if (!d) return;

    const targetEl = slide.elements.find(
      (el) => el.id !== d.fromId && el.type !== 'connector' && !el.locked &&
      wx >= el.x && wx <= el.x + el.width && wy >= el.y && wy <= el.y + el.height
    );

    if (!targetEl) {
      const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === d.fromId);
      if (source) {
        const rect = computeGhostRect(source, d.fromSide, wx, wy);
        setGhostRect(rect);
      }
    } else {
      setGhostRect(null);
    }

    setDraft({ ...d, toX: wx, toY: wy });
  }, [slide]);

  const commit = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const d = draftRef.current;
    if (!d) return;

    const source = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === d.fromId);
    if (!source) {
      setDraft(null);
      return;
    }

    const nearest = slide.elements
      .filter((el) => el.id !== d.fromId && el.type !== 'connector' && !el.locked)
      .map((el) => ({ el, anchor: findNearestAnchor(el, wx, wy) }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.anchor.distance - b.anchor.distance)[0];

    docStore.commit((draftState) => {
      const s = draftState.slides.find((x) => x.id === draftState.activeId);
      if (!s) return;

      let toId = '';
      let toAnchor: AnchorSide = 'left';

      if (nearest && nearest.anchor.distance <= ANCHOR_HIT_RADIUS) {
        toId = nearest.el.id;
        toAnchor = nearest.anchor.side;
      } else if (ghostRect) {
        const newEl = cloneForConnector(ghostRect, source);
        newEl.id = generateId();
        toId = newEl.id;
        toAnchor = oppositeAnchor(d.fromSide);
        s.elements.push(newEl as CanvasElement);
      } else {
        setDraft(null);
        return;
      }

      const conn: ConnectorElement = {
        id: generateId(), type: 'connector',
        x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, rotation: 0,
        fromId: d.fromId, fromAnchor: d.fromSide,
        toId: toId, toAnchor: toAnchor,
        stroke: '#374151', strokeWidth: 2, dash: 'solid',
        isArrow: true, routing: 'orthogonal',
      };
      s.elements.push(conn);
    });
    setDraft(null);
  }, [docStore, slide, ghostRect, setDraft]);

  const cancel = useCallback(() => setDraft(null), [setDraft]);

  return {
    startDraft, update, commit, cancel,
    current: () => draft,
    isDrafting: () => draftRef.current !== null,
    getGhostRect: () => ghostRect,
  };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasDblClick.ts

// src/presentation/hooks/canvas/useCanvasDblClick.ts
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { findTableCellAt } from '../../../domain/slide/hitTest';

export interface DblClickViewport {
  viewportLeft: number;
  viewportTop: number;
  clientX: number;
  clientY: number;
}

export function useCanvasDblClick(params: {
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  zoom: number;
}) {
  const { modalsStore, zoom } = params;

  const handle = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, vp: DblClickViewport) => {
    const screenX = vp.viewportLeft + el.x * zoom;
    const screenY = vp.viewportTop + el.y * zoom;
    const screenW = el.width * zoom;
    const screenH = el.height * zoom;

    /* ---------- text / footer ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'text' || el.type === 'footer') {
      const isRich =
        el.type === 'footer'
          ? true
          : (el.type === 'text' ? el.richText !== false : false);

      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id,
        field: el.type === 'footer' ? 'footerText' : 'text',
        kind: el.type,
        value: el.text ?? '',
        html: 'html' in el ? el.html : undefined,
        x: screenX, y: screenY,
        width: screenW, height: screenH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize ?? 16,
        fontFamily: el.type === 'text' ? el.fontFamily : 'Arial',
        fontStyle: el.type === 'text' ? el.fontStyle : 'normal',
        textDecoration: el.type === 'text' ? el.textDecoration : '',
        fill: el.fill ?? '#000',
        align: el.type === 'text' ? el.align : 'center',
        verticalAlign: el.type === 'text' ? el.verticalAlign : 'top',
        lineHeight: el.type === 'text' ? el.lineHeight : 1.5,
        padding: el.type === 'text'
          ? el.padding
          : { top: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 },
        background: el.type === 'text' ? el.background : 'transparent',
        isRich,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- shape: inline plain-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'shape') {
      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id, field: 'shapeText', kind: 'shape',
        value: el.text ?? '',
        x: screenX, y: screenY,
        width: screenW, height: screenH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize ?? 16,
        fontFamily: el.textFontFamily ?? 'Arial',
        fontStyle: el.textFontStyle ?? 'normal',
        textDecoration: el.textDecoration ?? '',
        fill: el.textFill ?? '#000',
        align: el.textAlign ?? 'center',
        verticalAlign: el.textVerticalAlign ?? 'middle',
        lineHeight: el.textLineHeight ?? 1.3,
        padding: el.textPadding ?? { top: 8, right: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8 },
        background: 'transparent',
        isRich: false,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- table: inline plain-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ СЏС‡РµР№РєРё ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'table') {
      const worldX = (vp.clientX - vp.viewportLeft) / zoom;
      const worldY = (vp.clientY - vp.viewportTop) / zoom;
      const cell = findTableCellAt(el, worldX, worldY);
      if (!cell) return;
      const cellW = (el.width * zoom) / el.cols;
      const cellH = (el.height * zoom) / el.rows;
      modalsStore.openEditor({
        id: el.id, field: 'tableCell', kind: 'table',
        tableRow: cell.row, tableCol: cell.col,
        value: el.data[cell.row]?.[cell.col] ?? '',
        x: screenX + cell.col * cellW,
        y: screenY + cell.row * cellH,
        width: cellW, height: cellH,
        fontSize: el.fontSize,
        fontFamily: 'Arial', fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
        fill: '#111', align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
        padding: { top: 6, right: 6, bottom: 6, left: 6 },
        background: '#fff', isRich: false,
      });
      return;
    }

    /* ---------- stamp / diagram ---------- */
    if (el.type === 'stamp') {
      modalsStore.openStampEditor(el.id);
      return;
    }
    if (el.type === 'diagram') {
      modalsStore.openDiagramEditor(el.id);
    }
  }, [modalsStore, zoom]);

  return { handle };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasDrag.ts

import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { computeSnap } from '../../../services/snap';
import { guidesStore } from '../../stores/GuidesStore';
import { dragLiveStore } from '../../stores/DragLiveStore';
import { SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '../../../shared/constants';

interface DragState {
  id: string;
  /** РСЃС…РѕРґРЅР°СЏ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РёР· state вЂ” РєРѕРјРїРµРЅСЃРёСЂСѓРµС‚ dragDistance (3px) Konva. */
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
  orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>;
  layer: Konva.Layer | null;
  pendingX: number;
  pendingY: number;
  rafId: number | null;
  lastEvt: MouseEvent | undefined;
  /** РўРµРєСѓС‰РёРµ В«РїСЂРёР»РёРїР°РЅРёСЏВ» СЃ РіРёСЃС‚РµСЂРµР·РёСЃРѕРј. */
  snappedX: number | null;
  snappedY: number | null;
  snappedXGuide: number | null;
  snappedYGuide: number | null;
}

const MIN_MOVE_PX = 1;
const HYSTERESIS_MULT = 2;

export function useCanvasDrag(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  selStore: SelectionStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, selStore, modalsStore, slide } = params;
  const state = useRef<DragState | null>(null);

  const slideRef = useRef(slide);
  slideRef.current = slide;

  const onDragStart = useCallback((el: CanvasElement, e: any) => {
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);

    const current = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
    const ids = current.includes(el.id) ? current : [el.id];
    if (!current.includes(el.id)) selStore.setIds([el.id]);

    const orig: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
    for (const ee of slideRef.current.elements) {
      if (!ids.includes(ee.id)) continue;
      if (ee.type === 'connector') continue;
      orig[ee.id] = { x: ee.x, y: ee.y };
    }

    // startX Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РёР· state (orig), Р° РЅРµ РёР· e.target.x() вЂ” Konva РЅР° dragstart
    // СѓР¶Рµ СЃРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚ РЅР° dragDistance (3px), С‡С‚Рѕ РґР°С‘С‚ РѕС‚СЃС‚Р°РІР°РЅРёРµ.
    const origEl = orig[el.id];
    const startX = origEl ? origEl.x : e.target.x();
    const startY = origEl ? origEl.y : e.target.y();
    if (!isFinite(startX) || !isFinite(startY)) return;

    const layer = (e.target.getLayer?.() as Konva.Layer | null) ?? null;

    state.current = {
      id: el.id,
      startX,
      startY,
      orig,
      layer,
      pendingX: startX,
      pendingY: startY,
      rafId: null,
      lastEvt: e.evt,
      snappedX: null,
      snappedY: null,
      snappedXGuide: null,
      snappedYGuide: null,
    };
  }, [selStore, modalsStore]);

  const flush = useCallback(() => {
    const curr = state.current;
    if (!curr) return;
    curr.rafId = null;

    const rawX = curr.pendingX;
    const rawY = curr.pendingY;
    if (!isFinite(rawX) || !isFinite(rawY)) return;

    const isCtrl = !!(curr.lastEvt?.ctrlKey || curr.lastEvt?.metaKey);
    const ids = Object.keys(curr.orig);
    const isMulti = ids.length > 1;

    let finalX = rawX;
    let finalY = rawY;
    const guidesV: number[] = [];
    const guidesH: number[] = [];

    if (!isCtrl) {
      // Р”Р»СЏ РіСЂСѓРїРїС‹ вЂ” snap РїРѕ bbox, РґР»СЏ РѕРґРёРЅРѕС‡РЅРѕРіРѕ вЂ” РїРѕ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚Сѓ
      let snapEl: { id: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } | null = null;
      if (isMulti) {
        const minX = Math.min(...ids.map(id => curr.orig[id].x));
        const minY = Math.min(...ids.map(id => curr.orig[id].y));
        const maxX = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return curr.orig[id].x + (el?.width || 0);
        }));
        const maxY = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return curr.orig[id].y + (el?.height || 0);
        }));
        snapEl = { id: 'group', x: minX, y: minY, width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY };
      } else {
        const activeEl = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === curr.id);
        if (activeEl) snapEl = activeEl;
      }

      if (snapEl) {
        const snap = computeSnap(snapEl, rawX, rawY, slideRef.current, ids);

        // X СЃ РіРёСЃС‚РµСЂРµР·РёСЃРѕРј: РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј snap РµСЃР»Рё РІ Р·РѕРЅРµ; РµСЃР»Рё Р±С‹Р» snapped вЂ”
        // РґРµСЂР¶РёРј РїРѕРєР° РЅРµ РІС‹Р№РґРµРј Р·Р° thresholdГ—2 (Р±РµР· РіРёСЃС‚РµСЂРµР·РёСЃР° вЂ” РґСЂРѕР¶Р°РЅРёРµ).
        if (snap.x !== rawX) {
          finalX = snap.x;
          curr.snappedX = snap.x;
          const nearGuides = snap.v;
          curr.snappedXGuide = nearGuides.length ? nearGuides[0] : null;
        } else if (curr.snappedX !== null) {
          if (Math.abs(rawX - curr.snappedX) < SNAP_THRESHOLD * HYSTERESIS_MULT) {
            finalX = curr.snappedX;
          } else {
            curr.snappedX = null;
            curr.snappedXGuide = null;
          }
        }

        // Y Р°РЅР°Р»РѕРіРёС‡РЅРѕ
        if (snap.y !== rawY) {
          finalY = snap.y;
          curr.snappedY = snap.y;
          curr.snappedYGuide = snap.h.length ? snap.h[0] : null;
        } else if (curr.snappedY !== null) {
          if (Math.abs(rawY - curr.snappedY) < SNAP_THRESHOLD * HYSTERESIS_MULT) {
            finalY = curr.snappedY;
          } else {
            curr.snappedY = null;
            curr.snappedYGuide = null;
          }
        }

        // Guides: РІСЃРµ РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹ РІ Р·РѕРЅРµ threshold (В«С‚СЂРё Р»РёРЅРёРёВ»)
        for (const g of snap.v) {
          if (!guidesV.includes(g)) guidesV.push(g);
        }
        for (const g of snap.h) {
          if (!guidesH.includes(g)) guidesH.push(g);
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Ctrl/Cmd вЂ” snap off, РіРёСЃС‚РµСЂРµР·РёСЃ СЃР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµРј
      curr.snappedX = null;
      curr.snappedY = null;
      curr.snappedXGuide = null;
      curr.snappedYGuide = null;
    }

    guidesStore.set(guidesV, guidesH);

    const dx = finalX - curr.startX;
    const dy = finalY - curr.startY;

    // Р”РІРёРіР°РµРј Konva-РЅРѕРґС‹ РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ (РЅРµС‚ React-rerender, РЅРµС‚ РґСЂРѕР¶Р°РЅРёСЏ)
    if (curr.layer) {
      for (const [id, o] of Object.entries(curr.orig)) {
        const node = curr.layer.findOne('#' + id);
        if (node) {
          node.x(o.x + dx);
          node.y(o.y + dy);
        }
      }
      curr.layer.batchDraw();
    }

    // Live-РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РґР»СЏ РєРѕРЅРЅРµРєС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ Рё bbox-СЂР°РјРєРё РіСЂСѓРїРїС‹ вЂ” С‚РѕР¶Рµ snapped.
    const live: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
    for (const [id, o] of Object.entries(curr.orig)) {
      live[id] = { x: o.x + dx, y: o.y + dy };
    }
    dragLiveStore.set(live);
  }, []);

  const onDragMove = useCallback((e: any) => {
    const curr = state.current;
    if (!curr) return;
    curr.lastEvt = e.evt;
    curr.pendingX = e.target.x();
    curr.pendingY = e.target.y();
    if (curr.rafId !== null) return;
    curr.rafId = requestAnimationFrame(flush);
  }, [flush]);

  const onDragEnd = useCallback((e: any) => {
    const s = state.current;
    if (!s) return;
    if (s.rafId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(s.rafId);
    state.current = null;
    guidesStore.clear();
    dragLiveStore.clear();

    const rawX = e.target.x();
    const rawY = e.target.y();
    if (!isFinite(rawX) || !isFinite(rawY)) return;

    const isCtrl = !!(e.evt?.ctrlKey || e.evt?.metaKey);
    const ids = Object.keys(s.orig);
    const isMulti = ids.length > 1;

    // РќР° dragEnd вЂ” С„РёРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ snap (СЃС‚СЂР°С…РѕРІРєР° РґР»СЏ Р±С‹СЃС‚СЂРѕРіРѕ drag, РєРѕРіРґР°
    // Р¶РёРІРѕР№ snap РЅРµ СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°Р» РёР·-Р·Р° РіРёСЃС‚РµСЂРµР·РёСЃР°).
    let endX = rawX;
    let endY = rawY;

    if (!isCtrl) {
      let snapEl: { id: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } | null = null;
      if (isMulti) {
        const minX = Math.min(...ids.map(id => s.orig[id].x));
        const minY = Math.min(...ids.map(id => s.orig[id].y));
        const maxX = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return s.orig[id].x + (el?.width || 0);
        }));
        const maxY = Math.max(...ids.map(id => {
          const el = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === id);
          return s.orig[id].y + (el?.height || 0);
        }));
        snapEl = { id: 'group', x: minX, y: minY, width: maxX - minX, height: maxY - minY };
      } else {
        const activeEl = slideRef.current.elements.find(e => e.id === s.id);
        if (activeEl) snapEl = activeEl;
      }

      if (snapEl) {
        const snap = computeSnap(snapEl, rawX, rawY, slideRef.current, ids);
        endX = snap.x;
        endY = snap.y;
      }
    }

    const dx = endX - s.startX;
    const dy = endY - s.startY;

    if (Math.abs(dx) < MIN_MOVE_PX && Math.abs(dy) < MIN_MOVE_PX) return;

    const orig = s.orig;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const sl = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!sl) return;
      for (const ee of sl.elements) {
        if (ee.type === 'connector') continue;
        const o = orig[ee.id];
        if (!o) continue;
        ee.x = o.x + dx;
        ee.y = o.y + dy;
      }
    }, 'move-group');
  }, [docStore]);

  return { onDragStart, onDragMove, onDragEnd };
}

// src/presentation/hooks/canvas/useCanvasDrop.ts вЂ” РїР°С‚С‡ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РЅРѕРІР°СЏ РІРµС‚РєР°)
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { RefObject } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import {
  makeArrayTable, makePlaceholder,
  newText, newSimpleText, newFooter, newTable, newLine, newShape,
} from '../../../lib/factories';
import { resolveSample } from '../../../lib/resolveSample';
import { newStampFromTemplate } from '../../../lib/stamps/factories';
import { getAllStamps } from '../../../lib/stamps/library';

export function useCanvasDrop(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  stageRef: RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  zoom: number;
}) {
  const { docStore, stageRef, zoom } = params;

  const onDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
  }, []);

  const onDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const raw = e.dataTransfer.getData('application/json');
    if (!raw) return;
    let payload: any;
    try { payload = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return; }

    const rect = stage.container().getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / zoom;
    const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / zoom;

    /* ---- array ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'array') {
      const tbl = makeArrayTable(x, y, payload);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(tbl);
      }, 'drop-array');
      return;
    }

    /* ---- field ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'field') {
      const data = docStore.getState().data;
      const sample = resolveSample(data, payload.path) ?? payload.sample ?? '';
      const ph = makePlaceholder(x, y, payload.path, payload.fieldType, sample);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(ph);
      }, 'drop-field');
      return;
    }

    /* ---- stamp-template ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'stamp-template') {
      const tpl = getAllStamps().find((t) => t.id === payload.templateId);
      if (!tpl) return;
      const stamp = newStampFromTemplate(tpl, x, y);
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
        if (s) s.elements.push(stamp);
      }, 'drop-stamp');
      return;
    }

    /* ---- new-element (Р¤Р°Р·Р° 1) ---- */
    if (payload.kind === 'new-element') {
      const cx = Math.max(0, x - 100);
      const cy = Math.max(0, y - 60);
      let el: any = null;
      switch (payload.elementType) {
        case 'text': el = newText(cx, cy); break;
        case 'simple-text': el = newSimpleText(cx, cy); break;
        case 'footer': {
          const state = docStore.getState();
          const active = state.slides.find((s) => s.id === state.activeId);
          if (!active) return;
          const f = newFooter(active);
          const minY = active.padding.top;
          const maxY = active.height - active.padding.bottom - f.height;
          f.y = Math.min(maxY, Math.max(minY, y));
          el = f;
          break;
        }
        case 'table': el = newTable(cx, cy); break;
        case 'line': el = newLine(cx, cy); break;
        case 'line-arrow': {
          const l = newLine(cx, cy); l.isArrow = true; el = l; break;
        }
        case 'rect': el = newShape('rect', cx, cy); break;
        case 'roundRect': el = newShape('roundRect', cx, cy); break;
        case 'circle': el = newShape('circle', cx, cy); break;
        case 'triangle': el = newShape('triangle', cx, cy); break;
        case 'star': el = newShape('star', cx, cy); break;
        default: return;
      }
      if (el) {
        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((xx) => xx.id === draft.activeId);
          if (s) s.elements.push(el);
        }, 'drop-new-element');
      }
    }
  }, [docStore, stageRef, zoom]);

  return { onDragOver, onDrop };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { Slide, CanvasElement, FillElement, ShapeElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../../shared/utils/id';

export function useCanvasFillMode(params: {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
  slide: Slide;
}) {
  const { docStore, modalsStore, slide } = params;

  const applyFill = useCallback((wx: number, wy: number) => {
    const state = modalsStore.getState();
    const color = state.fillColor;

    // РС‰РµРј РІРµСЂС…РЅРёР№ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ РїРѕРґ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂРѕРј (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ shape РёР»Рё fill)
    const hit = slide.elements.slice().reverse().find((el) => {
      if (el.type !== 'shape' && el.type !== 'fill') return false;
      return wx >= el.x && wx <= el.x + el.width && wy >= el.y && wy <= el.y + el.height;
    });

    if (hit) {
      // F-FM-03: РљР»РёРє РїРѕ shape/fill вЂ” Р·Р°Р»РёРІР°РµРј Рё РћРЎРўРђР•РњРЎРЇ РІ СЂРµР¶РёРјРµ
      docStore.commit((draft) => {
        const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
        const t = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === hit.id);
        if (!t) return;
        if (t.type === 'shape' || t.type === 'fill') {
          (t as ShapeElement | FillElement).fill = color;
        }
      });
      return;
    }

    // F-FM-04: РљР»РёРє РїРѕ РїСѓСЃС‚РѕРјСѓ РјРµСЃС‚Сѓ вЂ” РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј С‚РѕС‡РєСѓ
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([...state.fillPoints, wx, wy]);
  }, [docStore, modalsStore, slide]);

  const closePolygon = useCallback(() => {
    const state = modalsStore.getState();
    const pts = state.fillPoints;
    if (pts.length < 6) return;

    const xs: number[] = [];
    const ys: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) { xs.push(pts[i]); ys.push(pts[i + 1]); }
    const minX = Math.min(...xs);
    const minY = Math.min(...ys);
    const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
    const maxY = Math.max(...ys);

    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      const el: FillElement = {
        id: generateId(), type: 'fill',
        x: minX, y: minY,
        width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
        height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
        rotation: 0,
        points: pts.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY)),
        fill: state.fillColor, stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, opacity: 1,
      };
      s.elements.push(el as CanvasElement);
    });
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
  }, [docStore, modalsStore]);

  return {
    applyFill, closePolygon,
    points: () => modalsStore.getState().fillPoints,
  };
}

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { Slide } from '../../../shared/types';
import { findElementWithPadding } from '../../../lib/connectors';

export function useCanvasHoverAnchor(params: { slide: Slide }) {
  const { slide } = params;
  const [hoveredId, setHoveredId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const hoveredRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const update = useCallback((e: any, wx: number, wy: number) => {
    // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј РѕС‚СЃС‚СѓРї 20px, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ С‚РѕС‡РєРё РЅРµ РїСЂРѕРїР°РґР°Р»Рё, РєРѕРіРґР° РјС‹ РІРµРґРµРј Рє РЅРёРј РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂ
    const el = findElementWithPadding(slide.elements, wx, wy, 20);
    
    if (el) {
      if (hoveredRef.current !== el.id) {
        hoveredRef.current = el.id;
        setHoveredId(el.id);
      }
    } else {
      if (hoveredRef.current !== null) {
        hoveredRef.current = null;
        setHoveredId(null);
      }
    }
  }, [slide]);

  return { update, getHoveredId: () => hoveredId };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasRubberBand.ts

import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { getBBox } from '../../../lib/geometry';

interface Box { x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number }

export function useCanvasRubberBand(params: { selStore: SelectionStore }) {
  const { selStore } = params;
  const [box, setBox] = useState<Box | null>(null);
  const active = useRef<Box | null>(null);

  const begin = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    active.current = { x1: x, y1: y, x2: x, y2: y };
    setBox({ ...active.current });
  }, []);

  const update = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!active.current) return;
    const b = active.current;
    active.current = {
      x1: b.x1,
      y1: b.y1,
      x2: x,
      y2: y,
    };
    setBox({ ...active.current });
  }, []);

  const finish = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const b = active.current;
    active.current = null;
    setBox(null);
    if (!b) return;

    const minX = Math.min(b.x1, b.x2);
    const maxX = Math.max(b.x1, b.x2);
    const minY = Math.min(b.y1, b.y2);
    const maxY = Math.max(b.y1, b.y2);

    // РљР»РёРє Р±РµР· РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёСЏ вЂ” РЅРµ С‚СЂРѕРіР°РµРј selection (clear СѓР¶Рµ СЃРґРµР»Р°РЅ РІ onStageDown)
    if (maxX - minX < 4 && maxY - minY < 4) return;

    if (!Array.isArray(elements) || !elements.length) return;

    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (const el of elements) {
      if (el.type === 'connector' || el.type === 'fill' || el.locked) continue;

      // РЈС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµРј РїРѕРІРѕСЂРѕС‚: РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ bbox, Р° РЅРµ x/y/width/height.
      const bb = getBBox(el);
      if (bb.x + bb.w < minX) continue;
      if (bb.x > maxX) continue;
      if (bb.y + bb.h < minY) continue;
      if (bb.y > maxY) continue;
      ids.push(el.id);
    }

    selStore.setIds(ids);
  }, [selStore]);

  return {
    begin, update, finish,
    isActive: () => active.current !== null,
    ref: () => box,
  };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\canvas\useCanvasViewport.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';

interface PendingScroll {
  mx: number;
  my: number;
  oldScrollLeft: number;
  oldScrollTop: number;
  oldZoom: number;
}

interface Args {
  /** Scroll-РєРѕРЅС‚РµР№РЅРµСЂ (overflow: auto) РІРѕРєСЂСѓРі Stage. */
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
  zoom: number;
  onZoomChange: (z: number | ((prev: number) => number)) => void;
  stageRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Stage>;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
}

/** F-CV-07: С€Р°Рі 0.05 (РЅРµ РјСѓР»СЊС‚РёРїР»РёРєР°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ Г—1.1). */
const STEP = 0.05;

export function useCanvasViewport({
  wrapRef,
  zoom,
  onZoomChange,
  stageRef: _stageRef,
  minZoom = 0.2,
  maxZoom = 2,
}: Args) {
  const [isPanning, setIsPanning] = useState(false);
  const panStart = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0, scrollLeft: 0, scrollTop: 0 });

  /** РђРєС‚СѓР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ zoom РєР°Рє ref вЂ” С‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ rAF-flush, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРµС‚СЊ РѕС‚ Р·Р°РјС‹РєР°РЅРёСЏ. */
  const zoomRef = useRef(zoom);
  zoomRef.current = zoom;

  /** Scroll, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ РЅР° СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј layout-effect РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃРјРµРЅС‹ zoom. */
  const pendingScrollRef = useRef<PendingScroll | null>(null);
  /** РќР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ deltaY (throttle вЂ” 1 setZoom РЅР° РєР°РґСЂ). */
  const pendingDeltaRef = useRef(0);
  /** РџРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РєСѓСЂСЃРѕСЂР° РІ clientX/Y (РїРѕСЂС‚ viewport). */
  const pendingMouseRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  /* ================================================================
     1. РџСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ scroll вЂ” Р”Рћ paint, СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРЅРѕ СЃ РЅРѕРІС‹Рј scaleX Stage.
        Р­С‚Рѕ СѓСЃС‚СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ РІРёР·СѓР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ В«РїСЂС‹Р¶РѕРєВ» РїСЂРё Р·СѓРјРµ.
     ================================================================ */
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const pending = pendingScrollRef.current;
    if (!pending) return;
    pendingScrollRef.current = null;

    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    if (!wrap) return;

    /* Force reflow: Сѓ Stage РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ scaleX, РЅРѕ scrollWidth РјРѕРі РµС‰С‘ РЅРµ РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊСЃСЏ. */
    void wrap.scrollWidth;
    void wrap.clientWidth;

    const ratio = zoomRef.current / pending.oldZoom;
    const maxX = Math.max(0, wrap.scrollWidth - wrap.clientWidth);
    const maxY = Math.max(0, wrap.scrollHeight - wrap.clientHeight);
    if (maxX === 0 && maxY === 0) return;

    const contentX = pending.oldScrollLeft + pending.mx;
    const contentY = pending.oldScrollTop + pending.my;

    let newScrollLeft = contentX * ratio - pending.mx;
    let newScrollTop = contentY * ratio - pending.my;

    /* Clamp вЂ” РёРЅР°С‡Рµ scroll В«РІС‹РїСЂС‹РіРёРІР°РµС‚В» Р·Р° max Рё Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚
       СЃРІРѕС‘ РѕРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ, С‡С‚Рѕ РґР°С‘С‚ СЃСѓР±РїРёРєСЃРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РґСЂРµР№С„ РїСЂРё РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРЅС‹С… Р·СѓРјР°С…. */
    newScrollLeft = Math.max(0, Math.min(newScrollLeft, maxX));
    newScrollTop = Math.max(0, Math.min(newScrollTop, maxY));

    const fx = Math.round(newScrollLeft);
    const fy = Math.round(newScrollTop);

    if (wrap.scrollLeft !== fx) wrap.scrollLeft = fx;
    if (wrap.scrollTop !== fy) wrap.scrollTop = fy;
  }, [zoom, wrapRef]);

  /* ================================================================
     2. Wheel-handler: РЅР°РєР°РїР»РёРІР°РµРј delta, flush С‡РµСЂРµР· rAF.
        setZoom вЂ” РјР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј 1 СЂР°Р· Р·Р° РєР°РґСЂ (throttle).
     ================================================================ */
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = wrapRef.current;
    if (!el) return;

    const flush = () => {
      rafRef.current = null;
      const delta = pendingDeltaRef.current;
      if (delta === 0) return;
      pendingDeltaRef.current = 0;

      const oldZoom = zoomRef.current;
      const direction = delta < 0 ? 1 : -1;

      /* Р‘С‹СЃС‚СЂР°СЏ РїСЂРѕРєСЂСѓС‚РєР°: 1..4 С€Р°РіР° Р·Р° РєР°РґСЂ. Р‘РµР· clamp РїРѕ steps
         РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°РµРј В«РїСЂС‹Р¶РєРёВ» РЅР° Р±РѕР»СЊС€РѕРј deltaY. */
      const steps = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, Math.round(Math.abs(delta) / 100)));
      const raw = oldZoom + direction * STEP * steps;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, +raw.toFixed(3)));
      if (next === oldZoom) return;

      const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();

      pendingScrollRef.current = {
        mx: pendingMouseRef.current.x - rect.left,
        my: pendingMouseRef.current.y - rect.top,
        oldScrollLeft: el.scrollLeft,
        oldScrollTop: el.scrollTop,
        oldZoom,
      };

      onZoomChange(next);
    };

    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      if (!e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();

      pendingDeltaRef.current += e.deltaY;
      pendingMouseRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };

      if (rafRef.current === null) {
        rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(flush);
      }
    };

    /* Safari: gesturestart/change Р±РµР· Ctrl. */
    const onGesture = (e: Event) => e.preventDefault();

    el.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false, capture: true });
    el.addEventListener('gesturestart', onGesture as any, { passive: false });
    el.addEventListener('gesturechange', onGesture as any, { passive: false });

    return () => {
      if (rafRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
      rafRef.current = null;
      el.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { capture: true } as any);
      el.removeEventListener('gesturestart', onGesture as any);
      el.removeEventListener('gesturechange', onGesture as any);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, onZoomChange, minZoom, maxZoom]);

  /* ================================================================
     3. Pan (Space + drag) вЂ” Р±РµР· РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№.
     ================================================================ */
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (e.code === 'Space' && !e.repeat && target.tagName !== 'INPUT' && target.tagName !== 'TEXTAREA') {
        setIsPanning(true);
      }
    };
    const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.code === 'Space') setIsPanning(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleMouseDown = useCallback((e: React.MouseEvent | any) => {
    if (!isPanning) return;
    const container = wrapRef.current;
    if (!container) return;

    panStart.current = {
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      scrollLeft: container.scrollLeft,
      scrollTop: container.scrollTop,
    };

    const handleMouseMove = (moveEvent: MouseEvent) => {
      const dx = moveEvent.clientX - panStart.current.x;
      const dy = moveEvent.clientY - panStart.current.y;
      container.scrollLeft = panStart.current.scrollLeft - dx;
      container.scrollTop = panStart.current.scrollTop - dy;
    };

    const handleMouseUp = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
  }, [isPanning, wrapRef]);

  return { isPanning, handleMouseDown };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\editor\useClipboardActions.ts

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../../shared/utils/id';

export function useClipboardActions(
  docStore: DocumentStore,
  selStore: SelectionStore,
  modalsStore: ModalsStore,
) {
  const copy = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const copyable = elements.filter((e) => e.type !== 'connector');
    if (!copyable.length) return;
    modalsStore.copy(copyable);
  }, [modalsStore]);

  const cut = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    const ids = elements.map((e) => e.id);
    if (!ids.length) return;
    copy(elements);
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => {
        if (ids.includes(el.id)) return false;
        if (el.type === 'connector' && (ids.includes(el.fromId) || ids.includes(el.toId))) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }, 'cut');
    selStore.clear();
  }, [docStore, selStore, copy]);

  const paste = useCallback(() => {
    const buf = modalsStore.getClipboard();
    if (!buf.length) return;
    const newIds: string[] = [];
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      if (!s) return;
      for (const el of buf) {
        const clone = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
        clone.id = generateId();
        clone.x += 20;
        clone.y += 20;
        s.elements.push(clone);
        newIds.push(clone.id);
      }
    }, 'paste');
    selStore.setIds(newIds);
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);

  const duplicate = useCallback((elements: CanvasElement[]) => {
    copy(elements);
    paste();
  }, [copy, paste]);

  return { copy, cut, paste, duplicate };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { DiagramElement } from '../../../shared/types';

export function useDiagramCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<DiagramElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((e) => e.id === elementId);
      if (!el || el.type !== 'diagram') return;
      if (patch.svg !== undefined) el.svg = patch.svg;
      if (patch.xml !== undefined) el.xml = patch.xml;
    }, `diagram-${elementId}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\editor\useEditingCommit.ts

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { EditingState } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';

export function useEditingCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((editing: EditingState, html: string | undefined, text: string) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const slide = draft.slides.find((s) => s.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = slide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === editing.id);
      if (!el) return;

      const safeText = text ?? '';

      switch (editing.field) {
        case 'text': {
          if (el.type !== 'text') return;
          // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј, РµСЃС‚СЊ Р»Рё РІ HTML С‡С‚Рѕ-С‚Рѕ, РєСЂРѕРјРµ РїСѓСЃС‚С‹С… РїР°СЂР°РіСЂР°С„РѕРІ (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, С‚Р°Р±Р»РёС†Р°)
          const hasRichContent = html && (html.includes('<table') || html.includes('<img'));
          const isEmpty = !safeText.trim() && !hasRichContent;
          
          el.text = isEmpty ? 'Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ С‚РµРєСЃС‚' : safeText;
          el.html = isEmpty ? undefined : html;
          return;
        }
        case 'footerText':
          if (el.type !== 'footer') return;
          el.text = safeText;
          if (html !== undefined) el.html = html || undefined;
          return;
        case 'shapeText':
          if (el.type !== 'shape') return;
          el.text = safeText;
          return;
        case 'tableCell': {
          if (el.type !== 'table') return;
          const r = editing.tableRow ?? 0;
          const c = editing.tableCol ?? 0;
          const rows = el.data.map((row) => [...row]);
          if (rows[r] && c < rows[r].length) rows[r][c] = safeText;
          el.data = rows;
          return;
        }
        default:
          return;
      }
    }, `commit-${editing.id}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

// src/presentation/hooks/editor/useFileIO.ts
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore, useModalsStore,
} from '../../providers/EditorProvider';
import { migrateDocument } from '../../../lib/migrations';
import {
  exportSvgServer as doExportSvgServer,
  exportPdfaServer as doExportPdfaServer,
} from '../../../lib/serverExport';
import { CONFIG } from '../../../shared/config';

/* ---------- helpers ---------- */

function readFileAsText(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result as string);
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsText(file);
  });
}

function readFileAsDataUrl(file: File): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result as string);
    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

function triggerDownload(content: Blob | string, filename: string, mime: string): void {
  const blob = typeof content === 'string'
    ? new Blob([content], { type: mime })
    : content;
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

/* ---------- hook ---------- */

export function useFileIO() {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();
  const modalsStore = useModalsStore();

  const getActiveSlide = useCallback(() => {
    const state = docStore.getState();
    return state.slides.find((s) => s.id === state.activeId) ?? null;
  }, [docStore]);

  const clearUiState = useCallback(() => {
    selStore.clear();
    modalsStore.closeEditor();
    modalsStore.setFloatingPos(null);
    modalsStore.setContextMenu(null);
    modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
  }, [selStore, modalsStore]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-01: Export JSON
     ============================================================ */

  const exportJson = useCallback(() => {
    const state = docStore.getState();
    const payload = {
      version: '2.0',
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      slides: state.slides,
      data: state.data,
    };
    triggerDownload(
      JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2),
      `ticket-editor-${Date.now()}.json`,
      'application/json',
    );
    // F-AS-06: РѕС‡РёСЃС‚РєР° autosave РїСЂРё СЌРєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚Рµ
    try { localStorage.removeItem('ticket-editor:autosave'); } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }, [docStore]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-02..09: Import JSON СЃ РјРёРіСЂР°С†РёСЏРјРё
     ============================================================ */

  const importJson = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = '.json,application/json';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;

      // F-IO-06 / REG-P5: Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂР°
      if (file.size > CONFIG.MAX_IMPORT_SIZE) {
        const mb = (CONFIG.MAX_IMPORT_SIZE / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(0);
        alert(`Р¤Р°Р№Р» СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј Р±РѕР»СЊС€РѕР№ (РјР°РєСЃ. ${mb} MB)`);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const text = await readFileAsText(file);
        const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
        const migrated = migrateDocument(parsed);

        // activeSlideId РјРѕРі Р±С‹С‚СЊ РЅРµРІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Рј (JSON РѕС‚ РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё)
        const validActiveId =
          migrated.slides.find((s) => s.id === migrated.activeSlideId)?.id
          ?? migrated.slides[0].id;

        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          draft.slides = migrated.slides;
          draft.activeId = validActiveId;
          draft.data = migrated.data;
        }, 'import-json');

        clearUiState();
      } catch (err) {
        alert(`РћС€РёР±РєР° РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚Р°: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      }
    };
    input.click();
  }, [docStore, clearUiState]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-10: SVG Р»РѕРєР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ (Р±РµР· embed)
     ============================================================ */

  const exportSvgLocal = useCallback(async () => {
    const active = getActiveSlide();
    if (!active) return;

    const { serializeSlideToSvg } = await import('../../../lib/svg/serialize');
    const svg = serializeSlideToSvg(active);
    const safeName = (active.name || 'slide').replace(/[^\w.-]+/g, '_');
    triggerDownload(svg, `${safeName}.svg`, 'image/svg+xml');
  }, [getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-11: SVG С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂ
     ============================================================ */

  const exportSvgServer = useCallback(async () => {
    const active = getActiveSlide();
    if (!active) return;
    await doExportSvgServer(active);
  }, [getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IO-12: PDF/A С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂ
     ============================================================ */

  const exportPdfaServer = useCallback(async () => {
    const active = getActiveSlide();
    if (!active) return;
    await doExportPdfaServer(active);
  }, [getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-IMG-05: Р”РѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєСѓ
     ============================================================ */

  const addImage = useCallback((opts: { centered?: boolean } = {}) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;

      try {
        const src = await readFileAsDataUrl(file);
        const img = await new Promise<HTMLImageElement>((resolve, reject) => {
          const i = new Image();
          i.onload = () => resolve(i);
          i.onerror = reject;
          i.src = src;
        });

        const active = getActiveSlide();
        if (!active) return;

        const maxRatio = opts.centered ? 0.7 : 1;
        const k = Math.min(
          1,
          (active.width * maxRatio) / img.width,
          (active.height * maxRatio) / img.height,
        );
        const w = Math.max(20, Math.round(img.width * k));
        const h = Math.max(20, Math.round(img.height * k));
        const x = opts.centered ? Math.round((active.width - w) / 2) : 100;
        const y = opts.centered ? Math.round((active.height - h) / 2) : 100;

        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((sl) => sl.id === draft.activeId);
          if (s) {
            s.elements.push({
              id: Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10),
              type: 'image',
              x, y, width: w, height: h, rotation: 0,
              src,
            });
          }
        }, 'add-image');
      } catch (err) {
        alert(`РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РёС‚СЊ РєР°СЂС‚РёРЅРєСѓ: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      }
    };
    input.click();
  }, [docStore, getActiveSlide]);

  /* ============================================================
     F-LP-07: С„РѕРЅ СЃР»Р°Р№РґР°
     ============================================================ */

  const setBackgroundImage = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = async () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;
      try {
        const src = await readFileAsDataUrl(file);
        docStore.commit((draft) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((sl) => sl.id === draft.activeId);
          if (s) s.backgroundImage = src;
        }, 'set-bg');
      } catch (err) {
        alert(`РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ С„РѕРЅ: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      }
    };
    input.click();
  }, [docStore]);

  const removeBackgroundImage = useCallback(() => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((sl) => sl.id === draft.activeId);
      if (s) s.backgroundImage = undefined;
    }, 'remove-bg');
  }, [docStore]);

  return {
    exportJson,
    importJson,
    exportSvgLocal,
    exportSvgServer,
    exportPdfaServer,
    addImage,
    setBackgroundImage,
    removeBackgroundImage,
  };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { StampElement } from '../../../shared/types';

export function useStampCommit(docStore: DocumentStore) {
  return useCallback((elementId: string, patch: Partial<StampElement>) => {
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((e) => e.id === elementId);
      if (!el || el.type !== 'stamp') return;
      if (patch.width !== undefined) el.width = patch.width;
      if (patch.height !== undefined) el.height = patch.height;
      if (patch.shapes !== undefined) el.shapes = patch.shapes.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
      if (patch.texts !== undefined) el.texts = patch.texts.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
      if (patch.images !== undefined) el.images = patch.images.map((x) => ({ ...x }));
    }, `stamp-${elementId}`);
  }, [docStore]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore } from '../../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../../shared/types';

export function useStyleClipboard(
  docStore: DocumentStore,
  selStore: SelectionStore,
  modalsStore: ModalsStore,
) {
  const copyStyle = useCallback((el: CanvasElement) => {
    modalsStore.copyStyle(el);
  }, [modalsStore]);

  const applyStyle = useCallback(() => {
    const style = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard();
    const id = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
    if (!style || !id) return;
    docStore.commit((draft) => {
      const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
      const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (el) Object.assign(el, style);
    }, `style-${id}`);
    modalsStore.clearStyle();
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);

  return { copyStyle, applyStyle };
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export interface CanvasWrapSize { w: number; h: number; }

export function useStampCanvasWrap(
  ref: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>,
  fallback: CanvasWrapSize = { w: 800, h: 600 },
): CanvasWrapSize {
  const [size, setSize] = useState<CanvasWrapSize>(fallback);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const update = () => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      setSize({ w: r.width, h: r.height });
    };
    update();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(update);
    ro.observe(el);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, [ref]);

  return size;
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { uid } from '../../../lib/uid';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  selectedType: SelectionType;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: SelectionType) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
}

const PASTE_OFFSET = 12;

export function useStampCRUD({
  shapes, texts, images, selectedId, selectedType, commit, select, clearSelection,
}: Args) {
  const deleteSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !selectedType) return;
    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      commit(shapes.filter((s) => s.id !== selectedId), texts, images);
    } else if (selectedType === 'text') {
      commit(shapes, texts.filter((t) => t.id !== selectedId), images);
    } else {
      commit(shapes, texts, images.filter((im) => im.id !== selectedId));
    }
    clearSelection();
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, images, commit, clearSelection]);

  const duplicateSelected = useCallback(() => {
    if (!selectedId || !selectedType) return;
    const off = PASTE_OFFSET;

    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!s) return;
      const c: StampShapeItem = { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s)), id: uid(), x: s.x + off, y: s.y + off };
      commit([...shapes, c], texts, images);
      select(c.id, 'shape');
    } else if (selectedType === 'text') {
      const t = texts.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!t) return;
      const c: StampTextItem = { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t)), id: uid() };
      if (c.mode === 'straight') { c.x += off; c.y += off; }
      else { c.arcCx += off; c.arcCy += off; }
      commit(shapes, [...texts, c], images);
      select(c.id, 'text');
    } else {
      const im = images.find((x) => x.id === selectedId);
      if (!im) return;
      const c: StampImageItem = { ...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(im)), id: uid(), x: im.x + off, y: im.y + off };
      commit(shapes, texts, [...images, c]);
      select(c.id, 'image');
    }
  }, [selectedId, selectedType, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  return { deleteSelected, duplicateSelected };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampDragHandlers.ts
SIZE: 3338 bytes

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { snapPosition, snapToHostCenter, findContainingShape } from '../../../lib/stamps/geometry';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  snapEnabled: boolean;
  updateShape: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => void;
  updateText: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => void;
  updateImage: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
}

function isCtrlPressed(e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<any>): boolean {
  const evt = e.evt as MouseEvent | undefined;
  return !!(evt && (evt.ctrlKey || evt.metaKey));
}

export function useStampDragHandlers({ shapes, width, height, snapEnabled, updateShape, updateText, updateImage }: Args) {
  const handleShapeDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>) => {
    const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!s || s.kind === 'fill') return;
    const node = e.target;
    const rawX = node.x() - s.width / 2;
    const rawY = node.y() - s.height / 2;
    if (isCtrlPressed(e)) {
      node.x(Math.round(rawX) + s.width / 2);
      node.y(Math.round(rawY) + s.height / 2);
      updateShape(id, { x: Math.round(rawX), y: Math.round(rawY) });
      return;
    }
    const host = findContainingShape(rawX + s.width / 2, rawY + s.height / 2, id, shapes);
    const cs = snapToHostCenter(rawX, rawY, s.width, s.height, host);
    let nx = cs.x, ny = cs.y;
    if (!cs.snappedX || !cs.snappedY) {
      const general = snapPosition(nx, ny, s.width, s.height, id, shapes, width, height, snapEnabled);
      if (!cs.snappedX) nx = general.x;
      if (!cs.snappedY) ny = general.y;
    }
    node.x(nx + s.width / 2);
    node.y(ny + s.height / 2);
    updateShape(id, { x: Math.round(nx), y: Math.round(ny) });
  }, [shapes, snapEnabled, width, height, updateShape]);

  const handleTextDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, text: StampTextItem) => {
    const node = e.target;
    if (isCtrlPressed(e)) {
      node.x(Math.round(node.x())); node.y(Math.round(node.y()));
      updateText(id, { x: Math.round(node.x()), y: Math.round(node.y()) });
      return;
    }
    const snapped = snapPosition(node.x(), node.y(), text.width, text.fontSize * 1.4, id, shapes, width, height, snapEnabled);
    node.x(snapped.x); node.y(snapped.y);
    updateText(id, { x: snapped.x, y: snapped.y });
  }, [shapes, snapEnabled, width, height, updateText]);

  const handleImageDragEnd = useCallback((id: string, e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<DragEvent>, image: StampImageItem) => {
    const node = e.target;
    if (isCtrlPressed(e)) {
      node.x(Math.round(node.x())); node.y(Math.round(node.y()));
      updateImage(id, { x: Math.round(node.x()), y: Math.round(node.y()) });
      return;
    }
    const snapped = snapPosition(node.x(), node.y(), image.width, image.height, id, shapes, width, height, snapEnabled);
    node.x(snapped.x); node.y(snapped.y);
    updateImage(id, { x: snapped.x, y: snapped.y });
  }, [shapes, snapEnabled, width, height, updateImage]);

  return { handleShapeDragEnd, handleTextDragEnd, handleImageDragEnd };
}

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { findShapesContaining } from '../../../lib/stamps/geometry';
import { uid } from '../../../lib/uid';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number;
  height: number;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: 'shape' | 'text' | 'image') => void;
}

export function useStampFillMode({
  shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select,
}: Args) {
  const [fillMode, setFillMode] = useState(false);
  const [fillColor, setFillColor] = useState('#fbbf24');

  const handleCanvasClick = useCallback((px: number, py: number) => {
    if (!fillMode) return;
    const containing = findShapesContaining(px, py, shapes);
    if (containing.length === 0) return;

    const target = containing.reduce((a, b) => a.width * a.height <= b.width * b.height ? a : b);
    const localX = (px - target.x) / Math.max(1, target.width);
    const localY = (py - target.y) / Math.max(1, target.height);

    const fill: StampShapeItem = {
      id: uid(), kind: 'fill', x: 0, y: 0, width, height, rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
      stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
      fill: fillColor, fillTargetShapeId: target.id, fillAnchorLocal: { x: localX, y: localY },
      name: 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё',
    };
    commit([...shapes, fill], texts, images);
    select(fill.id, 'shape');
    setFillMode(false);
  }, [fillMode, fillColor, shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);

  return { fillMode, setFillMode, fillColor, setFillColor, handleCanvasClick };
}
