FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampHistory.ts
SIZE: 1636 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';

export interface StampSnapshot {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
}

const MAX_HISTORY = 60;

export function useStampHistory(initial: StampSnapshot) {
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState<StampSnapshot>(initial);
  const historyRef = useRef<StampSnapshot[]>([initial]);
  const indexRef = useRef(0);
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0);

  const push = useCallback((next: StampSnapshot) => {
    const trimmed = historyRef.current.slice(0, indexRef.current + 1);
    trimmed.push(next);
    while (trimmed.length > MAX_HISTORY) trimmed.shift();
    historyRef.current = trimmed;
    indexRef.current = trimmed.length - 1;
    setCurrent(next);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  }, []);

  const replace = useCallback((next: StampSnapshot) => {
    setCurrent(next);
  }, []);

  const undo = useCallback(() => {
    if (indexRef.current <= 0) return;
    indexRef.current -= 1;
    setCurrent(historyRef.current[indexRef.current]);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  }, []);

  const redo = useCallback(() => {
    if (indexRef.current >= historyRef.current.length - 1) return;
    indexRef.current += 1;
    setCurrent(historyRef.current[indexRef.current]);
    setVersion((v) => v + 1);
  }, []);

  return {
    state: current,
    push, replace, undo, redo,
    canUndo: indexRef.current > 0,
    canRedo: indexRef.current < historyRef.current.length - 1,
    version,
  };
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';

export function useStampImageCache(images: StampImageItem[]): Record<string, HTMLImageElement> {
  const [cache, setCache] = useState<Record<string, HTMLImageElement>>({});
  const key = images.map((i) => i.src).join('|');

  useEffect(() => {
    let changed = false;
    const next = { ...cache };
    for (const im of images) {
      if (im.hidden || next[im.src]) continue;
      const img = new window.Image();
      img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
      img.src = im.src;
      img.onload = () => setCache((prev) => ({ ...prev, [im.src]: img }));
      changed = true;
    }
    if (changed) setCache(next);
  }, [key]);

  return cache;
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { loadImage, fitImageSize } from '../../../lib/imageTools';

interface Args {
  updateImage: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
  stampW: number; stampH: number;
}

export function useStampImageReplace({ updateImage, stampW, stampH }: Args) {
  return useCallback((id: string) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file'; input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0]; if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = async () => {
        const src = reader.result as string;
        try {
          const img = await loadImage(src);
          const { width: iw, height: ih } = fitImageSize(img.naturalWidth || 100, img.naturalHeight || 100, stampW, stampH, 0.5);
          updateImage(id, { src, width: iw, height: ih });
        } catch { updateImage(id, { src }); }
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [updateImage, stampW, stampH]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { createImage } from '../../../lib/stamps/factories';
import { loadImage, makeImageTransparent, fitImageSize } from '../../../lib/imageTools';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number; height: number;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: 'shape' | 'text' | 'image') => void;
}

export function useStampImageUpload({ shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select }: Args) {
  return useCallback((transparent: boolean) => {
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file'; input.accept = 'image/*';
    input.onchange = () => {
      const file = input.files?.[0]; if (!file) return;
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = async () => {
        let src = reader.result as string;
        if (transparent) {
          try { src = await makeImageTransparent(src, { keyColor: 'auto', tolerance: 30 }); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
        }
        let nw = 100, nh = 100;
        try { const img = await loadImage(src); nw = img.naturalWidth || 100; nh = img.naturalHeight || 100; } catch {}
        const { width: iw, height: ih } = fitImageSize(nw, nh, width, height, 0.5);
        const im = createImage(src, iw, ih, width, height);
        commit(shapes, texts, [...images, im]);
        select(im.id, 'image');
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };
    input.click();
  }, [shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';

type LayerKind = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  clearSelection: () => void;
}

export function useStampLayerActions({ shapes, texts, images, selectedId, commit, clearSelection }: Args) {
  const toggleHidden = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind) => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, hidden: !s.hidden } : s)), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, texts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, hidden: !t.hidden } : t)), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, images.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, hidden: !im.hidden } : im)));
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  const toggleLocked = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind) => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.map((s) => (s.id === id ? { ...s, locked: !s.locked } : s)), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, texts.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, locked: !t.locked } : t)), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, images.map((im) => (im.id === id ? { ...im, locked: !im.locked } : im)));
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  const removeLayer = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind) => {
    if (type === 'shape') commit(shapes.filter((s) => s.id !== id), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, texts.filter((t) => t.id !== id), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, images.filter((im) => im.id !== id));
    if (selectedId === id) clearSelection();
  }, [shapes, texts, images, selectedId, commit, clearSelection]);

  const reorder = useCallback((id: string, type: LayerKind, dir: 1 | -1) => {
    const swap = <T extends { id: string }>(arr: T[]): T[] => {
      const i = arr.findIndex((x) => x.id === id);
      const j = i + dir;
      if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= arr.length) return arr;
      const copy = [...arr]; [copy[i], copy[j]] = [copy[j], copy[i]]; return copy;
    };
    if (type === 'shape') commit(swap(shapes), texts, images);
    else if (type === 'text') commit(shapes, swap(texts), images);
    else commit(shapes, texts, swap(images));
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  return { toggleHidden, toggleLocked, removeLayer, reorder };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampShortcuts.ts

import { useEffect } from 'react';

export interface StampShortcutsConfig {
  editingTextId: string | null;
  selectedId: string | null;
  onCloseTextEditor: () => void;
  onClearSelection: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onUndo: () => void;
  onRedo: () => void;
  onCloseEditor: () => void;
}

export function useStampShortcuts(config: StampShortcutsConfig) {
  const {
    editingTextId, selectedId, onCloseTextEditor, onClearSelection,
    onDelete, onDuplicate, onUndo, onRedo, onCloseEditor,
  } = config;

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const t = e.target as HTMLElement;
      const isField = t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.tagName === 'SELECT' || t.isContentEditable;
      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;

      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (editingTextId) { onCloseTextEditor(); return; }
        if (selectedId) { onClearSelection(); return; }
        onCloseEditor();
        return;
      }
      if (isField) return;

      if ((e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') && selectedId) {
        e.preventDefault(); onDelete();
      } else if (ctrl && e.code === 'KeyD' && selectedId) {
        e.preventDefault(); onDuplicate();
      } else if (ctrl && e.code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault(); onUndo();
      } else if (ctrl && (e.code === 'KeyY' || (e.code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey))) {
        e.preventDefault(); onRedo();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [editingTextId, selectedId, onCloseTextEditor, onClearSelection, onDelete, onDuplicate, onUndo, onRedo, onCloseEditor]);
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampTextEditor.ts

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';

interface Snapshot { shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[]; }

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  replace: (next: Snapshot) => void;
}

export function useStampTextEditor({ shapes, texts, images, commit, replace }: Args) {
  const [editingTextId, setEditingTextId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const openTextEditor = useCallback((id: string) => {
    if (editingTextId === id) return;
    commit(shapes, texts, images);
    setEditingTextId(id);
  }, [editingTextId, shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  const changeEditingText = useCallback((value: string) => {
    if (!editingTextId) return;
    replace({ shapes, texts: texts.map((t) => (t.id === editingTextId ? { ...t, text: value } : t)), images });
  }, [editingTextId, shapes, texts, images, replace]);

  const closeTextEditor = useCallback(() => {
    if (!editingTextId) return;
    commit(shapes, texts, images);
    setEditingTextId(null);
  }, [editingTextId, shapes, texts, images, commit]);

  return { editingTextId, openTextEditor, changeEditingText, closeTextEditor };
}

FILE: src\presentation\hooks\stamps\useStampTextUpdate.ts
SIZE: 3561 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import {
  MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG, DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET,
} from '../../../lib/stamps/geometry';

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
}

export function useStampTextUpdate({ shapes, texts, images, commit }: Args) {
  return useCallback((id: string, patch: Partial<StampTextItem>) => {
    const t = texts.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!t) {
      commit(shapes, texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)), images);
      return;
    }

    const merged: StampTextItem = { ...t, ...patch };

    if (merged.mode === 'arc') {
      let host: StampShapeItem | null = null;
      if (t.arcHostId) {
        host = shapes.find((s) => s.id === t.arcHostId) || null;
      }
      if (!host) {
        host = shapes.find((s) => {
          if (s.hidden || s.kind === 'line' || s.kind === 'fill') return false;
          return Math.abs(s.x + s.width / 2 - t.arcCx) < 1
              && Math.abs(s.y + s.height / 2 - t.arcCy) < 1;
        }) || null;
      }

      if (host) {
        const hostId = host.id;
        const baseR = Math.min(host.width, host.height) / 2;
        const arcOffset = Math.max(0, merged.arcOffset ?? DEFAULT_ARC_OFFSET);

        if ('arcOutside' in patch && Object.keys(patch).length === 1) {
          const isOutsideNew = !!merged.arcOutside;
          const newRadius = host.kind === 'rect' || host.kind === 'triangle'
              ? baseR : isOutsideNew ? baseR + arcOffset : Math.max(merged.fontSize * 0.8, baseR - arcOffset);
          commit(shapes, texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch, arcRadius: newRadius, arcHostId: hostId } : x)), images);
          return;
        }

        if ('arcOffset' in patch || 'fontSize' in patch) {
          const isOutside = !!merged.arcOutside;
          const newRadius = host.kind === 'rect' || host.kind === 'triangle'
              ? baseR : isOutside ? baseR + arcOffset : Math.max(merged.fontSize * 0.8, baseR - arcOffset);
          commit(shapes, texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch, arcRadius: newRadius, arcHostId: hostId } : x)), images);
          return;
        }

        if ('arcSpanDeg' in patch) {
          const spanDeg = Math.min(MAX_ARC_SPAN_DEG, Math.max(MIN_ARC_SPAN_DEG, merged.arcSpanDeg ?? DEFAULT_ARC_SPAN_DEG));
          const centerDeg = (t.arcStartDeg + t.arcEndDeg) / 2;
          const isTop = Math.sin((centerDeg * Math.PI) / 180) < 0;
          const half = spanDeg / 2;
          const startDeg = isTop ? centerDeg - half : centerDeg + half;
          const endDeg   = isTop ? centerDeg + half : centerDeg - half;
          commit(shapes, texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch, arcSpanDeg: spanDeg, arcStartDeg: startDeg, arcEndDeg: endDeg, arcHostId: hostId } : x)), images);
          return;
        }

        if ('arcStartDeg' in patch || 'arcEndDeg' in patch) {
          const span = Math.abs(merged.arcEndDeg - merged.arcStartDeg);
          commit(shapes, texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch, arcSpanDeg: span } : x)), images);
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    commit(shapes, texts.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, ...patch } : x)), images);
  }, [shapes, texts, images, commit]);
}

import { useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampTextItem, StampShapeKind, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { createShape, createStraightText, createArcText, type FactoryContext } from '../../../lib/stamps/factories';
import type { PlacementMode } from '../../../lib/stamps/geometry';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; texts: StampTextItem[]; images: StampImageItem[];
  width: number; height: number; placementMode: PlacementMode;
  selectedId: string | null; selectedType: SelectionType;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;
  select: (id: string | null, type: SelectionType) => void;
}

export function useStampToolbarActions({
  shapes, texts, images, width, height, placementMode, selectedId, selectedType, commit, select,
}: Args) {
  const ctx: FactoryContext = useMemo(() => ({
    shapes, selectedId, selectedType, stampW: width, stampH: height, placementMode,
  }), [shapes, selectedId, selectedType, width, height, placementMode]);

  const addShape = useCallback((kind: StampShapeKind) => {
    const s = createShape(kind, ctx);
    commit([...shapes, s], texts, images);
    select(s.id, 'shape');
  }, [ctx, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  const addStraightText = useCallback(() => {
    const t = createStraightText(ctx);
    commit(shapes, [...texts, t], images);
    select(t.id, 'text');
  }, [ctx, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  const addArcText = useCallback(() => {
    const t = createArcText(ctx);
    commit(shapes, [...texts, t], images);
    select(t.id, 'text');
  }, [ctx, shapes, texts, images, commit, select]);

  return { addShape, addStraightText, addArcText };
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';
import type { StampShapeItem, StampImageItem } from '../../../shared/types';
import { getShapeStrategy } from '../../../lib/stamps/shapes';

type SelectionType = 'shape' | 'text' | 'image' | null;

interface Args {
  shapes: StampShapeItem[]; images: StampImageItem[];
  selectedType: SelectionType;
  updateShape: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampShapeItem>) => void;
  updateImage: (id: string, patch: Partial<StampImageItem>) => void;
}

export function useStampTransform({ shapes, images, selectedType, updateShape, updateImage }: Args) {
  return useCallback((e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<Event>) => {
    const node = e.target; const id = node.id();
    const evt = e.evt as MouseEvent | undefined;
    const ctrl = !!(evt && (evt.ctrlKey || evt.metaKey));

    if (selectedType === 'shape') {
      const s = shapes.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!s || s.kind === 'fill') return;
      const strategy = getShapeStrategy(s.kind);
      const result = strategy.applyTransform({
        shape: s, nodeX: node.x(), nodeY: node.y(), nodeRotation: node.rotation(),
        scaleX: node.scaleX(), scaleY: node.scaleY(), ctrl, shapes,
      });
      if (result.resetScale) { node.scaleX(1); node.scaleY(1); }
      updateShape(id, result.patch);
      return;
    }

    if (selectedType === 'image') {
      const im = images.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!im) return;
      const nw = Math.max(8, im.width * node.scaleX());
      const nh = Math.max(8, im.height * node.scaleY());
      node.scaleX(1); node.scaleY(1);
      updateImage(id, { x: Math.round(node.x()), y: Math.round(node.y()), width: Math.round(nw), height: Math.round(nh) });
    }
  }, [shapes, images, selectedType, updateShape, updateImage]);
}

import { useCallback } from 'react';
import type { useStampHistory } from './useStampHistory';

interface Args {
  history: ReturnType<typeof useStampHistory>;
  clearSelection: () => void;
}

export function useStampUndoRedo({ history, clearSelection }: Args) {
  const undo = useCallback(() => {
    history.undo();
    clearSelection();
  }, [history, clearSelection]);

  const redo = useCallback(() => {
    history.redo();
    clearSelection();
  }, [history, clearSelection]);

  return { undo, redo };
}

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import type Konva from 'konva';

const MIN_ZOOM = 0.4;
const MAX_ZOOM = 6;

export function useStampZoomPan(stageRef: React.RefObject<Konva.Stage>) {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const onWheel = useCallback((e: Konva.KonvaEventObject<WheelEvent>) => {
    e.evt.preventDefault();
    const dir = e.evt.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const stage = stageRef.current;
    if (!stage) return;
    const oldZoom = zoom;
    const next = Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, +(oldZoom + dir * 0.08).toFixed(3)));
    if (next === oldZoom) return;
    const pointer = stage.getPointerPosition();
    if (!pointer) { setZoom(next); return; }
    const mx = (pointer.x - pan.x) / oldZoom;
    const my = (pointer.y - pan.y) / oldZoom;
    setPan({ x: pointer.x - mx * next, y: pointer.y - my * next });
    setZoom(next);
  }, [stageRef, zoom, pan]);

  const zoomIn = useCallback(() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, +(z + 0.1).toFixed(2))), []);
  const zoomOut = useCallback(() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, +(z - 0.1).toFixed(2))), []);
  
  const zoomFit = useCallback((wrapW: number, wrapH: number, stampW: number, stampH: number) => {
    const fitX = (wrapW - 60) / stampW;
    const fitY = (wrapH - 60) / stampH;
    setZoom(Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(fitX, fitY))));
    setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  }, []);

  return { zoom, pan, setZoom, setPan, onWheel, zoomIn, zoomOut, zoomFit, MIN_ZOOM, MAX_ZOOM };
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useAutoFitZoom.ts
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect } from 'react';

interface Args {
  wrapRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement>;
  slideWidth: number;
  slideHeight: number;
  setZoom: (z: number) => void;
  padding?: number;
  topReserve?: number;
  minZoom?: number;
  maxZoom?: number;
}

export function useAutoFitZoom({
  wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom,
  padding = 40, topReserve = 80, minZoom = 0.2, maxZoom = 2,
}: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const wrap = wrapRef.current;
    if (!wrap || !slideWidth || !slideHeight) return;

    const fit = () => {
      const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
      // РћС‚РЅРёРјР°РµРј РїР°РґРґРёРЅРіРё Рё СЂРµР·РµСЂРІ РїРѕРґ ZoomBar
      const availableW = Math.max(100, rect.width - padding * 2);
      const availableH = Math.max(100, rect.height - padding * 2 - topReserve);

      const zoomX = availableW / slideWidth;
      const zoomY = availableH / slideHeight;
      const next = Math.max(minZoom, Math.min(maxZoom, Math.min(zoomX, zoomY)));

      setZoom(+next.toFixed(3));
    };

    fit();

    let raf = 0;
    const onResize = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(fit);
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
    };
  }, [wrapRef, slideWidth, slideHeight, setZoom, padding, topReserve, minZoom, maxZoom]);
}

/* ============================================================
   useAutoSave.ts
   F-AS-02: Debounce 2000ms.
   F-AS-03: TTL 24h.
   REG-B8: РќРµ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РІРІРѕРґ (Р°СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРЅР°СЏ Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ).
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Slide, DataRoot } from '../../shared/types';

const AUTO_SAVE_KEY = 'ticket-editor:autosave';
const AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS = 2000;
const MAX_AGE_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

export interface AutoSaveData {
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
  activeSlideId: string | null;
  savedAt: string;
}

export function useAutoSave(
  slides: Slide[],
  data: DataRoot[],
  activeSlideId: string | null,
): void {
  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    
    timerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      const saveData: AutoSaveData = {
        slides, data, activeSlideId,
        savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      
      // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј requestIdleCallback (РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РЅРµ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ UI
      const doSave = () => {
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(saveData));
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('[useAutoSave] Failed to save:', err);
        }
      };

      if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
        (window as any).requestIdleCallback(doSave);
      } else {
        setTimeout(doSave, 0);
      }
    }, AUTO_SAVE_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => {
      if (timerRef.current) window.clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    };
  }, [slides, data, activeSlideId]);
}

export function loadAutoSave(): AutoSaveData | null {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY);
    if (!saved) return null;
    const data: AutoSaveData = JSON.parse(saved);
    
    if (new Date().getTime() - new Date(data.savedAt).getTime() > MAX_AGE_MS) {
      clearAutoSave();
      return null;
    }
    return data;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function clearAutoSave(): void {
  try { localStorage.removeItem(AUTO_SAVE_KEY); } catch { /* ignore */ }
}

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import type { DocumentStore, DocumentState } from '../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import type { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import type { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';
import type { CanvasElement } from '../../shared/types';
import { generateId } from '../../shared/utils/id';

interface Args {
  docStore: DocumentStore;
  selStore: SelectionStore;
  modalsStore: ModalsStore;
}

const isEditable = (el: EventTarget | null): boolean => {
  const h = el as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!h) return false;
  return (
    h.isContentEditable ||
    h.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
    h.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
    h.tagName === 'SELECT'
  );
};

/**
 * Р“Р»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ С€РѕСЂС‚РєР°С‚С‹ СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР° (В§38).
 *
 * - РќРµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ РїСЂРё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹С… РјРѕРґР°Р»РєР°С… (В§F-STMP-E-17, В§F-DG вЂ” СЃР°РјРё РѕР±СЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°СЋС‚).
 * - РќРµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РµС‚, РєРѕРіРґР° С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР° вЂ” РєСЂРѕРјРµ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°СЏ, РєРѕРіРґР° РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚
 *   inline-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ (С‚РѕРіРґР° Escape РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РµРіРѕ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ вЂ” В§F-IE-07).
 * - MergeKey РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ continuous-update (`update-<id>`), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹
 *   РЅРµ СЃРєР»РµРёС‚СЊ РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ РёРґСѓС‰РёРµ delete/paste/duplicate РІ РѕРґРЅСѓ Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ (В§I-13, В§I-14).
 */
export function useEditorShortcuts({ docStore, selStore, modalsStore }: Args) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const ms = modalsStore.getState();

      /* РњРѕРґР°Р»РєРё (StampEditor, DiagramEditor) СЃР°РјРё РІРµС€Р°СЋС‚ СЃРІРѕРё РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‡РёРєРё. */
      if (ms.stampEditorTarget || ms.diagramEditorTarget) return;

      /* Р¤РѕРєСѓСЃ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР° Рё inline-СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂ РЅРµ Р°РєС‚РёРІРµРЅ вЂ” РЅРµ РјРµС€Р°РµРј. */
      if (isEditable(e.target) && !ms.editing) return;

      const ctrl = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
      const code = e.code;

      /* -------- Escape (В§F-IE-07, В§F-FM-06) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (ms.editing) {
          modalsStore.closeEditor();
          return;
        }
        if (ms.fillPoints.length) {
          modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
          return;
        }
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Enter: Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїРѕР»РёРіРѕРЅ fill (В§F-FM-05) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !ms.editing && ms.fillPoints.length >= 6) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const pts = ms.fillPoints.slice();
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          const xs: number[] = [];
          const ys: number[] = [];
          for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i += 2) {
            xs.push(pts[i]);
            ys.push(pts[i + 1]);
          }
          const minX = Math.min(...xs);
          const minY = Math.min(...ys);
          const maxX = Math.max(...xs);
          const maxY = Math.max(...ys);
          const fillEl: CanvasElement = {
            id: generateId(),
            type: 'fill',
            x: minX,
            y: minY,
            width: Math.max(1, maxX - minX),
            height: Math.max(1, maxY - minY),
            rotation: 0,
            points: pts.map((v, i) => (i % 2 === 0 ? v - minX : v - minY)),
            fill: ms.fillColor,
            stroke: '',
            strokeWidth: 0,
            opacity: 1,
          } as CanvasElement;
          s.elements.push(fillEl);
        });
        modalsStore.setFillPoints([]);
        return;
      }

      /* Р”Р°Р»СЊС€Рµ вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРіРґР° С„РѕРєСѓСЃ РЅРµ РІ РїРѕР»Рµ РІРІРѕРґР°. */
      if (isEditable(e.target)) return;

      /* -------- Delete / Backspace (В§38) -------- */
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        if (!ids.length) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
        });
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Undo / Redo (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyZ' && !e.shiftKey) {
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.undo();
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && (code === 'KeyY' || (code === 'KeyZ' && e.shiftKey))) {
        e.preventDefault();
        docStore.redo();
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Copy / Cut / Paste / Duplicate (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyC') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (els.length) modalsStore.copy(els);
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyX') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (!els.length) return;
        modalsStore.copy(els);
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          s.elements = s.elements.filter((el) => !ids.includes(el.id));
        });
        selStore.clear();
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyV') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const buf = modalsStore.getClipboard();
        if (!buf.length) return;
        const newIds: string[] = [];
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          for (const el of buf) {
            const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
            cloned.id = generateId();
            (cloned as any).x += 20;
            (cloned as any).y += 20;
            s.elements.push(cloned);
            newIds.push(cloned.id);
          }
        });
        selStore.setIds(newIds);
        return;
      }

      if (ctrl && code === 'KeyD') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const ids = selStore.getState().selectedIds;
        const els = slide.elements.filter(
          (el) => ids.includes(el.id) && el.type !== 'connector',
        );
        if (!els.length) return;
        const newIds: string[] = [];
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          if (!s) return;
          for (const el of els) {
            const cloned = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement;
            cloned.id = generateId();
            (cloned as any).x += 20;
            (cloned as any).y += 20;
            s.elements.push(cloned);
            newIds.push(cloned.id);
          }
        });
        selStore.setIds(newIds);
        return;
      }

      /* -------- Copy / Apply style (В§38) -------- */
      if (ctrl && e.altKey && code === 'KeyC') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const state = docStore.getState();
        const slide = state.slides.find((x) => x.id === state.activeId);
        if (!slide) return;
        const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
        const el = slide.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
        if (el) modalsStore.copyStyle(el);
        return;
      }
      if (ctrl && e.altKey && code === 'KeyV') {
        e.preventDefault();
        const style = modalsStore.getStyleClipboard();
        if (!style) return;
        const selId = selStore.getState().selectedIds[0];
        if (!selId) return;
        docStore.commit((draft: DocumentState) => {
          const s = draft.slides.find((x) => x.id === draft.activeId);
          const el = s?.elements.find((x) => x.id === selId);
          if (el) Object.assign(el, style);
        });
        modalsStore.clearStyle();
        return;
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  }, [docStore, selStore, modalsStore]);
}

/* ============================================================
   presentation/hooks/useFillRasterizer.ts
   РўРѕРЅРєР°СЏ РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєР° РЅР°Рґ singleton FillRasterizer:
   РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРєР° РЅР° СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚ + Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РїСЂРё РјРѕРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРё/СЃРјРµРЅРµ key.
   ============================================================ */

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../../shared/types';
import type { FillSpec } from '../../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import {
  getFillRasterizer,
  type FillCacheEntry,
} from '../../lib/stamps/fillRasterizer';

export interface UseFillRasterizerArgs {
  key: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
  enabled: boolean;
}

export function useFillRasterizer(args: UseFillRasterizerArgs): FillCacheEntry | null {
  const { key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled } = args;

  const [entry, setEntry] = useState<FillCacheEntry | null>(
    () => getFillRasterizer().get(key) ?? null,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    const cached = rasterizer.get(key);
    if (cached) setEntry(cached);
    return rasterizer.subscribe((changedKey, changedEntry) => {
      if (changedKey === key) setEntry(changedEntry);
    });
  }, [key]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const rasterizer = getFillRasterizer();
    if (rasterizer.get(key)) return;
    if (rasterizer.isInflight(key)) return;
    rasterizer.request({ key, width, height, shapes, fills });
  }, [key, width, height, shapes, fills, enabled]);

  return entry;
}

import { useStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import { useDocumentStore, useSelectionStore } from '../providers/EditorProvider';

export function useSelectedElement() {
  const docStore = useDocumentStore();
  const selStore = useSelectionStore();

  const activeId = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.activeId);
  const slides = useStore(docStore, (s) => s.slides);
  const selectedIds = useStore(selStore, (s) => s.selectedIds);

  if (selectedIds.length !== 1) return null;
  const slide = slides.find((s) => s.id === activeId);
  return slide?.elements.find((e) => e.id === selectedIds[0]) ?? null;
}

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';

export interface UseStampFillModeArgs {

  texts: StampTextItem[];
  images: StampImageItem[];

  height: number;
  commit: (s: StampShapeItem[], t: StampTextItem[], i: StampImageItem[]) => void;

export interface UseStampFillModeReturn {
  fillMode: boolean;
  setFillMode: (v: boolean) => void;
  fillColor: string;
  setFillColor: (v: string) => void;
  handleCanvasClick: (px: number, py: number) => void;
}

export function useStampFillMode({
  shapes,
  texts,
  images,
  width,
  height,
  commit,
  select,
}: UseStampFillModeArgs): UseStampFillModeReturn {
  const [fillMode, setFillMode] = useState(false);
  const [fillColor, setFillColor] = useState('#fbbf24');

  const handleCanvasClick = useCallback((px: number, py: number) => {
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] enter', {
      fillMode,
      px, py,
      shapesCount: shapes.length,
      kinds: shapes.map((s) => `${s.kind}:${s.id.slice(0, 6)}`),
    });

    if (!fillMode) return;

    const containing = findShapesContaining(px, py, shapes);
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] containing', containing.length,
      containing.map((s) => `${s.kind}:${s.id.slice(0, 6)}`));

    if (containing.length === 0) {
      console.warn('[useStampFillMode] no shape at', { px, py,
        candidates: shapes
          .filter((s) => s.kind !== 'fill' && !s.hidden)
          .map((s) => ({
            id: s.id.slice(0, 6), kind: s.kind,
            x: s.x, y: s.y, w: s.width, h: s.height,
          })),
      });
      return;
    }

    const target = containing.reduce((a, b) =>
      a.width * a.height <= b.width * b.height ? a : b,
    );
    console.log('[useStampFillMode] target', target.kind, target.id.slice(0, 6),
      { x: target.x, y: target.y, w: target.width, h: target.height });

    const localX = (px - target.x) / Math.max(1, target.width);
    const localY = (py - target.y) / Math.max(1, target.height);

    const fill: StampShapeItem = {
      id: generateId(),
      kind: 'fill',
      x: 0, y: 0,
      width, height,
      rotation: 0, cornerRadius: 0,
      stroke: '', strokeWidth: 0, dash: 'solid',
      fill: fillColor,
      fillTargetShapeId: target.id,
      fillAnchorLocal: { x: localX, y: localY },
      name: 'Р—Р°Р»РёРІРєР° РѕР±Р»Р°СЃС‚Рё',
    };

    console.log('[useStampFillMode] committing fill', fill.id.slice(0, 6),
      'anchor', { x: localX, y: localY });

    commit([...shapes, fill], texts, images);
    select(fill.id, 'shape');
    setFillMode(false);
  }, [fillMode, fillColor, shapes, texts, images, width, height, commit, select]);

  return { fillMode, setFillMode, fillColor, setFillColor, handleCanvasClick };
}

import React, { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import { DocumentStore } from '../../infrastructure/state/DocumentStore';
import { SelectionStore } from '../stores/SelectionStore';
import { ModalsStore } from '../stores/ModalsStore';

export interface EditorStores {
  document: DocumentStore;
  selection: SelectionStore;
  modals: ModalsStore;
}

const EditorContext = createContext<EditorStores | null>(null);

export const EditorProvider: React.FC<{ stores: EditorStores; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ stores, children }) => (
  <EditorContext.Provider value={stores}>{children}</EditorContext.Provider>
);

export function useStores(): EditorStores {
  const ctx = useContext(EditorContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useStores must be used within EditorProvider');
  return ctx;
}

export const useSelectionStore = () => useStores().selection;
export const useModalsStore = () => useStores().modals;

export class DragLiveStore {
  private positions: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }> = {};
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  private version = 0;

  subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  };

  getSnapshot = () => this.version;

  get(id: string): { x: number; y: number } | undefined {
    return this.positions[id];
  }

  set(positions: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>) {
    this.positions = positions;
    this.version++;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  }

  clear() {
    if (Object.keys(this.positions).length === 0) return;
    this.positions = {};
    this.version++;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  }
}

export const dragLiveStore = new DragLiveStore();

/* ============================================================
   GuidesStore.ts
   РњРёРєСЂРѕ-СЃС‚РѕСЂ РґР»СЏ Р»РёРЅРёР№ РїСЂРёР»РёРїР°РЅРёСЏ.
   РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕСЂР° РќР• РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚ СЂРµСЂРµРЅРґРµСЂ Canvas,
   Р° РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРјРїРѕРЅРµРЅС‚ GuidesLayer.
   ============================================================ */

export class GuidesStore {
  private state = { v: [] as number[], h: [] as number[] };
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener); };
  };

  getSnapshot = () => this.state;

  set = (v: number[], h: number[]) => {
    // РџСЂРµРґРѕС‚РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј Р»РёС€РЅРёРµ РІС‹Р·РѕРІС‹, РµСЃР»Рё Р»РёРЅРёРё РЅРµ РёР·РјРµРЅРёР»РёСЃСЊ
    if (this.state.v.length === v.length && this.state.h.length === h.length) {
      let same = true;
      for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) if (this.state.v[i] !== v[i]) { same = false; break; }
      if (same) for (let i = 0; i < h.length; i++) if (this.state.h[i] !== h[i]) { same = false; break; }
      if (same) return;
    }
    this.state = { v, h };
    this.listeners.forEach(l => l());
  };

  clear = () => this.set([], []);
}

export const guidesStore = new GuidesStore();

import { SimpleStore } from '../../shared/store/store';
import type {

export interface EditingState {

export interface ModalsState {

  floatingPos: { x: number; y: number } | null;
  contextMenu: ContextMenuState | null;
  stampEditorTarget: string | null;
  diagramEditorTarget: string | null;
  fillMode: boolean;
  fillColor: string;
  fillPoints: number[];

const INITIAL: ModalsState = {
  editing: null,
  floatingPos: null,
  contextMenu: null,
  stampEditorTarget: null,
  diagramEditorTarget: null,
  fillMode: false,
  fillColor: '#fbbf24',
  fillPoints: [],
  showGrid: true,
  showMargins: true,
};

/**
 * UI-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РјРѕРґР°Р»РѕРє Рё transient-СЂРµР¶РёРјРѕРІ.
 * ARCH-STATE-02/06: РµРґРёРЅС‹Р№ store + С‚РѕС‡РµС‡РЅС‹Рµ СЃРµР»РµРєС‚РѕСЂС‹.
 */
export class ModalsStore extends SimpleStore<ModalsState> {
  private clipboard: CanvasElement[] = [];
  private styleClipboard: Partial<CanvasElement> | null = null;

  constructor() {
    super(INITIAL);
  }

  /* ---------- editing ---------- */

  openEditor(state: EditingState) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      editing: state,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
    }));
  }

  setEditing(state: EditingState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: state }));
  }

  closeEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, editing: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- floating ---------- */

  setFloatingPos(p: { x: number; y: number } | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, floatingPos: p }));
  }

  /* ---------- context menu ---------- */

  setContextMenu(m: ContextMenuState | null) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, contextMenu: m }));
  }

  /* ---------- stamp editor ---------- */

  openStampEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      stampEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeStampEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, stampEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- diagram editor ---------- */

  openDiagramEditor(id: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      diagramEditorTarget: id,
      floatingPos: null,
      contextMenu: null,
      editing: null,
    }));
  }

  closeDiagramEditor() {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, diagramEditorTarget: null }));
  }

  /* ---------- fill mode ---------- */

  setFillMode(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      fillMode: v,
      fillPoints: v ? prev.fillPoints : [],
    }));
  }

  setFillColor(c: string) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillColor: c }));
  }

  setFillPoints(pts: number[]) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, fillPoints: pts }));
  }

  /* ---------- view ---------- */

  setShowGrid(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, showGrid: v }));
  }

  setShowMargins(v: boolean) {
    this.setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, showMargins: v }));
  }

  /* ---------- clipboard ---------- */

  copy(elements: CanvasElement[]) {
    this.clipboard = elements.map(
      (el) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(el)) as CanvasElement,
    );
  }

  getClipboard(): CanvasElement[] {
    return this.clipboard;
  }

  copyStyle(element: CanvasElement) {
    const { id: _id, x: _x, y: _y, rotation: _r, type: _t, ...rest } = element as Partial<CanvasElement> & {
      id?: string; x?: number; y?: number; rotation?: number; type?: string;
    };
    this.styleClipboard = rest as Partial<CanvasElement>;
  }

  getStyleClipboard(): Partial<CanvasElement> | null {
    return this.styleClipboard;
  }

  clearStyle() {
    this.styleClipboard = null;
  }
}

import { SimpleStore } from '../../shared/store/store';

export interface SelectionState {
  selectedIds: string[];
}

export class SelectionStore extends SimpleStore<SelectionState> {
  constructor() { super({ selectedIds: [] }); }

  select(id: string, multi = false) {
    this.setState((s) => ({
      selectedIds: multi
        ? (s.selectedIds.includes(id)
            ? s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id)
            : [...s.selectedIds, id])
        : [id],
    }));
  }

  toggle(id: string) {
    this.setState((s) => ({
      selectedIds: s.selectedIds.includes(id)
        ? s.selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== id)
        : [...s.selectedIds, id],
    }));
  }

  setIds(ids: string[]) { this.setState({ selectedIds: ids }); }
  clear() { this.setState({ selectedIds: [] }); }
}

import type { Slide, CanvasElement } from '../shared/types';
import { GRID_SIZE, SNAP_THRESHOLD } from '../shared/constants';

export interface SnapResult {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  v: number[];
  h: number[];
}

const gridRound = (v: number): number => Math.round(v / GRID_SIZE) * GRID_SIZE;

export function computeSnap(
  el: CanvasElement | { id: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number },
  x: number,
  y: number,
  slide: Slide,
  excludeIds: string[] = []
): SnapResult {
  const v: number[] = [];
  const h: number[] = [];
  const bw = el.width;
  const bh = el.height;
  const xs: { v: number; d: number; guide: number }[] = [];
  const ys: { v: number; d: number; guide: number }[] = [];

  // 1. РЎРµС‚РєР°
  const gx = gridRound(x);
  const gy = gridRound(y);
  xs.push({ v: gx, d: Math.abs(x - gx), guide: gx });
  ys.push({ v: gy, d: Math.abs(y - gy), guide: gy });

  // 2. РЎР»Р°Р№Рґ: РєСЂР°СЏ + С†РµРЅС‚СЂ
  const cx = x + bw / 2;
  const cy = y + bh / 2;
  const stageW = slide.width;
  const stageH = slide.height;

  xs.push({ v: stageW / 2 - bw / 2, d: Math.abs(cx - stageW / 2), guide: stageW / 2 });
  xs.push({ v: 0, d: Math.abs(x), guide: 0 });
  xs.push({ v: stageW - bw, d: Math.abs(x + bw - stageW), guide: stageW });

  // 3. Padding
  const pad = slide.padding;
  xs.push({ v: pad.left, d: Math.abs(x - pad.left), guide: pad.left });
  xs.push({ v: stageW - pad.right - bw, d: Math.abs(x + bw - (stageW - pad.right)), guide: stageW - pad.right });

  ys.push({ v: pad.top, d: Math.abs(y - pad.top), guide: pad.top });
  ys.push({ v: stageH - pad.bottom - bh, d: Math.abs(y + bh - (stageH - pad.bottom)), guide: stageH - pad.bottom });
  ys.push({ v: stageH / 2 - bh / 2, d: Math.abs(cy - stageH / 2), guide: stageH / 2 });
  ys.push({ v: 0, d: Math.abs(y), guide: 0 });
  ys.push({ v: stageH - bh, d: Math.abs(y + bh - stageH), guide: stageH });

  // 4. Р”СЂСѓРіРёРµ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚С‹: L / R / C вЂ” С‚СЂРё Р»РёРЅРёРё РЅР° С„РёРіСѓСЂСѓ
  for (const other of slide.elements) {
    if (excludeIds.includes(other.id) || (el as any).id === other.id) continue;
    if (other.locked) continue;

    const oL = other.x;
    const oR = other.x + other.width;
    const oC = other.x + other.width / 2;
    const oT = other.y;
    const oB = other.y + other.height;
    const oM = other.y + other.height / 2;
    const l = x;
    const r = x + bw;
    const c = x + bw / 2;
    const t = y;
    const b = y + bh;
    const m = y + bh / 2;

    xs.push({ v: oL, d: Math.abs(l - oL), guide: oL });
    xs.push({ v: oR - bw, d: Math.abs(r - oR), guide: oR });
    xs.push({ v: oC - bw / 2, d: Math.abs(c - oC), guide: oC });
    ys.push({ v: oT, d: Math.abs(t - oT), guide: oT });
    ys.push({ v: oB - bh, d: Math.abs(b - oB), guide: oB });
    ys.push({ v: oM - bh / 2, d: Math.abs(m - oM), guide: oM });
  }

  // Snap РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј Рє Р›РЈР§РЁР•РњРЈ РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚Сѓ
  const bestX = xs.reduce((a, b) => (b.d < a.d ? b : a), xs[0]);
  const bestY = ys.reduce((a, b) => (b.d < a.d ? b : a), ys[0]);

  if (bestX && bestX.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD) x = bestX.v;
  if (bestY && bestY.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD) y = bestY.v;

  // Guides: Р’РЎР• РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚С‹ РІ Р·РѕРЅРµ threshold (СЌС‚Рѕ Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ В«С‚СЂРё Р»РёРЅРёРёВ»)
  for (const xc of xs) {
    if (xc.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD && !v.includes(xc.guide)) v.push(xc.guide);
  }
  for (const yc of ys) {
    if (yc.d < SNAP_THRESHOLD && !h.includes(yc.guide)) h.push(yc.guide);
  }

  return { x, y, v, h };
}

  version: z.string().regex(/^\d+\.\d+$/, 'version must be "N.N"').default('2.0'),
  exportedAt: z.string().optional(),
  slides: z.array(SlideSchema).min(1).max(SCHEMA_LIMITS.MAX_SLIDES),

}).passthrough();

/* === Public types === */
export type ValidatedDocument = z.infer<typeof DocumentSchema>;
export type ValidatedSlide = z.infer<typeof SlideSchema>;
export type ValidatedCanvasElement = z.infer<typeof CanvasElementSchema>;
export type ValidatedDataRoot = z.infer<typeof DataRootSchema>;

/* === Result helper === */
export type ValidationResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; value: T }
  | { ok: false; issues: z.ZodIssue[] };

export function validateDocument(raw: unknown): ValidationResult<ValidatedDocument> {
  const result = DocumentSchema.safeParse(raw);
  return result.success
    ? { ok: true, value: result.data }
    : { ok: false, issues: result.error.issues };
}

import { useSyncExternalStore, useCallback } from 'react';

export type Listener = () => void;

export class SimpleStore<T> {
  private state: T;
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>();

  constructor(initialState: T) {
    this.state = initialState;
  }

  getState = (): T => this.state;

  setState = (nextState: T | ((prev: T) => T)): void => {
    this.state =
      typeof nextState === 'function'
        ? (nextState as (prev: T) => T)(this.state)
        : nextState;
    this.listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  };

  subscribe = (listener: Listener): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function useStore<T, S>(store: SimpleStore<T>, selector: (state: T) => S): S {
  const getSnapshot = useCallback(() => selector(store.getState()), [store, selector]);
  return useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, getSnapshot, getSnapshot);
}

  fit?: ImageFit;
  name?: string; locked?: boolean; hidden?: boolean;
}

export interface StampElement extends BaseElement {
  type: 'stamp';
  /** РћС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶Р°РµРјРѕРµ РёРјСЏ С€С‚Р°РјРїР° (РґР»СЏ UI, Р±РёР±Р»РёРѕС‚РµРєРё, СЂРµРґР°РєС‚РѕСЂР°). */
  name?: string;
  shapes?: StampShapeItem[];
  texts?: StampTextItem[];
  images?: StampImageItem[];
  libraryId?: string;
  shape?: StampShape;
  stampColor?: string;
  strokeWidth?: number;
  strokeDash?: DashType;
  innerStrokeWidth?: number;
  innerStrokeDash?: DashType;
  showInner?: boolean;
  topText?: string; topFontSize?: number; topFontFamily?: string;
  topFontStyle?: string; topTextDecoration?: string; topColor?: string;
  centerText?: string; centerFontSize?: number; centerFontFamily?: string;
  centerFontStyle?: string; centerTextDecoration?: string; centerColor?: string;
  bottomText?: string; bottomFontSize?: number; bottomFontFamily?: string;
  bottomFontStyle?: string; bottomTextDecoration?: string; bottomColor?: string;
  centerImage?: string;
}

export type CanvasElement =
  | TextElement | ImageElement | ShapeElement | LineElement
  | TableElement | FooterElement | PlaceholderElement | FillElement
  | DiagramElement | ConnectorElement | StampElement;

export const isTextElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is TextElement => el.type === 'text';
export const isShapeElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is ShapeElement => el.type === 'shape';
export const isLineElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is LineElement => el.type === 'line';
export const isTableElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is TableElement => el.type === 'table';
export const isFooterElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is FooterElement => el.type === 'footer';
export const isPlaceholderElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is PlaceholderElement => el.type === 'placeholder';
export const isFillElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is FillElement => el.type === 'fill';
export const isConnectorElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is ConnectorElement => el.type === 'connector';
export const isStampElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is StampElement => el.type === 'stamp';
export const isImageElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is ImageElement => el.type === 'image';
export const isDiagramElement = (el: CanvasElement): el is DiagramElement => el.type === 'diagram';

export interface Slide {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  format: DocFormat;
  orientation: Orientation;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  background: string;
  backgroundImage?: string;
  padding: Padding;
  elements: CanvasElement[];
}

export interface Document {
  version: string;
  slides: Slide[];
  data: DataRoot[];
  activeSlideId: string;
}

/* ============================================================
   shared/utils/formats.ts

   Р§РёСЃС‚Р°СЏ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ: СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂ СЃР»Р°Р№РґР° РїРѕ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Сѓ + РѕСЂРёРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёРё.
   Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ Р¶РёР»Р° РІ domain/slide/operations.ts, РЅРѕ lib РёРјРїРѕСЂС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°Р»
   РµС‘ в†’ lib в†’ domain РЅР°СЂСѓС€РµРЅРёРµ ARC-LAYER.
   ============================================================ */

import { FORMATS } from '../constants';
import type { DocFormat, Orientation } from '../types';

export interface FormatSize {
  readonly w: number;
  readonly h: number;
}

export function getFormatSize(
  format: DocFormat,
  orientation: Orientation,
  customSize?: FormatSize,
): FormatSize {
  const base =
    format === 'custom' && customSize
      ? customSize
      : FORMATS[format]?.portrait ?? FORMATS.a4.portrait;

  return orientation === 'portrait'
    ? { w: base.w, h: base.h }
    : { w: base.h, h: base.w };
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: src\shared\utils\id.ts
SIZE: 597 bytes
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/**
 * ID generation utilities
 */

let counter = 0;

/**
 * Generate unique ID
 */
export function generateId(): string {
  const timestamp = Date.now().toString(36);
  const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);
  const count = (counter++).toString(36);
  return `${timestamp}-${random}-${count}`;
}

/**
 * Generate ID with prefix
 */
export function generateIdWithPrefix(prefix: string): string {
  return `${prefix}-${generateId()}`;
}

/**
 * Check if string is valid ID
 */
export function isValidId(id: string): boolean {
  return typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0;
}

// src/templates/helpers.ts
import type {
  TextElement, ShapeElement, PlaceholderElement,
  TableElement, LineElement, FooterElement, Padding,
} from '../shared/types';
import { FORMATS, DEFAULT_PADDING } from '../shared/constants';

export const uid = (): string => Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);

export function makeText(o: Partial<TextElement> = {}): TextElement {
  const base: TextElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'text', x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, height: 30, rotation: 0,
    text: '', richText: true, fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#111',
    fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
    align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
    padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    background: 'transparent',
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeShape(o: Partial<ShapeElement> = {}): ShapeElement {
  const base: ShapeElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'shape', shape: 'rect',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 100, rotation: 0,
    fill: 'transparent', stroke: 'transparent', strokeWidth: 0,
    text: '', fontSize: 16, textFill: '#111', radius: 0,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makePh(o: Partial<PlaceholderElement> = {}): PlaceholderElement {
  const base: PlaceholderElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'placeholder',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, height: 30, rotation: 0,
    fieldPath: '', fieldType: 'string', sample: '', label: '',
    inline: true,
    fontSize: 14, fontFamily: 'Arial', fill: '#111',
    fontStyle: 'normal', textDecoration: '',
    align: 'left', verticalAlign: 'top', lineHeight: 1.2,
    padding: { top: 4, right: 4, bottom: 4, left: 4 },
    background: 'transparent',
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeTable(o: Partial<TableElement> = {}): TableElement {
  const base: TableElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'table',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 400, height: 120, rotation: 0,
    rows: 3, cols: 3,
    data: [['A', 'B', 'C'], ['', '', ''], ['', '', '']],
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1', headerBg: '#e2e8f0', fontSize: 13,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeLine(o: Partial<LineElement> = {}): LineElement {
  const base: LineElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'line',
    x: 0, y: 0, width: 300, height: 20, rotation: 0,
    points: [0, 10, 300, 10],
    stroke: '#1a1a1a', strokeWidth: 1, dash: 'solid', isArrow: false,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeFooter(o: Partial<FooterElement> = {}): FooterElement {
  const base: FooterElement = {
    id: uid(), type: 'footer',
    x: 0, y: 1000, width: FORMATS.a4.portrait.w, height: 40, rotation: 0,
    text: 'РћРћРћ В«РљРѕРјРїР°РЅРёСЏВ» В· +7 (000) 000-00-00',
    fill: '#ffffff', bgColor: '#1e293b', fontSize: 12,
  };
  return Object.assign({}, base, o);
}

export function makeFooterInPrintArea(
  slideW: number, slideH: number,
  padding: Padding,
  o: Partial<FooterElement> = {},
): FooterElement {
  const height = o.height ?? 40;
  const { x: _x, y: _y, width: _w, ...rest } = o;
  return {
    id: uid(), type: 'footer',
    x: padding.left,
    y: slideH - padding.bottom - height,
    width: Math.max(0, slideW - padding.left - padding.right),
    height,
    rotation: 0,
    text: 'РћРћРћ В«РљРѕРјРїР°РЅРёСЏВ» В· www.example.com',
    fill: '#ffffff', bgColor: '#1e293b', fontSize: 12,
    ...rest,
  };
}

/// <reference lib="webworker" />
/* ============================================================
   workers/fillWorker.ts
   РўРѕРЅРєР°СЏ РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєР° РЅР°Рґ rasterizeCompositeFills.
   РќРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ Р±СЂРѕСЃР°РµС‚ вЂ” РЅР° Р»СЋР±РѕРµ РёСЃРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ ok:false,
   РёРЅР°С‡Рµ async onmessage Р·Р°РІРёСЃРЅРµС‚ Рё main thread Р±СѓРґРµС‚ Р¶РґР°С‚СЊ РІРµС‡РЅРѕ.
   ============================================================ */

import { rasterizeCompositeFills } from '../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { FillWorkerRequest, FillWorkerResponse } from './protocol';

const ctx = self as unknown as DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope;

ctx.onmessage = async (e: MessageEvent<FillWorkerRequest>) => {
  const { seq, width, height, shapes, fills } = e.data;

  let response: FillWorkerResponse;

  try {
    const result = await rasterizeCompositeFills({ width, height, shapes, fills });
    response = result.ok
      ? { seq, ok: true, blob: result.value }
      : { seq, ok: false, reason: result.reason };
  } catch (err) {
    response = { seq, ok: false, reason: 'all-fills-rejected' };
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error('[fillWorker] unhandled exception', err);
  }

  ctx.postMessage(response);
};

/* ============================================================
   workers/protocol.ts
   РўРёРїС‹ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёР№ main thread <-> fill worker.
   ============================================================ */

import type { FillSpec, FillError } from '../lib/stamps/rasterFill';
import type { StampShapeItem } from '../shared/types';

export interface FillWorkerRequest {
  seq: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  shapes: StampShapeItem[];
  fills: FillSpec[];
}

export type FillWorkerResponse =
  | { seq: number; ok: true; blob: Blob }
  | { seq: number; ok: false; reason: FillError };
